Source: Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki (https://hypixelskyblock.minecraft.wiki/), license: CC BY-NC-SA 3.0.
How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

(part 4/96)

# Changelog/2025/October 21
Categories: 2025 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[October 21] Vesuvius and Mythological Ritual
Updated: October 21, 2025
Hello!
Today we're back on Alpha again with the Mythological Ritual. Joining us on Alpha this time is a new guest - Vesuvius.
Check out the thread from the Mythological Ritual Alpha session HERE
Spoiler: Additional Changes
Since the posting of this thread, some additional changes have been made to the release candidate, a list of which can be found below. We'd also like to apologize for the delay in having a changelog of these available to you all ❤️
- Added King of Kings perk back to the Legendary Griffin Pet
- Added flavor text to Anguish Talisman line
- Adjusted Griffin Pet, replacing 25 Health Regen with 25 Bonus Attack Speed
- Adjusted Manticore Claw, removing Pet Luck and increasing Bonus Attack Speed from 2 -> 2.5
- Adjusted Mythological mobs to no longer take knockback
- Adjusted King Minos attribute order of operations to ensure it properly increases final coin gain by 10%
- Adjusted name of Fabled Stinger to Fateful Stinger to avoid confusion with Fabled Sting
- Adjusted some Attribute Shard ID's due to multiple shards sharing the same ID:
  - Changed Cretan Bull’s Attribute from C36 → C39
  - Changed Sphinx’s Attribute from E35 → E39
  - Changed King Minos’ Attribute from E32 → E42
- Adjusted Challenger's Armor & Equipment + Mythos Armor + Equipment:
  - Added new ability doubling the base stats of the armor while in The Hub during the Mythological Ritual
  - Added new tiered bonus to Mythos Equipment that boosts coin gain on Mythological mob kill by up to 20%
  - Added 1x Combat Gemstone Slot to each piece of Mythos Armor
  - Increased "Unearthed" ability damage reduction, now maxes out at 25% instead of 20%
  - Increased "Familiarity" ability, now maxes out at 20 Magic Find instead of 10
  - Increased base rarity of Challenger's Armor/Equipment from RARE -> EPIC
  - Increased base rarity of Mythos Armor/Equipment from EPIC -> LEGENDARY
- Adjusted some drop rates:
  - Shimmering Wool: 0.1% -> 0.2%
  - Manti-core: 0.1% -> 0.2%
  - Fateful Stinger: 0.2% -> 0.5%
- Reduced Manticore damage stat from 9,500 -> 8,000
- Reduced King Minos damage stat from 8,000 -> 2,500
- Removed invincibility frames from King Minos' hitshield
- Fixed an issue causing Daedalus Blade to deal significantly less damage to Mythological mobs than intended.
UPDATE 3/11/25:
- Fixed being able to attach a lead to Siamese Lynxes and Cretan Bulls.
- Added sounds when completing final burrow if combat burrow.
- Changed Sword of Revelations lore to accurately reflect damage buff.
- Fixed Hephaestus Relic giving wrong multiplier.
- Fixed Kuudra Gatekeeper not having Crag as an alias in the Abiphone.
- Fixed Elle, Ugo and Captain Ahone quest locations in the changed Kuudra area.
- Fixed Cretan Bull dealing collision damage when dead or dying.
- Fixed Beacons holding a no-longer valid stat.
- Fixed being able to shear King Minos sheep.
UPDATE 23/10/25: We are testing the removal of hidden elections. Let us know what you think about it.
## ► Vesuvius
Vesuvius is a new NPC that stores your Kuudra Chests. He can be found at the Forgotten Skull in the Crimson Isle.
You can now also access Kuudra from the Dungeon Hub. Croesus deals with all chests while in the Dungeon Hub. You can access your Kuudra chests at Croesus. Don't look for Vesuvius in the Dungeon Hub, he's not there.
The chest claiming GUI (of Croesus and Vesuvius) has been modernized with better visual cues and bug fixes. Let us know if you notice any issues or have any suggested improvements.
The Wheel of Fate has been fixed to work as described. If the reward chest has an Attribute Shard, you can use a Wheel of Fate to reroll it into one of a higher rarity.
In other news, the Dungeon Hub has been slightly updated, with some NPCs having moved locations. That is all.
## ► Mythological Ritual Changes
- Crown of Avarice changes:
  - Damage bonus reduced from 0.1x -> 0.015x per digit
  - Damage bonus is now multiplicative.
  - Damage bonus is no longer restricted to Mythological mobs
  - Coins can now be consumed by killing Mythological mobs
- Added Daedalus' Notes Reforge Stone
  - Can be applied to Mythological spades
  - Grants Speed and +2 Griffin Burrows per chain
- Removed +2 Griffin Burrow chain length from Mythos Equipment.
- Griffin Burrow chains now have a 75% chance to be a mob and 25% for treasure.
- Mythological mobs now teleport back faster if they fall in the void.
- Added Griffin Upgrade Stones to the Bazaar.
- Added seasonal variants to the new Mythological mobs when using the Four Seasons Griffin Pet skin.
- Griffin pet Changes:
  - Removed Perpetual Empathy
  - Added Sacred Strength which gives +0.15% Strength per pet level when above 85% Health.
- Renamed Fabled Stinger to Fateful Stinger
- Changed some Shard IDs
  - Changed Cretan Bull's Attribute from C36 → C39
  - Changed Sphinx's Attribute from E35 → E39
  - Changed King Minos' Attribute from E32 → E42
- Ragnarock's ability strength buff no longer deteriorates over time (reverted back to live behavior).
- Fateful Stinger droprate changed from 0.2% to 0.5%
- Manti-core droprate changed from 0.1% to 0.2%
- Shimmering Wool droprate changed from 0.1% to 0.2%
- Added new Myth the Fish item.
  - 1 in 10,000 chance to replace any treasure found in Griffin Burrows.
- Added sell price to Cretan Urn.
- Diana's Pet Luck perk "Lucky!" replaced with "Huntress' Intuition", granting +10 Tracking.
- Anguish Talisman line recipes changed.
- Diana's Bookshelf +1 Pet Luck replaced with +1 Tracking
- Mythological Dye now drops from Mythological mobs directly.
- Future Calories Talisman replaced +1 Fishing Wisdom with +0.5 Tracking.
- Mythos Fragments adjustments in recipes:
  - MYTHOS ARMOR: Each piece now requires 8 → 2 Mythos Fragments
  - MYTHOS EQUIPMENT: Each piece now requires 16 → 4
  - DEIFIC SPADE: Now requires 64 → 16 Mythos Fragments
  - Griffin Upgrade Stones
    - Epic requires 16 → 4 Mythos Fragment
    - Legendary requires 32 → 16 Mythos Fragments
    - Mythic requires 64 → 48 Mythos Fragments
- Hephaestus Anvil: Now requires 64 → 32 Mythos Fragments
- Spider Queen's Stinger requires 12 → 16 Soul String per slot.
- Mana costs removed from Ancestral/Archaic/Deific Spade.
- Replaced Beacon's Pet Luck Bonus with Tracking.
We will be continuing to refine the update and hope you like the changes so far. Please report any bugs at https://hypixel.net/bug-reports/create, making sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
We'd like to once again remind you that you can obtain the majority of the items featured in today's update by talking to Centauri in The Hub.
That's all for today!

# Changelog/2025/September 4
Categories: 2025 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[September 4] Dungeonbreaker 3
Updated: September 4, 2025
Hello!
We are once again back with the Dungeonbreaker on the Hypixel Alpha Network.
Today's changes include:
- New Wither Essence Shop Perk: Echoes of the Lost
  - Gain 1-5 charges on your Dungeonbreaker after collecting a secret.
  - Costs 250/500/1000/2000/5000 Wither Essence to upgrade.
- Tic-Tac-Toe puzzle room has been redesigned.
- Weaker variants of the Dungeonbreaker are available at Centauri for testing purposes.
  - The idea is to find a comfortable number of charges and generation coupled with the secrets perk to allow for purposeful stonking instead of just aimlessly breaking blocks to go places.
  - If the charge gain is underwhelming, that can also be adjusted.
With this last round of testing, we should be ready for a release soon.
Warm reminder that the recommended version is 1.21.5+.
Developer's Note: To clear up some confusion, with the release of Dungeonbreaker we will be able to re-enable anti-cheat modules that were limited or disabled to facilitate the previous behavior. This should eliminate certain map exploits and make dungeon moderation much more effective.
Thanks for testing, and looking forward to your feedback.

# Changelog/2025/April 1
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[April 1st] SkyBlock Patch Notes
Updated: April 1, 2025
Good day to you, my friends, my dear.
It's April Fools' again, did you hear?
Lord Chuckleton is now in town.
Rumors say that he's a clown.
His presence lingers throughout the week.
Surprises hidden, for you to seek.
Many items, big and small.
Are you able to find them all?
Talk to Chuckleton in the Hub to get started on your Honk and Seek Adventure today!
## ▶ Event Details
[[A Honk and Seek Adventure]] adds about a dozen new items that can be found around SkyBlock while the event is active. The event will be active for 7 days, from April 1st (7:55am EDT) to April 8th.
Most of these items should be fairly simple finds, with varying ways to obtain them throughout SkyBlock. Aside from your initial [[Suspicious Red Gift|red gift]], acquiring these items does not involve interacting with [[Chuckleton]].
You can keep track of the items to be obtained by talking to Chuckleton.
Edit (4/1/2025 9:45):
- Fixed an issue where new servers may not boot with the NPC visible.
- Fixed an issue where crafting Jerry Helmet wouldn't give credit for the item (just recraft the item!!)
- Fixed lore in the Chuckleton menu indicating certain items couldn't drop multiple times.
- SIGNFICANTLY increased chances for Honk and Seek item drops.
- The Catgirl Black Cat fire sale skin has been removed for now and will come back this weekend in a cooler and purchasable form.
Edit (4/3/2025 12:30):
- The Ultimate Black Cat Catgirl Fire Sale skin is now up and contains the original skin that was displayed on Tuesday, plus 95 other skins and a special feature.
Good luck!

# Changelog/2025/April 24
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
ANNOUNCEMENT
Team Update - One Unified Rank
Updated: April 24, 2025
Hello everyone!
You may have already noticed this change live, but we’ve officially updated how our staff ranks are displayed across the Hypixel Network. Game Master and Admin ranks are now combined under a single Hypixel Staff rank.
This change is purely cosmetic. There are no changes to how our team operates. Developers will keep developing, support will keep supporting, moderation will keep moderating, and so on. It’s all part of our effort to better align as a team while continuing to provide the best experience possible.
Please note, GMeralds and Admin Diamonds will no longer be able to be received, however those already received will not be removed or replaced.
Due to the size and complexity of our systems, you may still see some places that show Game Master or Admin temporarily. We’ll be cleaning these up over the next few days.
Thank you for your understanding, and we’ll see you around the Network!

# Changelog/2025/April 8
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
This post contained non-SkyBlock information, which has been omitted for clarity.
ANNOUNCEMENT
Hypixel 12th Anniversary & Easter Event
Updated: April 8, 2025
This year we're celebrating the 12th anniversary of the Hypixel Network! We've got a major rework to our Bingo Event, as well as all of our usual Easter festivities!
- Easter Sale - Up to 25% off!
- Anniversary Bingo Event
- New Event Shop Cosmetics
- Main Lobby Egg Hunt
- Easter Simulator Returns
- New Bed Wars and SkyWars Maps
Read on for the full details!
## ► Easter Sale
Our Easter Sale is now active! It will offer up to a 25% discount on everything in the store in-game, while the web store will offer up to a 20% discount.
Check out the web store here!
## ► Anniversary Bingo
We're bringing back Bingo with some major changes! For those who missed our feedback post, here's what's new!
### ➥ Smaller Cards & Two Difficulties
We've made the size of our Bingo Cards smaller, they're now a 4x4 grid with 16 total tasks (down from 25 total tasks). We've also removed the Medium difficulty, now with just Easy and Hard. This allows for slightly less time commitment for the average player and allows us to make our task difficulty distribution a little more even. Below is an example of our new Bingo Menu.
### ➥ Game Categories
When you first open the Bingo Menu you will be asked to choose a game category to focus on. This will be the set of cards displayed in your Bingo Menu. You can still complete any objective at any time, this is simply to streamline the cards you're currently working on. Each category has two difficulties, Easy and Hard.
We made this change to allow players to focus on games they care more about and enjoy playing, so they spend less time having to play games they don't want to. Each category contains one Easy and one Hard card to complete. There will be more details in the next section, but you only need to complete one Easy and one Hard card to unlock the newest cosmetic rewards.
The game categories are as follows:
- Casual Games
  - Includes games such as Murder Mystery, Arcade, Build Battle, and more.
  - PvP Games
- Includes games such as Bed Wars, Skywars, Duels, and more.
  - Classic Games
- Includes games such as TNT Games, Quakecraft, Mega Walls, and more.
### ➥ Bingo Shop
Our new reward system is based in the new Bingo Shop, with Bingo Bucks as its currency! The Bingo Shop contains all rewards we've introduced for completing Bingo Cards throughout the last couple of years (excluding some statuses), so you can choose which cosmetics you'd like! You can earn enough currency for the newest set of cosmetics, one priced according to the Easy card and one for the Hard card, by completing one of each difficulty.
However, if you're missing some of the older cosmetics and would like to catch up, you can still complete the remaining Bingo Cards and earn some extra Bingo Bucks to spend, just at only half the amount of your first two cards. You can save up Bingo Bucks for next year if you wish, but the newest cosmetic rewards will require you to complete their respective difficulty this year first!
### ➥ Conclusion
We hope these changes make our Bingo Event more enjoyable! As previously announced, Bingo will now happen annually tied into our Anniversary Event. We hope you have fun completing your cards!
## ► Event Shop Updates
Our Easter Event Shop has been stocked with a ton of new cosmetics!
SkyBlock Black Angora Ferret Skin & Ablaze Yog Helmet Skin
SkyBlock Spring Barn Skin
## ► SkyBlock Bundles
Our Rank Bundles for Easter have been refreshed with our brand-new Sprays and Furniture! Claim them before the event ends!
New Sprays
New Furniture
We hope every has a wonderful spring, enjoy the warm weather (where applicable)!

# Changelog/2025/August 14
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[August 14] SkyBlock Patch Notes
Updated: August 14, 2025
Hello!
Today we have a quick patch with small changes to several areas of the game following the release of Hypixel SkyBlock 0.23.3.
- Added "Daily Bonus" to the Forest Whisper replacement reward when HOTF is maxed.
- Removed the conflictions between the "Sharpness" enchantment and mob-specific enchantments, such as "Smite".
  - Designer Note: Please note that this change may require additional balancing in the future, however we feel it's the most intuitive change to make following the introduction of mob types.
- Removed the requirement to occasionally move while fishing to prevent yourself from catching nothing.
- Fixed an issue where the "Woodsplitter" enchantment was an unknown Bazaar product.
- Fixed an issue causing damage to not be dealt correctly to some ☽ Spooky mobs during the Spooky Festival.
- Fixed an issue causing Scaffolding not to work while in shallow water.
- Adjusted some HOTF/HOTM messages to say "Exp" instead of "EXP".
- Adjusted the "Smite" enchantment to now also grant its damage bonus on Skeletal and Wither mobs.
- Adjusted the Mob Types of several mobs to now match the below:
  - Abyssal Miner → Aquatic/Undead/Subterranean
  - Automaton → Subterranean/Construct/Arcane
  - Boss Corleone → Humanoid/Subterranean
  - Butterfly → Arcane/Airborne/Subterranean
  - Cellar Spider → Arthropod/Subterranean
  - Fire Eel → Magmatic/Animal
  - Fireproof Witch → Magmatic/Humanoid/Arcane
  - Ghosts → Arcane/Subterranean
  - Glacite Mutt → Glacial/Animal
  - Kada Knight → Animal/Undead
  - Key Guardian → Undead/Subterranean
  - Lava Flame → Magmatic/Infernal
  - Mimic → Arcane/Construct
  - Night Squid → Aquatic/Animal
  - Reindrake → Frozen/Shielded/Elusive
    - Designer Note: This is a weird case, since it's also airborne by nature. We may make it the first mob to be four types in the future - time will tell.
  - Rider of the Deep → Aquatic/Undead/Animal
  - Skeletor → Skeletal/Construct
  - Squid → Aquatic/Animal
  - The Loch Emperor → Aquatic/Skeletal/Arcane
  - Trash Gobbler → Aquatic/Arthropod
  - Wither Miner → Wither/Skeletal
- Adjusted the bracket points for Agatha Contests in accordance with community feedback:
  - Epic: 2,500 -> 2,000
  - Legendary: 5,000 -> 3,000
  - Mythic: 7,500 -> 5,000
  - Divine: 10,000 -> 8,000
  - Special: 12,500 -> 12,000
- Adjusted point gain for Starlyn Contests,
  - Designer Note: The goal here is for activities outside of Fig Trees to be capable of reaching top brackets.
  - Per 100 Mangrove Logs: 4 → 6
  - Hunting Verdant/Coral/Azure: 1 → 3
  - Hunting Mossybit/Cod: 2 → 6
  - Hunting Coralot/Lumisquid/Spike/Salmon: 3 → 15
  - Hunting Shellwise/Mudworm: 5 → 45
  - Hunting “Other”: 10 → 30
  - Fishing Treasure: 5 → 25
  - Per Catch: 1 → 4
  - Per Sea Creature Catch: 3 → 10
  - Per Sea Creature Kill: 2 → 8
  - Per Lushlilac: 3 → 9
  - Per Sea Lumies: 2 → 6
If you run into any issues following this release, please report them HERE.
Thanks!

# Changelog/2025/August 5/0
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
0.23.3
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.23.3 - Foraging Changes, Mob Types, and more!
Updated: August 5, 2025
Hello!
This week we have tons of improvements to Foraging, a new Mob Types system, and plenty of bug fixes! Keep reading to learn all of the details!
## ► Foraging Improvements
### ➥ New Museum Items
We've added many of the items from the Foraging update to the Museum.
New Museum Items
- Abysmal Lasso (+1 XP)
- Abyssal Armor (+10 XP)
- Basic Fishing Net (+1 XP)
- David's Cloak (+2 XP)
- Decent Axe (+2 XP)
- Entangler Lasso (+4 XP)
- Everstretch Lasso (+10 XP)
- Fig Armor (+7 XP)
- Fig Hew (+2 XP)
- Figstone Splitter (+5 XP)
- Hunting Toolkit (+2 XP)
- Large Scaffolding (+4 XP)
- Mangrove Equipment (+8 XP)
- Medium Fishing Net (+2 XP)
- Medium Pocket Black Hole (+4 XP)
- Medium Scaffolding (+2 XP)
- Park Armor (+1 XP)
- Prime Huntaxe - Nex Titanum (+15 XP)
- Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus (+4 XP)
- Savage Huntaxe - Praedator (+10 XP)
- Seriously Damaged Axe (+1 XP)
- Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus (+2 XP)
- Small Pocket Black Hole (+2 XP)
- Snorkeling Armor (+3 XP)
- Tiny Scaffolding (+1 XP)
- Tormentor (+20 XP)
- Turbo Fishing Net (+7 XP)
- Vinerip Lasso (+2 XP)
- Worn Huntaxe - Genesis (+1 XP)
### ➥ New Achievements
We've added 135 new AP through some new Foraging-related achievements. We've also renamed 'The Dragon's Egg' to 'The Golden Dragon's Egg' and it now gives 15 AP instead of 10.
New Achievements
- Predator: Achieve Hunting level 5/10/15/20/25 (+5/10/15/20/25 AP)
- The Family: Donated 20 Fishes to Coral (+10 AP)
- Handyman: Tune the beacon on Galatea (+5 AP)
- Gotcha!: Hunt a mob with a Black Hole (+5 AP)
- Shine Bright: Reach Starlyn Contest Level 5 (+10 AP)
- Forgotten Shrine: Reactivate a Starborn Temple (+10 AP)
- Weird Science: Syphon 50 Attribute (+10 AP)
- Evolution: Fuse into 50 Shards (+10 AP)
### ➥ Other Foraging Changes
- Added 3 new hidden brackets to Agatha's Contest with even better rewards
  - MYTHIC: 7,500 Points Requirement
  - DIVINE: 10,000 Points Requirement
  - SPECIAL: 12,500 Points Requirement
- Melody Changes
  - You now only need to get 90% perfection on Melody's Songs for the maximum SkyBlock XP and Intelligence bonus
  - By extension, Melody's Hair now also only requires 90% perfection on all non-Personal Harp songs to earn
  - Melody's Harp now physically appears as damaged before you fix it
- New Galatea Sea Creature: Bogged
  - Drops Mangrove Logs and Sea Lumies
  - Unlocked at Fishing V
  - Sea Archer Shard is now the Bogged Shard and is dropped from hunting Bogged
  - Renamed the Attribute from "Monster Bait" to "Creature Fisher"
- New Epic Attribute: Chop
  - Effect: Grants a 10-100% chance to Chop an extra log, on top of the ones you swept.
  - Obtaining: 0.15% drop from Fig Tree Gifts and a 0.25% drop from Mangrove Tree Gifts
- New Legendary Attribute: Bayou Biter
  - Effect: Grants +0.5-5 Double Hook Chance while in the Backwater Bayou.
  - Obtaining: From hunting a Titanoboa
- Kuudra now gives 1/1/1/1.25/1.5 Kraken Shards for tiers 1-5.
  - Instead, the Daemon fusion now outputs 2 shards instead of 1.
- In Master Mode 7, an Apex or Power Dragon Shard is now guaranteed to appear in Emerald Chests and higher.
  - The shards do not affect what other loot you get from the chests, it's purely a bonus reward.
- Improved some fusion recipes:
  - 2× Sun Fish = 5× Shinyfish + 5× Firey Mob (same as firefly)
  - 2× Cropeetle = 5× Termite + 5× Praying Mantis
  - 2× Wartybug = 5× Dragonfly + 5× Lunar Moth
    - 2× Dragonfly = 5× Firefly + 5× Dragon (unchanged)
      - 2× Firefly = 5× Praying Mantis + 5× "fiery mob" (same as firefly)
        - 2× Praying Mantis = 5× Pest + 5× Combat
    - 2× Lunar Moth = 5× Ladybug + 5× Bug Rare+ (unchanged)
      - 2× Ladybug = 5× Termite + 5× Invisibug
        - 2× Termite = 5× Bug + 5× Mining
- Added Emblems for reaching Agatha Level 5, HOTF 5, and HOTF 7
- HOTF EXP rewards are now converted to Forest Whispers once HOTF is maxed.
- Added Flexbones to the Bazaar
- Doubled the Foraging Fortune given by Citrine Gemstones
- Booster Changes
  - All Boosters can now be sold on the Bazaar
  - All Boosters are now stackable
    - NOTE: Old Boosters created before this update will still not stack
  - Sweep and Foraging Wisdom Boosters can now be salvaged for 5 Forest Essence
  - Sweep and Foraging Wisdom Boosters can now be sold to NPCs for 25,000 Coins
- Foraging Armor Progression Changes
  - Reduced Park Armor's rarity to Common
  - Reduced Canopy Armor's rarity to Uncommon
  - Fig Armor now uses Canopy Armor in its crafting recipes
- Abysmal Lasso Changes
  - Can now also be crafted with x5 Sea Lumies now
  - Added to the recipe to Sea Lumies I
  - Vinegrip Lasso now uses the Abysmal Lasso in its crafting recipe
- Kiara will now give you her Abiphone Contact if you give her a Chameleon Shard
  - Having her contact will also allow you to buy an additional Shard each day
- Only Galatea Sea Creatures can now be fished up in Galatea
  - This does not include Event or Hot Spot Sea Creatures
- Scaffolding Changes
  - Now stays up as long as you are within 5 blocks of it
  - Can now be placed next to blocks, in water, and on non-full blocks
  - Can now be placed in Tangleburg
- Added 4 new Rabbit Residents to Galatea
- Added Emperor's Skulls to the crafting recipe of Abyssal Armor
- Stretching Stick Recipe Changes
  - Fig Hew now requires x8 Stretching Sticks
  - Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus now requires x8 Stretching Sticks
  - Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus now requires x16 Stretching Sticks
  - Savage Huntaxe - Praedator now requires x32 Stretching Sticks
  - Prime Huntaxe - Nex Titanium now requires x64 Stretching Sticks
  - Mangrove Vine now requires x64 Stretching Sticks
- Sturdy Bones are now used to craft the Medium and Turbo Fishing Nets
- Fig Tree Gift Changes
  - Stretching Sticks: 20% → 10%
  - Sweep Booster: 1% → 0.5%
  - Foraging Wisdom Booster: 0.5% → 0.3%
  - First Impression I: 0.4% → 0.25%
  - Missile I: 0.2% → 0.15%
- Mangrove Tree Gift Changes
  - Sweep Booster: 1.5% → 1%
  - Foraging Wisdom Booster: 0.8% → 0.5%
  - First Impression I: 0.5% → 0.4%
  - Missile I: 0.25% → 0.2%
- Trevor the Trapper now gives Hunting XP for completing hunts
  - Trackable: +50 Hunting XP
  - Untrackable: +100 Hunting XP
  - Undetected: +250 Hunting XP
  - Endangered: +500 Hunting XP
  - Elusive: +3,000 Hunting XP
- You no longer get Hunting XP for lootsharing
- Snorkeling Shoes now come with Depth Strider III when purchased
- Buffed the Hunter's Pressure (Python) attribute, now scales from 5-50%
- The Reborn (Prince) attribute now sends a chat message when it procs
- You can now shift-right click Shards in your Hunting Box to convert all of that Shard into an item
- Axolotl's now "lock on" to the player that alerted them and ignore any other players
  - This change should make their Shards easier to collect without getting accidentally griefed by others
- Phantom Changes
  - They no longer deal any damage
  - When lassoing them they no longer dive super far
  - They now despawn after 60s of not interacting with them
- Salamanders are now fused into by using a Newt Shard + Any Water Shard
- Chameleons now only require 1 Shard to fuse
- Getting multiple Shards via Hunting Fortune now doubles/triples/etc the points you get for Agatha's Contests
- Buffed the amount of Foraging XP and SkyBlock XP earned from Swoop's Milestones
- Shulker bullets now ignore block collisions for the first few seconds after being fired, they also home in slightly now
- Stealth I Books are now x10 as rare from Chillblades/Chillshots
- The Hina's Chapter task that required you to get 100 Shards from Lushlilac Bonbons now only requires 25 Shards
- Added /warp murk and /warp loch as aliases of /warp murkwater
- All Shards now actually have the word "Shard" in their name, rather than just being the name of the mob
  - e.g. "Seagull" → "Seagull Shard"
- You are now able to click on the Attribute Fusion and Transfer chat messages to quickly open your Hunting Box
- Sorting filters are no longer retained when reopening the Attribute Menu to reduce confusion
- Ryan can now detect logs obtained from the Personal Compactor and Bazaar for the Campfire questline
- Added a "Right-click to view recipes!" to the Runeblade Talisman and Ring
- You can no longer place Hunting Toolkit transformed items into the Reforge Anvil
- Tree Gift chat messages are now dark green instead of blue
- Removed the '+' from the description of the Cookie Eater (Bitbug) attribute as it was misleading.
- Removed the Catacombs level requirement for purchasing Forest Essence from the Bazaar
## ► Mob Types
Is an Endermite an Ender mob or an Arthropod? Does it take more damage from Ender Slayer or Bane of Arthopods? We've designed a simple typing system to help players understand what categories mobs fall into and how enchantments interact with them. You can figure out what type a mob is by looking at the symbol next to their name.
### ➥ List of Mob Types
|Mob Type|Special Behavior|
|-|-|
|༕ Undead|Takes increased damage from the Smite enchantment.|
|Skeletal|N/A|
|⊙ Ender|Takes increased damage from the Ender Slayer enchantment.|
|Ж Arthropod|Takes increased damage from the Bane of Arthropods enchantment.|
|✰ Humanoid|N/A|
|♨ Infernal|Takes increased damage from the Smoldering enchantment.|
|⚂ Cubic|Takes increased damage from the Cubism enchantment.|
|❆ Frozen|N/A|
|☽ Spooky|N/A|
|✿ Mythological|N/A|
|☠ Wither|N/A|
|⸕ Subterranean|N/A|
|Aquatic|Takes increased damage from the Impaling enchantment.|
|ൠ Pest|Can only be damaged with Vacuums.|
|☮ Animal|N/A|
|♆ Magmatic|N/A|
|♣ Elusive|N/A|
|⚙︎ Construct|N/A|
|♃ Arcane|N/A|
|⛨ Shielded|Takes only 1 Damage per hit. (e.g. Golden Goblin, Worm)|
|✈ Airborne|Takes increased damage from the Gravity enchantment.|
|❆ Glacial|Can only be damaged with Mining Tools.|
|⸙ Woodland|Takes more damage from the Woodsplitter enchantment. Can only be damaged with Axes.|
### ➥ The Mob Types House
As part of this update, a new house has been added to the Spider's Den that aims to onboard new players in the art of mob types:
- Michael will introduce players to the concept of mob types
- Ike lists all of the mob types and will reward players for the first time they slay each of them
### ➥ Enchantment Changes
- The Arcane enchantment now works on all ⸙ Woodland mobs
  - It has also been renamed to 'Woodsplitter'
- The Cubism enchantment now works on all ⚂ Cubic mobs
- The Dragon Hunter enchantment now works on all ✈ Airborne mobs
  - It has also been renamed to 'Gravity'
- The Impaling enchantment now works on all Aquatic mobs
- The Smite enchantment now works on all ༕ Undead mobs
- The Smoldering enchantment now works on all ♨ Infernal mobs
- Replaced Ender Slayer VII drop with "End Stone Idol", which upgrades Ender Slayer VI to VII
- Replaced Smite VII drop with "Severed Hand", which upgrades Smite VI to VII
### ➥ Item Changes
- The End Sword now deals bonus damage to ⊙ Ender mobs
- The following items now deal bonus damage to ༕ Undead mobs:
  - Undead Bow
  - Super Undead Bow
  - Undead Sword
  - Revenant Falchion
  - Reaper Falchion (also removed damage reduction)
  - Halberd of the Shredded (also removed damage reduction)
- Reaper Armor's "Trolling the Reaper" ability now applies to all ༕ Undead mobs
- The following items now deal bonus damage to Ж Arthropod mobs:
  - Spider Hat
  - Spider Sword
  - Scorpion Foil
  - Recluse Fang
  - Sting
  - Tarantula Ring
- Wither Blades now deal bonus damage to ☠ Wither m0bs
## ► Other Changes
- All Fortune stats are now disabled on Private Islands
- Golden Dragon's Symbiosis perk and Hound's Scavenger perk no longer work on Private Islands
- Diamond the Fish are now Soulbound and are sold in Odger's Lost Rewards Shop for 10m Coins
- Stew the Fish is now x6 more common from drinking Suspicious Stew
- Flake the Fish is now an OUTSTANDING Treasure catch from fishing in Jerry's Workshop
  - It was previously not tied in to the Treasure Fishing system at all, this should make it easier to obtain
- Renamed Thunder Shards to Thunder Fragments
- You can no longer fish in water on the Crimson Isle
- Hid vanilla item attributes for icons in the Stats breakdown menu
- Some large stat bonuses now dynamically recalculate to prevent players obtaining infinite stats.
- Ankylosaurus’ ‘Armored Tank’ perk now has a cap of +1,000 Strength
- Removed the hidden passive from Sponge Armor and Shark Armor that prevented air loss
## ► Bug Fixes
- Fixed David's Cloak upgrading its rarity at the wrong Attribute Milestone levels
- Fixed the Hellwisp (Matriarch Cubs) Attribute not working
- Fixed your Trap Hunts Milestones incorrectly just showing you Salt Hunts Milestone
- Fixed an issue with Joydives despawning in certain situations when they shouldn't be
- Fixed the "Max out a Foraging Attribute" task in Hina's Chapters not unlocking for some players
- Fixed the Fish the Fish task in Hina's Chapters not completing under certain circumstances
- Fixed Fig and Mangrove Log collections not counting towards the Raider Sword's bonus
- Fixed Ocelot Pet's Tree Essence perk not working on Fig or Mangrove trees
- Fixed being unable to buy the Early Bird HOTF perk when you had Tree Whisperer perk but not the 250 Gifts perk
- Fixed Chameleon Shards not being Syphon-able, not giving XP, and falsely displaying as maxed in the Hunting Box
- Fixed Chameleon Shards saying they required Hunting Level XX when they actually don't
- Fixed the Rain Slime Mana Steal attribute description not being accurate to what it actually does
- Fixed the Woodcutting Crystal recipe not using Enchanted Spruce Logs despite being in the Spruce Log Collection
- Fixed Molbert's quest not sending a chat message when players have collected enough Jungle Logs
- Fixed Yogs being able to bypass the damage cap on Huntaxes
- Fixed Carrot Kings acting weird around Black Holes
- Fixed being able to use a Signal Enhancer even after the Beacon is maxed out
- Fixed Loch Emperor Shards being called Sea Emperor Shards
- Fixed Hideonleaves not using their names in death messages
- Fixed a text inconsistency with Molbert's quest
- Fixed a couple of instances that mentioned needing to be on 1.21.4 to join instead of 1.21.5
- Fixed some weird coloring in the chat message that you get when sending a Shard to your Hunting Box
- Fixed two missing exclamation points in the Beacon tuning menu
- Fixed an issue with the dialogue of the Jade Dragon
- Fixed a minor text issue when obtaining a Bambuleaf Shard
- Fixed a minor grammatical issue with one of Hina's tasks
- Fixed a minor grammatical issue with Molbert's trap
- Fixed Shell Attribute not granting knockback immunity against certain mobs
- Fixed inability to hit Glacite Walkers with Huntaxes
- Fixed an issue with Jacob's Contest brackets not scaling properly when Finnegan's GOATed perk was active
- Fixed teleport abilities not resetting fall damage, leading to taking more damage than expected when landing
- Fixed a misplaced ladder in the Sand Castle Barn Skin (requires pasting the skin again)
- Fixed the Jerry Visitor for the Garden tutorial not showing up
- Fixed players not receiving Experimentation Table rewards if their game ends from the server restarting
## ► What's Next?
### Second Foraging Island
We have now completed most of the remaining work for the Moonglade Marsh, the first island of Galatea. While we already have designed the majority of the features for the Torrhus Canyon and Lunarise (the others islands of Galatea), we have not started development just yet. That being said, having these plans ready should make things easier once we do.
### Stonking
Stonking, the use of a ghost pickaxe in the Catacombs to reach secrets faster and increase efficiency, has been part of the game for a long time. Although the game wasn't initially designed with this mechanic in mind, we recognize the skill and mastery players have developed around it. Additionally, we've found that the Catacombs might simply be less enjoyable if players were forced to reach each secret slowly, using only the intended methods.
As we continue introducing more features from newer Minecraft versions into SkyBlock, we're officially implementing a Stonking-like mechanic accessible across all game versions.
If you're interested in seeing our plans in action, we'll be holding multiple testing sessions on Alpha in the coming weeks.
### Guardian Slayer
We previously announced we were working on new Slayers, and now feel comfortable sharing the identity of a new fishing-based one. Guardian Slayer takes place on a dedicated instanced island, accessible via the Ship Navigator, where players can fish up that island's exclusive sea creatures and kill them until the boss spawns. Since the boss has an island all to itself and can only be spawned there, it has allowed us to tie some really cool map mechanics into the boss' abilities, which we're excited for you to see soon.
Guardian Slayer will have to release after Lotus Atoll on account of it relying upon some new systems introduced by the Atoll, however both updates are currently deep into development.
That's all for today! :)

# Changelog/2025/December 15/0
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
0.24
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.24 - The Greenhouse
Updated: December 15, 2025
Today we are releasing Hypixel Skyblock 0.24, which features the Greenhouse, a new plot that can be placed in your Garden. This update also includes 3 new crops, a revamp to Farming Tools, a new Chip System, Crop & Visitor milestone nerfs, and much more!
## ► Greenhouse
The Greenhouse is a new placeable plot in the Garden that lets you plant, water, and grow crops over multiple real-life hours or days whether your are online or offline. The goal is to offer a slower, more passive alternative to farming crops actively, while still contributing to your Garden progression (including crop milestones).
Instead of spending a lot of hours farming directly, you’ll be able to plan out your Greenhouse, water your crops, and check back later for the results.
Also, it introduces new content through the mutation system, but more on that below.
So let’s dive a bit deeper on how the Greenhouse will work in the Garden.
### ➥ Unlocking
You’ll unlock the Greenhouse at Garden Level 7, where you’ll meet a familiar Visitor: the Carpenter. Completing his offer rewards you with the Greenhouse Blueprint.
After building it, you’ll begin an introductory quest that walks you through most of the basics of the how the Greenhouse works and how to interact with it.
### ➥ Planting & Watering
The Greenhouse is smaller than a standard garden plot (10x10), but crops grown inside the Greenhouse yield significantly more on harvest, but they also take longer to grow. After planting, crops will require water to keep growing. To water a plant, you’ll need to use a watering can that sprays water on each of the soil plots and can be upgraded to increase the amount of water and area it sprays in.
From there, crops will grow on their own while you’re away. The design intention is that players only need to spend a short amount of your time per day on your Garden, deciding what to plant and how to arrange it and then come back the next day to harvest them.
Although this process sounds fairly straightforward, there are a couple features of the Greenhouse we have not yet talked about: Crop Effects and Mutations.
### ➥ Crop Effects
Let’s start by talking about Crop Effects first. Each crop you plant on the Greenhouse actually provides a buff to a nearby crop. For example:
- Wheat grants Harvest Boost to nearby crops, granting them 20% increased yield when harvested.
Each crop has it’s own unique buff that it applies to other crops, some even can provide multiple buffs. Other crops can even apply negative buffs to nearby crops, but they also usually have stronger versions of positive buffs aswell.
Take Nether Wart for example:
- Nether Wart applies Improved Harvest Boost (+30% yield) and XP Loss (-20% Farming XP) to nearby crops.
As you can see, stronger buffs usually come with a certain drawback, so you have to think about which crops you want to place next to each other. Other interesting buffs include Immunity, removing negative effects from nearby crops or even Effect Spread, spreading it’s currently held buffs to other nearby crops.
As you can see the Crop Effects system can be quite intricate, allowing you to strategize how you want to plant your crops in the Greenhouse to achieve your optimal result. It will be interesting to see the different setups players will come up with!
### ➥ Mutations
Now let’s talk about another feature part of the Greenhouse, Mutations. When specific crops are arranged around an empty soil plot, that plot has a chance to spread a new Mutated Crop. There are over 30+ different mutations, each with unique requirements and their own growth mechanics and minigames.
Each mutation is unique and will need a specific combination of other nearby crops or even other mutations for a chance to spread. As you spread different mutations you will naturally unlock more new Mutations.
Mutations stack and build on each other, unlocking more intricate Mutations as you progress through the Mutation Tree.
For example, let’s showcase one of these Mutations:
Here you can see the Dustgrain Mutation surrounded by 4 Wheat, which is the requirement for it to spread to the middle plot. Whether it actually spreads onto the middle plot depends on the growth cycle, which triggers every few hours in the Greenhouse and updates all crops and mutations simultaneously.
Dustgrain is one of the simplest Mutations: it grows instantly, requires no water, has no unique minigame and only needs 4 Wheat nearby to spread.
But as you unlock more unique Mutations, things get more complex. Mutations may take time to grow, need water or have unique mechanics, such as a flower that falls occasionally asleep and must be woken up, a cheese-like mutation that attracts rats to your Greenhouse, or even a chorus fruit mutation that teleports around your Greenhouse as it keeps growing.
## ► Crop Analyzer
The Crop Analyzer is unlocked after you complete the new part of Beth's questline, and will allow you to analyze Mutations you have discovered through the Greenhouse to unlock new rewards, while also providing a good source of Copper!
Analyze new mutations to progress through your milestones and uncover some strange surprises along the way!
## ► Sunflower, Moonflower and Wild Rose
Part of this update, we also have added new custom crops that can both grow in the Greenhouse or can be farmed in any other plot aswell. These 3 new crops come with new tools, new farming contests, new crop milestones, new pests, new presets, new collections and also the new Helianthus Armor, a tier above Fermento!
These new crops also come with their own Collections, here are just a few items they will feature:
## ► Farming Tool Revamp
Farming Tools have been revamped, they have been standardized in terms of their Fortune and upgrades, and we also added a brand new levelling system that improves them while farming, similar to the old mathematical hoes.
Tools now gain experience everytime you break a crop and once they level up, they gain additional Farming Fortune, Gemstone Slots and increase in rarity.
We also went ahead and moved Farming Tools away from Anita and directly into the SkyMart, so the progression isn't inherently reliant on Jacob's Contest.
We hope all these changes make it easier to obtain these tools and also provide signifcant upgrades while progressing through the Garden.
## ► Chip System
When farming with these new Farming Tools, you'll also start gaining a new currency, Sowdust. Sowdust is used to upgrade Chips, special items that grant perks when consumed.
Chips can be unlocked from many different sources in the game and upgraded with Sowdust.
Consuming duplicate Chips increases their rarity, raising both their maximum level and how strongly their perks scale.
Jeff is a new NPC in the Garden who will introduce you to Specialized Farming Tools and helps you get started with Chips.
## ► New Visitors
We have added over 40 new Garden Visitors, including many familiar NPCs you may already know.
On top of that, there are several special Visitors. Mayors will not visit your Garden only when elected, and a new mythic Visitor can appear who sells a unique dragon pet.
## ► Misc
### ➥ Crop & Visitor Milestones and Composter Nerfs
Even with the addition of the Greenhouse, we felt that the current Crop Milestones (especially in the later stages) were still too high, which is why we have cut down Crop Milestones further by a whopping 68% across the board. We think that with the combination of the Greenhouse and this nerf, crop milestones should be a lot more achieveable for more players and no longer be a massive grind as it is right now.
In accordance with the above change, we also went ahead and adjusted Visitor Served Milestones by cutting it down by 75% from 10,000 to 2,500 total and also have reduced the Composter Upgrade costs by about 20% for most crops and up to 40% for rare crops.
### ➥ Changes
- Beth's questline has been properly integrated into the Quest system and she will also show up more frequently
- Removed Sunflower from the Flower Minion
- Jacob's Contest Completionist Emblems will now also require Sunflower, Moonflower and Wild Rose
- Changed upgrade recipes for Farming Tools
- Enabled Sweep on the Garden
- Added menu when a player interacts with Carrolyn without an exportable crop in their hand
- Created SkyBlock XP tasks when exporting 3000 of a crop to Carrolyn
- Modified the Farming Armors to also have chances to drop Cropie/Squash/Fermento for crops harvested within the Greenhouse
- Added Bonus Pest Chance to all Farming Armors:
  - Melon Armor - 10,10,10,10
  - Cropie Armor - 12.5,12.5,12.5,12.5
  - Squash Armor - 15,15,15,15
  - Fermento Armor - 17.5,17.5,17.5,17.5
- Removed the 3x multiplier on Biohazard Armor, instead buffed it's base stats:
  - Biohazard Armor - 16.5,16.5,16.5,16.5
- Standardized all Farming Tools' Museum donation XP and categories:
  - Mk. I → Intermediate, 4 XP
  - Mk. II → Skilled, 5 XP
  - Mk. III → Expert, 8 XP
- Changed rarities of Farming Armors and their drops
- Added reaching certain Garden Levels to the SkyBlock Guide
- Increased Organic Matter Cap upgrade values for the Composter
- Rebalanced visitor requirements for Lotus and Blossom equipment bonuses
- Changed Golden Carrot and Enchanted Mushroom Block's recipes, both now require 160x of the previous tier
- Increased Warty drop chance through the Wart Eater attribute: 0.01-0.05% → 0.02-0.1%
  - Warty is now counted as a rare crop and is affected by Rarefinder and Rose Dragon pet
- Changed the Hedgehog Pets perk from Nocturnal to Hunter's Insight which gives Farming Fortune based on your Bestiary Pest Tier
- Added Fermento to the Garden Sack
- The Ultimate Harvester Helmet Swappable skin now has 9 skins for each of the 3 new collections. This is available retroactively to anyone that has the skin applied.
- Added Sulphur to Mining Sacks
- Formatted leaderboard values with commas in Museum entries
### ➥ Bug Fixes
- Fixed being able to pick up arrows shot from traps in Crystal Hollows & Dungeons
- Fixed being able to claim the Fallen Star Tracker from Silnar multiple times
  - You can now purchase the tracker from Rusty
- Fixed HOTM/HOTF perks with reset timers resetting at 12am UTC instead of EST
- Fixed Training Weights and a couple other items having the Brewing Ingredient label
We plan on releasing our annual SkyBlock Recap later this week to reflect on what we've worked on this year and what is to come next year. Stay tuned!

# Changelog/2025/December 20

Taylor's Winter 2025 Bundle
Updated: December 20, 2025
Hey folks!
Today we are releasing another edition of seasonal cosmetics, along with new permanent offerings within Taylor's Shop.
## ► Taylor's Winter Bundle
Taylor's Winter 2025 Bundle is this year's edition of our Winter Cosmetics bundle. It features 10 new items that will hopefully make your holiday season more holly and jolly.
This bundle will be available starting from December 2oth up until March 21st the following year.

- Paladin Glossy Mineral Helmet Skin NEW!
Legendary Rarity

- Antlers Helmet Rune NEW!
Epic Rarity
- Ham Barn Skin

Dyes

Other

- Fishy Penguin Minion Skin (250 Gems) NEW!
- Polar Bear Minion Skin (250 Gems) NEW!
Showing off the Paladin Glossy Mineral Skin, Antlers Rune, and the Fishy Penguin & Polar Bear Minions
## ► Availability
Here's a warm reminder regarding the availability of items in the two Taylor's Shop collections and previous seasonal bundles:
- The Taylor's Seasonal Bundles will start to return beginning this winter, with the above listed items and goodies!
- Items from Taylor's Collection will be available for the foreseeable future. Items within Taylor's Collection may eventually rotate but you can expect them to be made available one way or another.
- We currently have no plans to bring back seasonal items from past bundles.
We hope that these non-limited offerings will allow for players to be able to use and enjoy these cosmetics!
Thanks for the support throughout the years and we hope you enjoy the new cosmetics.
Happy Holidays!

# Changelog/2025/February 1
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
0.21.2
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.21.2 - Year 400 Raffle Event
Updated: February 1, 2025
Hello all, and welcome to Hypixel SkyBlock Year 400! What a party we have in store for you, featuring new century cakes, dozens of prizes ranging from our new and esteemed Paint Drying Simulator to the elusive Shattered Locket, gift bags, and more!
Read on for all the details!
### ► Year 400 Raffle Changes​
In celebration of 400 years of Hypixel SkyBlock, we will be rerunning Year 300's Raffle event alongside a number of changes that aim to make the event more enjoyable for all.
- More Medium Rewards and Major Rewards are being handed out than in Year 300.
- All Raffle Rewards are now entirely randomized. When you win a minor reward, it can be anything from that pool.
- Raffle Ticket gain from simply being online now has a daily cap of 24 tickets (12 hours).
- Rezzus offers 63 daily quests. Completing a daily task rewards you with 1 raffle ticket and 1 slice of cake.
- Slices of cake come in a number of different flavors:
  - Slices of Blueberry Cake must be fed to members of BLUE team.
  - Slices of Lemon Cheesecake must be fed to members of YELLOW team.
  - Slices of Strawberry Shortcake must be fed to members of PINK team.
  - Slices of Matcha Cake must be fed to members of GREEN team.
  - Slices of Red Velvet Cake must be fed to members of RED team.
- Feeding someone a slice of cake rewards you both with a raffle ticket and some random loot!
- This loot can be coins, more raffle tickets, unique items, and even century cakes!
### ► New Century Cakes​
A party is not much of a party without cake! Released alongside this event are two brand-new century cakes for you to enjoy!
The Corpse-flavored Century Cake (left) grants Cold Resistance, while the ekac yrutnec tfir (right) grants +10 Rift Time.
### ► Raffle Rewards​
Unlike Year 300, where each raffle randomly selected several rewards from each tier, leading to some players not wanting to participate in certain raffles because their rewards weren't as valuable. This time around, each raffle will simply drop a guaranteed number of MINOR, MEDIUM, and MAJOR rewards. The reward is randomly selected from the corresponding loot pool only when a player goes to claim their reward from this tier.
Also included in this Raffle are 8 new items that have been added to the game. Some of these items will remain obtainable once the event concludes via other means, while others will remain exclusive. If you want to take a look at what's up for grabs in each reward pool, take a look below!
Spoiler: Major Rewards
- Space Helmet
- Divan's Alloy
- Shattered Locket
- Magic 8 Ball
- Hocus-pocus Cipher
- Ender Artifact
- Plasma Nucleus
- Hegemony Artifact
- Eccentric Painting Bundle
- Shen's Regalia
- Artifact of Control
- Pandora's Box
- Complete Century Cake Bundle
- Judgement Core
- Chimera V
- Necron's Handle
- Titanium Drill DR-X655
- Divan's Powder Coating
- Dark Purple Dye
- Spirit Mask
- Warden Heart
- Rift Prism
Spoiler: Medium Rewards
- NEW! Diana's Bookshelf
  - Chimera drops are saved on this item. Complete the item to gain permanent stats!
    - This item will be added to the Regalia Room sometime after Year 400.
- Necron's Ladder
  - This item will be added to the Regalia Room sometime after Year 400.
- Minos Relic
- Pure Blue Dye
- Pure Yellow Dye
- Reaper Pepper
- Flycatcher
- Infinileap
- Infinityboom TNT
- Flower Minion
- Overgrown Grass
- Perfect Peridot Gemstone
- Perfect Citrine Gemstone
- Perfect Aquamarine Gemstone
- Perfect Onyx Gemstone
- Random Century Cake Bundle
- Perfect Plate
- Travel Scroll to the Dark Auction
- Sirius' Personal Phone Number
- Golden Bounty
- Dead Cat Food
- Flex Helmet
- Placable Fairy Soul
- Prismatic V
- The Art of Peace
Spoiler: Minor Rewards
- NEW! Century Party Invitation
  - Click on other players with your invitation to save their SkyBlock level to the item. Collect all the different level colors to gain a permanent reward!
- NEW! Loudmouth Bass
  - Fight other players in a Bass vs Bass showdown. The winning bass eats the loser and grows larger.
- NEW! Paint Drying Simulator
  - Watch paint dry.
- NEW! Pufferfish Hat (Thorns V)
  - Very spiky.
- NEW! Mysterious Package
  - ???
- NEW! Small Brain III
  - Reduces your intelligence but grants some true defense.
- NEW! Runebook
  - A new accessory that grows stronger as you slay more runic mobs.
    - This item will occasionally drop from Runic Mobs after Year 400.
- Purple Jerry Box
- Summoning Eye
- Dungeon Chest Key
- Gift of Learning
- Chyme
- Metaphysical Serum
- Hot Potato Book
- Party Hat
- Flying Pig
- Fruit Bowl
- Cake Counter
- Salmon Hat
- Aspect of the Jerry, Signature Edition
- Rainbow III
- Red Gift
- Powder Pumpkin
- Filet O' Fortune
- Chilled Pristine Potato
### ► New Raffle Milestones​
The Raffle Milestones system has been adjusted this time around! As you acquire Raffle Tickets, you'll unlock new Raffle Milestones, which award Slices of Century Cake, Emblems, a Pet Skin, Century the Fish, and even a Giftbag that will allow you to pick out a specific minor reward from a selection!
### ► Participation Rewards​
Compensation Rewards have been adjusted this time around, and are now known as Participation Awards. In addition to all players once again receiving a memento at the end of the event commemorating the team that gathers the most tickets, raffle compensation has been adjusted:
- Failure to win a Speed/Daily Raffle grants +1 Cake Slice for every 5 tickets you entered without winning.
- All players who contribute 50+ tickets to The Big One will receive 1 Century Cake as a participation award once it rolls.
And that's all for today! Thanks for sticking around to see Year 400, and we hope to see you at the Year 500 celebrations when they inevitably roll around!
### ► Hotfixes​
We are actively monitoring the release and rolling out some minor bug fixes, which may not be seen immediately. We'll keep this list updated as we add more fixes:
- Disabled Recombobulators on Runebooks.
- Fixed Gifts of Learning from raffles being corrupted and unable to be opened.
- Increased the eaten Cake Slice limit from 20 to 50.
- Fixed Daily Tasks and Cake Slices eaten per day not resetting at the correct time.
  - They will now reset at 12am EST UTC for the rest of the event.
- Fixed the Daily Task time until reset counter showing the wrong time.
- Fixed being unable to gain any playtime tickets on a new day after reaching the daily cap.
- Fixed occasionally not marking a Daily Task as complete.

# Changelog/2025/February 11
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
0.21.3
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.21.3 - Year of the Seal
Updated: February 11, 2025
Hello!
Welcome to Hypixel SkyBlock Patch 0.21.3 . Today's update introduces the yearly events system headlined by Year of the Seal and bringing back the Shiny Pig event as Year of the Pig, along with some other changes.
Read on for the details!
## ► Year of the Seal
The Year of the Seal is a new event that lasts an entire SkyBlock Year and follows a 12-year rotation. We are aiming to have a yearly event for each year within the 12 year rotation, but for now, only the Year of the Seal and the Year of the Pig will be available. These two events will alternate every six years until more yearly events are added.
### ( •ᴥ• ) | How the Year of the Seal works
- During the Year of the Seal, you have a chance to fish up Beach Balls or Giant Beach Balls from Treasure.
- Use the Beach Balls to play a minigame by yourself that involves bouncing it on your head.
- Use the Giant Beach Ball to play the same minigame but everyone around you can participate as well.
- Based on your performance, you receive Fishy Treats which you can spend at Lukas near the Hub Blacksmith for various items.
Spend your hard-earned Fishy Treats at Lukas near the Hub Well to unlock a variety of new rewards, including Fishing Tier 12 and Clay Tier 12 Minions, two new Accessories, a brand-new enchantment, and more!
Spoiler: New Items
### (՞•Ꙫ•՞) | Return of the Shiny Pigs Event
The Shiny Pig Event is back, now in the form of the Year of the Pig as part of the new yearly rotation system! While the event remains familiar, we’ve made a few adjustments to make it easier to access and understand:
- The Pig Shop has been moved to near the Hub Blacksmith.
- Talking to Piggy gives a brief introduction to the event
- Added an Event Info within the Pig Shop menu
## ► Other Changes and Features
### ➥ [[Crystal Hollows]] Changes
#### Jungle Temple
- Significantly shortened parkour and removed some lava and traps.
- Moved respawn location closer to temple.
- Added more gemstones.
- Improved navigation through the room significantly by raising the river canyon.
- Prevented people breaking into the temple by mining through it.
- Added ways to escape the lava.
#### Lost Precursor City
- Significantly improved navigation throughout the city.
- Added more Automaton spawns.
#### Mines of Divan
- Improved navigation to aid travel between treasure points.
- Removed difficult to access treasure locations.
- Widened pathing around the Dwarven statues and crystal.
### ➥ General Changes
- Added a new Rift objective for obtaining Detective Amog's wallet.
- Added better error message when trying to collect a Relic after collecting all of them.
- Adjusted the Time Chamber modifier descriptions to be more accurate and consistent.
- Changed the Spiky achievement to unlock when applying Thorns 3 to an armor piece.
- Rabbits now give Combat Exp like other animals.
- Limited the Squeaky Mousemat's pitch and yaw settings to their actual minimum and maximums.
- Updated Carrolyns initial dialogue to say mysterious instead of spooky
## ► Bug Fixes
### Rift Fixes
- Fixed being able to use Hemoglass in the Wizardman Brawl.
- Fixed Temporal Pillars being able to spawn as Runic.
- Fixed Super Sharp Steak Stake giving the wrong amount of Combat Exp boost.
- Fixed the Hazard Zone in the Sun Gecko fight showing the wrong damage dealt.
- Fixed the time shown when Ubik's Cube has one second or less left on cooldown.
- Fixed Wizard's Assistant having purple inner arms.
- Fixed Wizardman forgetting that he gave you fragments in some cases.
- Fixed some dialogue inconsistencies.
### Item Fixes
- Fixed Biohazard Armor not linking to the wiki page in the guide.
- Fixed Bottles of Jyrre and Dark Cacao Truffles not getting upgraded if you leave the room immediately after completing the Dungeon Quiz.
- Fixed Extremely Real Shurikens working on The Garden and when guesting on private islands.
- Fixed Flamebreaker Armor not counting towards Madam Eleanor I's quest.
- Fixed Medium Cyan Backpack showing an incorrect texture.
### Combat Fixes
- Fixed the Golden Dragon's perk "Gold's Power" not applying to Blaze Slayer Daggers in Auric mode.
### Farming Fixes
- Fixed message that says "only farming tools can break crops" when only swords are disallowed.
- Fixed Ravenous Rhino and Baker not showing up when Blooming Business is active.
### Mining Fixes
- Fixed an issue that allowed players to keep the same Sky Mall Perk for multiple days.
- Fixed gap behind wall in Royal Mines that caused Golden and Diamond Goblins to spawn outside the map.
- Fixed Maniac Miner perk capping bonus at 990 instead of 1000.
- Fixed Quartz Blocks in fossil structures not instantly breaking.
### Misc. Fixes
- Fixed /savethejerrys attempting to move you while Jerry's Workshop is closed.
- Fixed Aranya not giving Chymes if speaking to her immediately after feeding her.
- Fixed being able to increment multiple Runebooks, Blood God Crests, and Trapper Crests at the same time.
- Fixed Bingo profiles being able to access the Museum.
- Fixed double drops being possible for Speckled Teacup when fishing with Blessing Enchantment.
- Fixed Lava Shells being missing from the Crimson Isle Large Treasure loot pool.
- Fixed not being able to add enrichments to campfire badges.
- Fixed the "Shrimp!?!" Achievement not being awarded after obtaining Shrimp The Fish from an Ender Node.
- Fixed the dialogue options from Lone Adventurer not working correctly.
- Fixed the More Trading Options menu icons sometimes not being centered.
- Fixed the Viking Longhouse area not playing the Park music.

# Changelog/2025/February 14

ANNOUNCEMENT
Taylor's Lunar & Valentine Bundles
Updated: February 14, 2025
Howdy folks!
Today we're releasing a brand new set of Lunar & Valentine themed skins to Taylor's Seasonal Shop! Here's a breakdown of what we got going on:
## ► Taylor's Lunar Bundle
Taylor's Lunar Bundle contains another set of skins inspired by the Lunar New Year!
Here's a full list of the items being included in these two bundles:
### ► Epic Skins (650 SkyBlock Gems)
- Zodiac Snake Scatha Pet Skin
- Zodiac Horse Reindeer Pet Skin
- Zodiac Goat Sheep Pet Skin
- Zodiac Monkey Pet Skin
- Zodiac Rooster Chicken Pet Skin
- Zodiac Wolf Pet Skin
- Zodiac Pig Pigman Pet Skin
- Zodiac Rat Pet Skin
- Zodiac Ox Mooshroom Cow Pet Skin
- Zodiac Tiger Pet Skin
- Zodiac Rabbit Pet Skin
- Zodiac Ender Dragon Pet Skin
## ► Taylor's Valentine Bundle
Taylor's Valentine Bundle contains lovely skins from Valentine's Day!
### ► Legendary Rune (950 SkyBlock Gems)
- Heartsplosion Kill Effect Rune
### ► Epic Skins (650 SkyBlock Gems)
- Pink Plushie Megalodon Pet Skin
- Plushie Tiger Pet Skin
- Plushie Tyrannosaurus Pet Skin
## ► Other Info
Taylor's Valentine & Lunar Bundles will be available alongside the skins in Taylor's Winter Bundle for the rest of its runtime during the year.
- Taylor's Valentine, Lunar, and Winter Bundles will be available until Saturday, March 22nd.
- As mentioned previously, the items currently present in these bundles will return in next year's respective bundles!
In addition, we made a few bug fixes:
- Added a reminder to collect unclaimed Year 400 rewards.
- Made Necron's Ladders and Diana Bookshelves one-time consumable items. If the item reaches its max potential, you can consume it to gain its permanent bonus. The item will be destroyed in the process.
  - We have added both Necron's Ladder and Diana's Bookshelf to Shen auctions, at a cost of 75M.
- Made Necron Ladders require 3 Necron Handles instead of 4.
- Only 1 Necron's Ladder or Diana's Bookshelf can be increased at once, and the item with the highest count on it (that is not at its maximum) will be increased. If they are all the same progress, the first one found will be increased.
- Fixed some typos in the Fruit Bowl and Century Party Invitation items.
- Placed Cake Counters no longer show '300' on the side.
Check out the store at store.hypixel.net to stock up on SkyBlock Gems to get these skins!
Thanks!

# Changelog/2025/January 21
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
0.21.1
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.21.1 - Experimentation Table RNG Meter, Abandoned Quarry, and More!
Updated: January 21, 2025
Hello!
Happy new year and welcome to Hypixel SkyBlock Patch 0.21.1! Today we bring to you an update containing substantial changes to several different areas of the game.
Read on for all the details!
## ► Experimentation Table RNG Meter
Our latest RNG Meter has been added to the game, and it's for the Experimentation Table! All players have been given 10%EnchantingXP/1000 as retroactive progress. In addition to the meter, we have also done a pass on the various loot the Table has to offer. The new loot tables for rare and ultra-rare books can be seen below. All books have the same chance of dropping.
Spoiler: Rare Books
- Respite 3
- Chance 4
- Syphon 4
- Life Steal 4
- Scavenger 5
- Giant Killer 6
- Growth 6
- Titan Killer 6
- Projectile Protection 6
- Fire Protection 6
- Blast Protection 6
- Protection 6
- Power 6
- Ender Slayer 6
- Sharpness 6
- Thunderbolt 6
Spoiler: Ultra-rare Books
- Snipe 4
- First Strike 5
- Triple-Strike 5
- Life Steal 5
- Looting 5
- Chance 5
- Syphon 5
- Prosecute 6
- Cleave 6
- Execute 6
- Venomous 6
- Cubism 6
- Thunderlord 7
- Thunderbolt 7
- Critical 7
- Titan Killer 7
- Luck 7
- Sharpness 7
- Protection 7
- Projectile Protection 7
- Power 7
- Blast Protection 7
- Fire Protection 7
- Giant Killer 7
- Growth 7
- Ender Slayer 7
- Smite 7
- Bane of Arthropods 7
- Golden Bounty
- A Beginner's Guide to Pesthunting
### ➥ Experimentation Table QoL
- Removed "Basic" and "Advanced" books from the Experimentation Table and replaced them with varying amounts of Enchanting XP.
  - To level this out, the Enchanting XP given upon table completion has been reduced.
- Added the following dyes to their respective RNG Meters:
  - Jade Dye (Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter)
  - Matcha Dye (Zombie Slayer RNG Meter)
  - Brick Red Dye (Spider Slayer RNG Meter)
  - Celeste Dye (Wolf Slayer RNG Meter)
  - Byzantium Dye (Enderman Slayer RNG Meter)
  - Flame Dye (Blaze Slayer RNG Meter)
  - Sangria Dye (Vampire Slayer RNG Meter)
  - Livid Dye (Master Mode Floor 5 RNG Meter)
  - Necron Dye (Master Mode Floor 7 RNG Meter)
- Added a /meter alias to the /rng command.
- "Your RNG Meter was reset" message now only plays AFTER obtaining your selected drop. No more ruined surprises!
- Added some hologram text above the Experimentation Table to make it stand out more.
- Added a search bar to all RNG Meters.
- Added overflow charges to the Experimentation Table.
- Changed the reset button on all RNG Meters from a Sign to an Exclamation Mark player head.
## ► The Abandoned Quarry
A new area, named the Abandoned Quarry, has been added to the Dwarven Mines! Players can travel to this area by talking to the Station Master, and can choose to either spend their time mining veins of Dwarven Quartz, which have a higher base yield than regular Quartz, or to cast their rods in the hopes of catching some Mithril Grubbers.
Mithril Grubbers drop varying amounts of Mithril Power when killed, the amount varying with the size of the Grubber:
- Small (55% of Grubbers): 250 Mithril Powder
- Medium (30% of Grubbers): 500 Mithril Powder
- Large (10% of Grubbers): 1000 Mithril Powder
- Bloated (5% of Grubbers): 5000 Mithril Powder
Powder drops from Grubbers are affected by your standard powder-interacting perks, such as drill parts, HOTM perks, and the 2x powder event.
Also added to the game is the Speckled Teacup, a GREAT CATCH! tier of treasure that can be returned to its rightful owner, Brynmor, in exchange for his Abiphone contact.
## ► Small Pest Changes
With this update comes some small balancing and improvements to the Pest system, following on from our recent Pesthunter's Wares update.
### ➥ Features
- Added the Pesthunter's Cloak, Pesthunter's Necklace, and Pesthunter's Belt to the Pesthunter's Wares.
- Added NPC sell prices to the following items:
  - Wriggling Larva: 250k
  - Squeaky Mousemat: 100k → 1m
- Added Squeaky Mousemat to the Museum.
- Added a Pest Cooldown Indicator to the Pest Tablist Widget.
### ➥ Balancing
- Added more bulk trading options to /ltrades.
- Added a "Release All" button to Pest Traps.
- Added EPIC and LEGENDARY Slug Pet that can drop from the Slug pest to the same drop pool.
- Updated the itemlore of Sprayonator materials to make their function clearer.
- Improved Pest Trap hologram text.
- Made the message sent when Pest Traps catch a Pest also activate when the player's online.
- Swords can no longer break crops in The Garden.
- The type of Pest that spawns in a Pest Trap is now influenced by the current disc you have playing in your vacuum.
- Adjusted Strawberry Dye's drop chance from Cactus, Cane from 1/300M → 1/150M on account of the dye now only rolling once.
- Buffed Dung Dye's Drop chance from Field Mouse to 1/50k
- Removed the "Vacuums don't work on these creatures!" when vacuuming near a Pest Trap.
- The Field Mouse now gives 1.5x the crop drops of other pests.
- Made Pest Trap messages clickable.
- Disabled Ragnarock Axe being used in The Garden
- Made Pest Spawn Cooldown additive instead of multiplicative, and shifted around some numbers. The optimal Pest CDR setup now looks like:
  - Pesthunter's Necklace: 10% CDR
  - Pest Vest: 15% CDR
  - Pesthunter's Gloves: 10% CDR
  - Pesthunter's Belt: 10% CDR
  - Squeaky Reforge: 10% CDR (2.5x4)
  - Finnegan's "Pest Eradicator" perk: 20% CDR
    - Total: 75% Pest Cooldown Reduction.
- Added a Tiered Set Bonus to the Pesthunter's Equipment granting FF on Pests.
- Increased the rarity of each piece of Pesthunter's Equipment from UNCOMMON → RARE
## ► Other Changes and Features
### ➥ Mountaintop/Rift Changes
- Add /tb as a shortcut for /timebag.
- Added a combo counter in the action bar for Puffs in The Rift.
- Added a new item to Vampire Slayer crafts that allows you to combine the Wizardman Face and the Anti-Bite Scarf.
- Added a new secret achievement to the Rift.
- Added a parkour leaderboard for the Temporal Race.
- Added skill experience for mining and farming resources in the Rift.
- Fixed Chrono pickaxes not working on Living Metal ore, despite claiming to.
- Fixed breaking Living Metal ores with the wrong tool spawning unmineable blocks around it.
- Golden Belt now has the stats and ability of Vermin Belt.
- Player health is now restored after fighting Sun Gecko.
- Rancher’s Boots can now be used with any farming set to meet Eleanor requirements.
- Reduced the loot share from Puffs from 100% to 10%.
- Shen's Ringalia now includes Disinfestor Gloves in it’s price.
- Timite Collection items have been adjusted.
- Satelite NPC sell price increased to 37800 motes.
### ➥ Crimson Isle Changes
- Buffed Habanero Tactics
  - Added +2.5/5 Combat Wisdom if using a slayer weapon
  - Habanero Tactics IV damage from +4% to +20%
  - Habanero Tactics V damage from +5% to +25%
  - You now no longer lose Magic Find when not having Smoldering Polarization active. Instead, you gain a 10/15 MF bonus while Smoldering Polarization is active.
- Added a CLICK tag to the Blazetekk Ham Radio as it can use any click.
- Added a new item Tera Shell Necklace that prevents Trophy Fish from being fished up.
- Added Crimson Isle lava immunity to Magma Lord Armor.
  - This includes Kuudra’s Hollow.
- Buffed Radioactive Vial drop chance from 0.3% to 0.5%.
- Lowered particles emitted by mobs and bosses throughout the Crimson Isle
- ReworkedTrophy Fish Chance to increase the individual probability of each roll.
  - This fixes being unable to fish up trophy fish while not having the stat.
  - This, in theory, should also fix Lava Shell rates being bad. If they are still an issue, please report it as a bug!
- Fixed being able to gain Crimson Armor stacks using bows.
- Fixed being able to gain Crimson Armor stacks through hitting other players.
- Fixed not being able to gain Hollow Armor stacks by dealing magic damage.
### ➥ Other Changes
- Added a shortcut recipe for Mutant Nether Warts.
- Added Volcanic Rock, Snow Ball, Walnut and Winter Fragment to the Events Sack.
- Removed "undodgeable" description from Riftstalker Bloodfiend’s Twinclaws ability.
- The stat tunings at Maxwell now refers to sneaking as "sneak" instead of "shift".
## ► Bug Fixes
### Item Fixes
- Fixed Enchanted Clock not decreasing the time remaining for Capsaicin Eyedrops.
- Fixed Hyper Furnace not Smelting.
- Fixed Storm and Hurricane in a Bottle only giving 50k Charges when combined with a Pulse Ring.
### Combat Fixes
- Fixed the Axe thrown by Minotaurs not rotating.
### Farming Fixes
- Fixed an issue causing the Sprayonator to unintentionally reduce Pest spawn cooldowns.
- Fixed an issue causing Finnegan's Pest Eradicator perk unintentionally reduce Pest cooldowns.
- Fixed Visitors sometimes standing on top of each other in the Garden.
- Fixed some typing errors within Pest Traps.
### Mining Fixes
- Fixed Gemstone Gauntlet’s texture glitching out while using the Pickobulus ability.
- Fixed King Talisman not always giving the 10 HOTM XP bonus.
- Fixed unclaimable Royal Compass commission milestone reward.
### Misc. Fixes
- Fixed a visual glitch in The Forge with Cole's Molten Forge perk.
- Fixed an issue with trapper mobs.
- Fixed Chest+ page icons having the incorrect skin numbers.
- Fixed empty flower pots still being present after being destroyed.
- Fixed Frost Walker Enchantment not working outside of the Hub during the Carnival.
- Fixed Hard Stone and Mithril Minions not breaking blocks when their slots are partially filled.
- Fixed Jake not accepting animals fished with the Farmer's Rod.
- Fixed muted players being unable to complete Eleanor’s mirror quest.
- Fixed not being able to enchant some items that you have the requirements to use.
- Fixed not being able to upgrade new Eerie Pets if you've upgraded one in the past.
- Fixed the error message when trying to join a Dungeon via the clickable message from the Party Finder.
- Fixed the Spooky Festival not giving out the correct amount of Carnival Tickets to the top 50 players.

# Changelog/2025/January 22

PATCH
[January 22] SkyBlock Patch Notes
Updated: January 22, 2025
Hey all!
We are here to inform you about the latest fixes that were rolled to SkyBlock in the past day for 0.21.1.
- Fixed an issue where a player's RNG meter progress would reset if they dropped a dye.

- Fixed RNG Meter not giving retroactive exp progress on any public islands.

- Moved Scavenger book from the Ultra Rare to the Rare pool in the RNG meter.

# Changelog/2025/January 28
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[January 28] Reducing NPC Sell Price of Corrupted Fragments
Updated: January 28, 2025
Hey Everyone!
Today we're releasing a couple changes:
- Fix Pest Traps rolling for wrong amount of time.
- Corrupted Fragments now sell for 1 coin to NPCs rather than 20 coins.
- Preparations for the Year 400 event.
However, we wanted to bring the attention to one change in particular, with the intent of fixing a current economic imbalance within SkyBlock.
- Corrupted Fragments now sell for 1 coin to NPCs rather than 20 coins.
At the time of writing this, Automated Shipping (Minion Hoppers) are responsible for 53% of all coin generation within SkyBlock, which contributes heavily to inflation in the game. In a perfect scenario, to combat inflation, the balance of coin generation and coin removal from the game needs to be close, and we will continue to make adjustments in the game to ensure this balance is maintained as much as possible.
To put it bluntly, players running Slime Minion setups are generating far too many coins every day, through corrupted fragments. This sell price nerf only applies to corrupted fragments.
We understand that this is a frustrating change for those of you who have sunk coins into your Minion setups, but it is necessary for us to make this change to maintain a healthy economy. Inflation hurts the playerbase as it makes everything more expensive, creating a vicious cycle where players feel the need to exploit these heavy money making schemes to even be able to compete in the market, causing inflation to grow further out of control. We still want players to be able to earn passive income, but things were getting out of hand on this specific method.
At this time, we do not have any plans to revisit the sell price of corrupted fragments.
Thank you for your time.

[July 1] Extreme Bingo & Bug Fixes
Updated: July 1, 2025
Hello! We've rolled out some bug fixes for the newly released Foraging content along with a few changes for the upcoming Extreme Bingo event. Enjoy!
➥ Extreme Bingo
Extreme Bingo is returning today, July 1st! Will you be able to complete all of the extra-difficult tasks before July 15th? There are a few changes this time around:
- Added a Bingbow (Bingo Shortbow)
- Added the Bingo Rod (Water Rod)
- Changed the item model of the Binghoe to an Axe
- Fixed an issue with "A Hard Day's Work" Community Goal not correctly counting Jacob's Tickets
➥ Foraging Bug Fixes
- Fixed several miscellaneous issues with the Hunting Toolkit
- Fixed several issues with loot sharing Shards
- Fixed some related errors with getting Shard types from mobs
- Fixed an issue regarding using Traps on Private Islands or The Garden
- Fixed a miscellaneous issue with The Bazaar
- Fixed an issue with the Eleanor questline in the Park
- Fixed Heart of the Forest menu showing lifetime earned Forest Whispers instead of current Forest Whispers
- Fixed SkyBlock XP decreasing from obtaining Forest Whispers after spending them
➥ Miscellaneous Bug Fixes

➥ A public notice about the Dark Auction
Following recent updates and backend changes to the game, we have identified a performance issue that is negatively affecting the servers right now. Though we are always working on improving performance over time, there was always the risk that things would catch up to us someday as we release more content.
This has led to the Dark Auction specifically going, well, dark. We have a few potential solutions in the pipeline, but it is not possible at this time to tell how long it will take.
