# Changelog/2025/July 22/0
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
0.23.2
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.23.2 - The Primordial Broodfather
Updated: July 22, 2025
Hello! Welcome to Hypixel SkyBlock Patch 0.23.2 . Today's update brings you some long-awaited slayer content and fixes to other parts of the game.
## ► The Primordial Broodfather
The Primordial Broodfather is the fifth and final tier Spider Slayer boss, being a step up from the Tier V Revenant Horror. It brings with it not just several exciting new drops that aim to alter the overarching slayer progression, but also several key revamps to older items.
### ➥ Boss Abilities
We didn't just add a fifth tier to Spider Slayer and call it quits - we instead took the time to rebuild the slayer from the ground up, aiming for a more active and varied gameplay experience. Each tier of the boss is now entirely different from what it was before.
T1 Tarantula Broodfather
Spoiler: Stats & Abilities
1,000 HP
35 Damage
- Backstab : Occasionally leaps behind you. Attacks from behind deal +50% Damage.
T2 Tarantula Broodfather
Spoiler: Stats & Abilities
30,000
55 Damage
- Backstab : Occasionally leaps behind you. Attacks from behind deal +75% Damage.
- Noxious Paralysis : Reduces your healing by 25% and inflicts a stacking -2.5% debuff to your Speed Cap every 5th time you get damaged.
T3 Tarantula Broodfather
Spoiler: Stats & Abilities
900,000 HP
260 Damage
- Backstab : Occasionally leaps behind you. Attacks from behind deal +100% Damage.
- Noxious Paralysis : Reduces your healing by 50% and inflicts a stacking -5% debuff to your Speed Cap every 5th time you get damaged.
- Web of Lies : Spews webbing along the ground, disabling teleportation abilities until they are cleared. Also spawns 2 Eggs Sacs, break them before they hatch! (at 66% and 33% Health)
T4 Tarantula Broodfather
Spoiler: Stats & Abilities
2.4M
530 Damage
- Backstab : Occasionally leaps behind you. Attacks from behind deal +125% Damage.
- Noxious Paralysis : Reduces your healing by 75% and inflicts a stacking -7.5% debuff to your Speed Cap every 5th time you get damaged.
- Web of Lies : Spews webbing along the ground, disabling teleportation abilities until they are cleared. Also spawns 2 Eggs Sacs, break them before they hatch! (at 66% and 33% Health)
- Bat Barrage : Becomes immune to magic damage for the rest of the fight and releases a horde of Bats. Take 1% of Max Health as Damage per second for each Bat alive. (starting at 50% Health)
T5 Primordial Broodfather (NEW!)
Spoiler: Stats & Abilities
10,000,000 HP
3,500 Damage
- Backstab : Occasionally leaps behind you. Attacks from behind deal +150% Damage.
- Noxious Paralysis : Reduces your healing by 100% and inflicts a stacking -10% debuff to your Speed Cap every 5th time you get damaged.
- Web of Lies : Spews webbing along the ground, disabling teleportation abilities until they are cleared. Also spawns 3 Eggs Sacs, break them before they hatch! (at 66% and 33% Health)
- Bat Barrage : Becomes immune to magic damage for the rest of the fight and releases a horde of Bats. Take 2% of Max Health as Damage per second for each Bat alive. (starting at 66% Health)
- Til Death Do Us Part : Upon initial "death", calls out to the Primordial Broodmother, who will awaken and fuse with the Broodfather, creating the Primordial Brood. The Brood has x2 Health and Damage of the Primordial Broodfather but only uses passive abilities.
### ➥ New Items
Please note that we are aware of just how much Spider Slayer RNG Meter XP some players have stockpiled over the years. While we will not be removing any Meter XP from players, we will also not allow for the new chase items to be redeemed in the RNG Meter for a little while after the release of this update. We have made this choice to prevent the market from being flooded on day 1 and to give everyone an equal chance at obtaining the rarer drops.
Spider Slayer 6 Items
Spoiler: List of Items
- Tarantula Fang
  - A new upgrade to the Recluse Fang.
- Vial of Venom
  - A consumable that can be ingested up to 5 times for a total of +5 Crit Damage.
Spider Slayer 7 Items
Spoiler: List of Items
- Shriveled Bracelet
  - A handy bracelet that provides some useful stats to players in the Spider's Den.
- Primordial Armor
  - An upgrade to Tarantula Armor that increases its base stats and abilities.
Spider Slayer 8 Items
Spoiler: List of Items
- Sting
  - The final weapon in the line of Spider Slayer swords. Deals +250% damage to Spiders and gains a large amount of Combat Wisdom against them. Attacks from this weapon will always critically strike.
- The Primordial
  - The chase craft of the Primordial Broodfather, "The Primordial," is a belt that gives a small chance for players to instantly respawn slayers and slayer minibosses while worn, meaning less downtime and more slaying!
Spider Slayer 9 Items
Spoiler: List of Items
- Tier XII Tarantula Minion
  - Players may upgrade Tier XI Tarantula Minions to Tier XII via the Bartender upon reaching Spider Slayer 9.
### ➥ Other Spider Slayer Changes
The following changes have been made to existing parts of Spider Slayer.
Spoiler: Other Spider Slayer Changes
- Added an ability that buffs the base stats of the Tarantula Pet by up to 50% to the Tarantula Leggings.
- Added a "Webcutter" ability to the Recluse Fang - allows you to cut through webs left by the new Broodfather.
- Added the ability to dungeonize the Scorpion Foil.
- Added a free Combat Gemstone Slot to the Scorpion Foil.
- Adjusted Spider Egg Mixin to now grant +15 Crit Damage.
- Adjusted Scorpion Foil's "Heartstopper" ability - now has a flat 2s cooldown.
- Adjusted Mosquito Bow - it is now a Shortbow.
- Adjusted Slayer Sacks to now unlock at Zombie Slayer II (Small), Spider Slayer II (Medium), and Wolf Slayer II (Large).
- Increased the amount of Combat XP required to spawn Spider Slayer bosses
  - T2: 600→650 Combat XP
  - T3: 1,000→1,500 Combat XP
  - T4: 2,000→3,000 Combat XP
- Reduced the amount of healing reduction immunity granted by the Tarantula Chestplate.
- Reduced the amount of Tarantula Webs required in the Tarantula Boots recipe.
- Reduced the cost of Tarantula Boots' Double Jump ability from 40 -> 20 mana.
- Removed Recluse Fang's "Squash 'em" ability and replaced it with a +150% Damage to Spiders passive.
- Replaced all obtainment sources of Bane of Arthropods 7 with the new "Ensnared Snail" endcap item.
Please note that any further changes mentioned on the July 8th Alpha Thread that have not yet been implemented are still planned for a later date.
## ► Kuudra Swapping Shenanigans
- Added support for swapping your Kuudra Armor Type.
  - The menu can be accessed with the /kuudratransfer command, or at the faction blacksmiths Alwin and Igor at the Crimson Isle.
## ► Changes to Unclaimed Event Rewards
As previously announced on our last release thread, we have now deployed a change that will cause any Event rewards (Spooky Festival, Carnival, etc.) to expire if it has been 10 or more SkyBlock years since they were given. If you have any rewards sitting around that haven't expired already, now is the time to claim them.
## ► Damage Reduction Fixes
When it came to damage reduction, some items were working in strange and mysterious ways. The most notable issue is being able to stack 100% damage resistance to get full damage immunity. Fear no more, for they have been corrected!
- Adjusted how damage reduction was calculated in Pets, resulting in lower reduction than stated.
- Adjusted End Stone Sword's ability to now gain damage reduction for every 100 mana consumed (was 5) and added a cap at 20% multiplicative damage reduction.
- Adjusted the following items from an additive damage reduction to a multiplicative reduction instead:
  - Monster Hunter’s Set Bonus
  - Magma Lord Armor and Magma Lord Gauntlet
  - Taurus Helmet, Flaming Chestplate, Moogma Leggings, Slug Boots
  - Thunder Armor and Thunderbolt Necklace
  - Wither Impact and Wither Shield
  - Soulweaver Gloves
  - Pumpkin Armor
  - Ender Relic/Artifact
  - Nether Artifact
  - Zombie Artifact/Ring/Talisman
  - Spider Artifact/Ring/Talisman
  - Wolf Ring/Talisman
  - Fire Talisman
  - Lava Talisman
  - Wither Relic/Artifact
- Fixed an issue which allowed all variations of the Sea Creature Accessories to stack their damage reduction together.
## ► Bug Fixes
- Fixed an issue preventing the placing of deployables near the Soul Campfire.
- Fixed an issue causing Cinderbats to die to lava when attempting to lasso them.
- Fixed an issue causing scaffolding to despawn when sneaking near the edge or jumping on it.
- Fixed the Sea Creature Guide not detecting when you'd killed a Tadgang for the first time.
- Fixed an issue causing Black Holes not pulling in mobs correctly while underwater or when obstructed by transparent blocks such as wheat.
- Fixed an issue allowing players to circumvent Emerald Blade's Curse of Greed by placing it in a Huntaxe.
- Fixed an issue allowing players to fish up Common shards from a GOOD CATCH! while using Wooden Bait in the Tomb Floodway.
- Fixed an issue allowing Pandas to phase through blocks when growing via Bamboo.
- Fixed collecting an auction while nicked shows the nick to your co-op members.
- Fixed some typos and cleared up some descriptions.
- Fixed Crystal Nucleus Guide sometimes not opening correctly.
- Fixed being able to damage sea creatures while wearing Hunter Armour using Explosive Arrows.
- Fixed non-Zombie kills on Reaper/Revenant Armor counting.
- Fixed Dwarven Mines events color on scoreboard not displaying correctly.
- Fixed not being able to use Etherwarp on carpet.
- Added an "Essence Guide" button to the Forest Essence Shop.
- Added a menu icon to claim coins from inaccessible co-op bank, due to the profile being a solo profile.
- Added Unlock Cost to HOTM and HOTF Ability descriptions.
- Changed requirements to reset HOTM and HOTF to their first perks (Mining Speed for HOTM and Sweep for HOTF).
And that's all for this update! We hope you enjoy the challenge posed by the Primordial Broodfather - now get out there and get slaying!

# Changelog/2025/July 22/1
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
ANNOUNCEMENT
Regarding Event Rewards and Habanero Tactics
Updated: July 22, 2025
Hey Guys!
We've decided to postpone the deletion of unclaimed event rewards for another 2 weeks.
Weeks ago, we added a warning inside of the claim event rewards menu letting you know that from now on, after 10 SkyBlock years, your rewards would expire and would no longer be obtainable. We warned players through patch note threads and this menu, and today we rolled the change that officially made it a reality.
We realized after receiving player feedback that the communication for this change may not have reached everyone. For this reason, we've decided to restore all event rewards, and delay this change for another 2 weeks.
Upon logging in, from now on, if you have unclaimed event rewards, you will be notified in chat. Please make sure you claim your event rewards, and avoid leaving event rewards unclaimed. On the 5th of August, we will re-enable deletion of event rewards.
We apologize for any confusion or panic this might have caused.
This patch also includes the fix for Habanero Tactics not applying Magic Find buffs on kills; the buff is now inherent to the Magic Find calculation similar to Bestiary or Magma Lord Set bonuses.

# Changelog/2025/July 28

PATCH
[July 28] SkyBlock Patch Notes
Updated: July 28, 2025
Hi,
Following our previous wave of fixes, we have rolled some further changes that are related to Spider Slayer.
- The Shriveled Cornea is now obtainable.
  - The recipe unlocks at Spider Slayer 8, and requires 4 Shriveled Wasps and 64 Enchanted Eye of Ender.
  - The Bloodshot passive works on minibosses.
    - However, Barbarian Duke X, Ashfang, Bladesoul, Mage Outlaw, and Magma Boss are now classified as bosses and therefore won’t be affected by the ability.
- The Mosquito Pet's upgrade costs have been modified, as follows:
  - Crafting Recipe now requires 4 Digested Mosquito, 4 Tarantula Silk, and 1 Super Enchanted Egg.
- The Mosquito Pet's maximum Base Pest Chance has been increased from 40% to 50%.
- Kat upgrades for the Mosquito Pet are now as follows:
||COMMON → UNCOMMON|UNCOMMON → RARE|RARE → EPIC|EPIC → LEGENDARY|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Time in h|1 Day|2 Days|3 Days|5 Days|
|Coin Cost|250k coins|500k coins|1m coins|2.5m coins|
|Additional Cost|8x Tarantula Silk|16x Tarantula Silk|32x Tarantula Silk|64x Tarantula Silk|
- The Shellwise attribute now works exactly the same as a Dwarf Turtle Shelmet when at level 10.
  - At lower tiers, it now grants a chance to negate knockback instead of slightly reducing knockback.
- Egg sac spawn heights have been raised by a couple blocks.
- Revamped the Gravel Mines to be easier to navigate, and Gravel now respawns much faster.
### ► Shriveled Cornea Hotfix
Due to an issue that allowed players to get the Bloodshot reforge without using the reforge stone we've removed Bloodshot from all items. Players who previously used a Shriveled Cornea to apply Bloodshot will have one refunded to them within a few days. Keep an eye on Jerry if you're expecting a refund!
Update 7/31/25 Refunds have been sent out!
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!
Please note: We are still balancing and patching this release. This list is not exhaustive and is subject to change.

# Changelog/2025/July 8
Aliases: 0.23.1
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
0.23.1
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.23.1 - Hina's Chapters, Pet Crafting Changes, and more!
Updated: July 8, 2025
Hello!
This week we've got a new progression system that you can access by talking to Hina, an overhaul to how Pet Crafting works, and a whole bunch of smaller changes/bug fixes! Read on for all of the details!
NOTE: In the near future we'll be deploying a change that makes it so unclaimed Spooky Festival Rewards expire after 10 SkyBlock Years. Claim any old rewards you have now! spelt in coordinates
## ► Hina's Chapters
After receiving your Heart of the Forest from Hina you'll unlock Hina's Chapters, a new system which will guide you through Galatea's first island! Complete objectives to learn more about the island and get free rewards! Let us know what you think about this system, and if you would like to see it implemented in new islands from now on!
## ► Pet Crafting Changes
We've revamped how Pet Crafting works to remove any and all randomness. Any Pet you craft now starts at Common rarity, and you'll need to upgrade it at Kat now to get it up to Legendary. We've rebalanced all of the material, coin, and time costs. Generally speaking most Pets should be easier to obtain and upgrade for early game players, which is what our ultimate goal is. Since this update hit the Alpha Network we've done another balance pass over all of the resource costs for Pet upgrades. You can find all of the updated values here.
### ➥ New Pet Rarities
Now that all crafted Pets start at Common rarity, we needed to add some lower rarity versions of certain existing Pets.
- Added Common, Uncommon, and Rare Blaze Pets
- Added Common, Uncommon, and Rare Glacite Golem Pets
- Added Common, Uncommon, and Rare Pigman Pets
- Added Common, Uncommon, and Rare Wither Skeleton Pets
### ➥ Revamped Chicken Pet
We've revamped the Chicken Pet to make it more useful for early game players as an early game Farming Pet.
### ➥ Husbandry Drop Changes
We've taken this opportunity to make a couple of consistency changes to certain Husbandry items.
- Added Enchanted Cooked Rabbit (crafted with x160 Enchanted Raw Rabbit)
- Enchanted Rabbit Hide Recipe: x576 → x160 Rabbit Hide
- Enchanted Leather Recipe: x576 → x160 Leather
- Changed a variety of recipes that utilize Enchanted Leather to compensate for this change
- Rabbit Minion recipes now only use Raw Rabbit
- Fixed an issue where Rabbit Minions were only dropping half the Rabbit Hide/Rabbit's Feet they should've been
We want to make more improvements to SkyBlock's new player experience in the future, stay tuned!
## ► Other Changes
### ➥ Farming
- Added a line to Pest and Mouse traps that shows the trap limit
- /tptoplot now tries to teleport you to a yaw that's a multiple of 90°
- Farming For Dummies can now be applied to Vacuums
### ➥ Mining
- The Better Together event now takes into account players that are in your zone rather than distance in blocks
- Reduced the collection requirements for Glacite, Tungsten, and Umber
- Added Suspicious Scrap to the Dwarven Sack
- Flowstate now only requires Enchanting XV to use
- Eternal Flame Rings can now be sold to NPCs for 10,000 coins
### ➥ Combat
- Blood God Crests now have a cap of +7 Strength
- Blood God Crests can now be bought more than once
- Increased the drop rate of Rampart Armor from Bezals from 0.2→1%
- Increased the drop rate of Cyclamen Dye from Matcho
### ➥ Fishing
- Reverted fix to being able to catch Obfuscated 1 Trophy Fish outside of the Crimson Isle
- Added Diver Fragments to Fishing Sacks
- Adjusted the action speed of Fishing Minions so that it's better distributed across upgrade tiers
- Increased the weight of Bayou Sludge
- Reduced the Rusty Coins required to purchase Backwater Armor and Equipment
- Increased the George Sell Price of the Mythic Hermit Crab Pet to 2,000,000 coins
- Added Enchanted Glowstone Dust as a drop from Fireproof Witches
- Reduced the drop rate of Enchanted Glowstone Blocks from Fireproof Witches (because of the above change)
- Reduced the NPC Sell Price of Fried Feathers to 1,000 coins
- Bobbin' Time now requires Enchanting XX to use (swapped with Flowstate)
### ➥ Miscellaneous
- Unclaimed Spooky Festival rewards are now only saved for 10 SkyBlock Years
- Added Enchanted Ancient Claws to the Event Sack
- /ahsearch now tries to automatically select the Auction House category with the most results
- You can no longer sell Abiphones to NPCs
- Highlighted → the fact that Pets in the Dark Auction are sold at Lvl 1. seek spreadsheet
- Updated the Booster Cookie description on Ironman Profiles. like a spelling bee, spell with three, this ain't no fake neuroscience degree
## ► Bug Fixes
### ➥ Farming
- Fixed Chicken Pet's Light Feet perk not working properly
- Fixed Lotus Equipment not requiring double tap to drop them
- Fixed Rancher's Boots being able to trample dirt under Cocoa Beans
- Fixed Cow Hat not granting immunity from Trapper Animal debuffs
- Fixed being able to reach the Desert Settlement without hitting the requirement
### ➥ Mining
- Fixed "When you gain Cold" autopet rule triggering when leaving the Glacite Tunnels
- Fixed Redstone Pigmen losing aggression shortly after being provoked
- Fixed Drill rarity not being updated in chat after recombobulating it
- Fixed Gemstone Infusion's description mentioning Pickaxes instead of Drills
- Fixed Abandoned Quarry using the wrong color in the Ticket Master Dialogue
### ➥ Combat
- Fixed being able to get stacks for Crimson Armor with non-melee kills (like magic and arrows)
- Fixed not getting stacks for Terror Armor when hitting a Reindrake
- Fixed Dropship deaths not counting towards Dropship Bestiary
- Fixed the Annihilation Cloak not carrying over data from the Destruction Cloak when upgrading
- Fixed Soul Eater Ultimate Enchant not working on the Flower of Truth/Bouquet of Lies' abilities
- Fixed Lifeline Attribute not taking into account Absorption Hearts
- Fixed Enderman Pet's "Teleport Savvy" perk not scaling with level
- Fixed Skeleton Pet's "Skeletal Defense" perk insta-killing certain enemies through bypassing damage caps
- Fixed Tarantula Pet's "Arachnid Slayer" not working on Cave Spiders
- Fixed an issue with an S Rank quest in the Crimson Isle requiring way too many Blobfish
- Fixed Kuudra upgrades saying you didn't have enough Tokens when you had exactly enough
- Fixed blocks from the Headless Horseman's reward platform not going away
- Fixed being able to use the Spider Boot's ability as a ghost
- Fixed Delirium Necklace not increasing immediate fire damage

### ➥ Fishing

### ➥ Miscellaneous
- Fixed being able to teleport around corners with the Aspect of the End/Void
- Fixed Hoppity calling you with Rabbits he has already sold you this SkyBlock Year
- Fixed instances where multiple pairs of Guardian Pets could show up in the same game of Superpairs
- Fixed Minion recipes not showing requirements in the upgrade and next tier menus

- Fixed island guests being able to trigger pressure plates with Grappling Hooks

- Fixed some Booster Cookie exclusive commands not being mentioned in the Booster Cookie menu

- Fixed the SkyBlock menu staying on your cursor when opening via the player inventory.
- Fixed holograms not being displayed once a chunk has been unloaded.
### ➥ Not-So-Important Typo/Grammatical Fixes
- Fixed Time Pocket listing the wrong collection requirement
- Fixed Hattie having redundant dialogue
- Fixed a grammatical error in the drop message for Dyes
- Fixed a grammatical error in the Titanoboa spawn message
- Fixed a typo in the Fishing Merchant's dialogue
- Fixed Enchanted Clock messages lacking consistency
- Fixed the word "Essence" not being colored in Bestiary listed drops
- Fixed Fashion Shop Dyes having a miscolored period
- Fixed Gavin's Dialogue using British English spelling
- Fixed the Red Sand and Mycelium Minions listing their Faction requirement twice
- Fixed Condensed Fermento being classified as a Collection Item
- Fixed Enchanted Titanium being classified as a Collection Item
## ► Followup Fixes
- Fixed Enchanted Titanium not being compactable.
- Removed HOTM/HOTF skill tree reset price.
- Fixed and re-enabled Winter Serendipity Attribute.
That's all for today! look again

# Changelog/2025/June 23
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
0.23
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.23 - The Foraging Update
Updated: June 23, 2025
Hello,
The Foraging Update is here, and it's the most technically ambitious update in the history of Hypixel SkyBlock.
Today, we're proud to finally reveal it to you. Thank you for your patience!
EDIT 8:20pm EST: We have disabled Tree Gifts and Black Holes while we investigate some issues. We expect to have these resolved shortly; thanks for your patience.
EDIT 9:10pm EST: A fix for the above issues are now rolled and should be re-enabled now; for any issues please report them at hypixel.net/bugs! Loot Share for Shards will remain temporarily disabled until we have a more permanent fix.
## ► Latest Version
This is the first SkyBlock update to fully utilize mechanics from the latest versions of Minecraft.
To access The Park and Galatea, you'll now need to play on Minecraft 1.21.5 or higher .
Don't worry; the rest of SkyBlock remains fully accessible on older versions.
This is just the beginning, but here's what you can already find on the new islands:
- New blocks
- New mobs
- New particle effects
- New sounds
- New swimming mechanics
- And more!
This update lays the foundation for something much bigger. We're now able to ramp up the complexity and quality of our content using the latest Minecraft tech.
If you're interested in how far we're taking it, stay tuned! We'll be releasing a separate dev blog soon covering resource packs, custom menus, models, and more.
## ► The Park Revamp
We're planning to gradually upgrade all islands to the latest version — and, with each one, improve its gameplay and design. The Park is the first island to receive this treatment.
Here's what's new:
- The main Foraging tutorial quest has been completely revamped and streamlined.
- The Foraging Collections have been improved.
- There are now early game Foraging Armors and new items tied to the new progression system.
- Meet Molebert , a new NPC with a short quest for curious players.
## ► Galatea
The Moonglade Marsh is the first of three planned Foraging Islands of Galatea, each with its own trees and mysteries.
### ➥ Heart of the Forest
When you first arrive, you'll meet Hina , who grants you access to the Heart of the Forest, the core of Galatea's progression system.
Just like the Heart of the Mountain, you'll level up this system, unlocking perks and upgrades as you go.
Its main currency is Forest Whispers , earned by cutting down trees across the island.
### ➥ Swoop
At the entrance of the village, you'll meet Swoop , your first Foraging guide.
He teaches you the basics of tree cutting on Galatea and tracks your Foraging Milestones, rewarding you as you chop your way through the island.
### ➥ Trees Toughness, Styles and Gifts
- Sweep is a new stat that determines how many logs you can cut in a row, similar to the Treecapitator effect.
- Toughness is a property of each tree that reduces the impact of your Sweep stat, requiring more Sweep to take down tougher trees.
- Style defines the correct order in which each tree should be chopped. Cutting them the wrong way is inefficient.
There are two types of trees on the first island of Galatea:
### Fig Trees
- Toughness: 7
- Style: Start with the Trunk , then finish with the Branches
- You can throw your axe into the air to cut branches! On Foraging islands, all axes can be thrown, though thrown strikes only cut half the usual logs.
### Mangrove Trees
- Toughness: 50 (We recommend at least 50 Sweep before attempting these!)
- Style: Start with the Branches , then the Trunk , and finally the Roots
Once a tree is fully cut down, any player who contributed at least 10% to its removal will receive a Tree Gift .
These contain:
- Foraging EXP
- Heart of the Forest EXP
- Forest Essence
- Unique Collection Items
- And sometimes... surprises.
Working together with other players to fell trees is not just helpful; it's rewarding.
### ➥ New Collections
The Moonglade Marsh, Galatea's first island, introduces 6 brand new collections tied to specific resources found on the island.
Spoiler: Click to view details on the six new Collections!
#### Fig Log Collection
Unlocked by cutting down Fig Logs. This collection focuses on the foundations of foraging progression:
- Scaffolding Items
- Entry level Foraging Armor
- First tier of Foraging Axes
- The Sparrow Shard
- +1 Foraging Level Cap
#### Mangrove Log Collection
Unlocked by cutting down Mangrove Logs. This collection is a step up, offering more specialized tools and gear:
- A new Reforge Stone
- Start of the Foraging Equipment progression
- The Seagull Shard
- +1 Foraging Level Cap
#### Tender Wood Collection
Obtained from Tree Gifts dropped by Fig Trees. It unlocks a few hunting tools:
- Hunting Trap recipes
- A new Accessory line
#### Vinesap Collection
Obtained from Tree Gifts dropped by Mangrove Trees. It focuses on new hunting gear:
- Hunting Weapon recipes
- Hunting Lasso recipes
#### Sea Lumies Collection
Unlocked by collecting Sea Pickles found underwater. This aquatic collection is packed with utility:
- Fishing Net recipes
- Recipes for Diver and Abyssal Armors
- Bubbles of Air recipe
- The new Scuba Enchantment
- Pressure Accessories to aid in deep-water exploration
#### Lushlilac Collection
Unlocked by harvesting berries across the island. It introduces:
- Salt Items, used to hunt Combat creatures
### ➥ Agatha, the first Starlyn Sister
Throughout each in-game day, Agatha will track your activity across the island, awarding you points for:
- Cutting Trees
- Collecting Berries and Sea Lumies
- Hunting
- Fishing
- Killing monsters
At the end of each day, she'll reward you based on your total score, including a special currency called Agatha's Coupons, which you can spend in her personal shop.
### ➥ And More!
Galatea is packed with secrets and systems waiting to be uncovered.
Here are just a few more things to look out for:
- Pressure , a new mechanic that will make your underwater exploration challenging.
- Beacons, that you will need to turn to grant island-wide buffs to everyone on the island.
- Boosters , new modifiers that enhance your Foraging items with unique properties.
- Coral , the enigmatic witch of the Fish Family , and many more unique NPCs.
- Combat monsters , both on land and deep underwater.
- The first Starborn Temple.
- ...and plenty of Hunting Creatures that roam the wild
## ► Hunting
The release of attributes brought a wave of new possibilities, but also several design challenges, as outlined in previous design threads.
With the Hunting update, we introduce a brand new skill that adds layered gameplay, fresh progression, and most importantly, transforms attributes into permanent buffs tied to your profile.
### ➥ David Hunterborough
In the village of Galatea, you'll quickly meet David Hunterborough, a brilliant hunter who will teach you the fundamentals of this new skill.
Progress far enough under his guidance, and he'll reward you with his prized David's Cloak , the most powerful cloak in the game.
### ➥ Hunting Tools
Hunting revolves around tracking and capturing a wide variety of unique creatures, each with their own Shard to collect. There are multiple ways to hunt and acquire shards, depending on the species:
- Some creatures feature unique, custom hunting mechanics
- Others can be caught using Lassos or Fishing Nets
- Certain shards are obtained via shops, crafting recipes, or the Fusion Machine
- Some creatures are hunted through combat, using specialized Hunting Weapons
It may sound like a lot but don't worry; it quickly becomes intuitive as you progress.
### ➥ Attributes and Menus
When you obtain a creature's Shard, it will be stored in your Hunting Box, found within the Hunting Skill Menu. From there, you can Syphon the shard to unlock or level up the corresponding Attribute, permanently.
- These new Attributes work similarly to the old system, but they are no longer tied to items
- All active Attributes can be seen inside the Attribute Menu, also within the Hunting Skill interface
- If you already own attributes from before, you can use the Transfer System to obtain the new shards
- Players with "Godrolls" will receive Ananke Feathers, based on the value of that Godroll.
### ➥ Fusions
Finished leveling an Attribute? Your extra shards don't go to waste.
Head over to the Fusion Machine and start experimenting, simply select a shard, pair it with another, and see what combinations appear.
- Each fusion will present 1 to 3 possible outcomes, including exclusive Fusion-only shards
- Most fusions will require 5 copies of each shard, so plan accordingly
- There's an element of discovery here, and some rare combinations may lead to unexpected rewards
Fusions are still ongoing large balancing changes, and we plan to finalize the details within a week.
### ➥ Kuudra
The Kuudra loot table has been overhauled to fit this new ecosystem.
Key changes:
- Significantly increased Crimson Essence gains
- Existing Crimson Isles shards remain obtainable, alongside new exclusive shards
- 1 to 3 Kraken Shards will now drop from every Paid Chest , based on the fight's tier
- Ananke Feathers have been added, powerful items that can progress any RNG meter
- Enrager is no longer obtainable, however there is a new item: the Tormentor , rare, but reasonably attainable
- The Hellstorm Wand has received a full revamp
- and more!
## ► More changes
### ➥ Changes to items
- The Diver Armor is now unlocked in the Sea Lumies Collection, and grants Pressure Resistance.
- Emperor Skulls now craft a new Accessory line.
### ➥ New accessories from the Mountaintop
The Respiration Accessory line can now be crafted using Puff Cruxes from the Mountaintop.
## ► What's Next
As usual, the list below is not exhaustive, it's just what we're comfortable sharing at this point.
Spoiler: Click to read all about our current plans!
Foraging Follow-up patch and future islands
We have already a few improvements planned and being developed for the Moonglade Marsh, and will release patch notes in the following week alongside eventual balancing changes.
The Hunting Update changed the whole Attribute system and we will be monitoring the effects closely during the following weeks. In particular, Crimson Isle attribute-centric content will be revisited with necessary updates to them.
While we have already planned out the second and third islands of Galatea, production is on hold until we have gathered enough feedback from this update.
Mythological Ritual v2
We are looking to release an update in the near future that will modernize Diana's Mythological Ritual event, adding some new mobs, drops and, obviously, the mythic griffin pet.
Slayer Content
Back at the start of the year, we promised all of you some slayer content. While we ended up reallocating some of our resources to the Foraging project, we took that time to design a brand-new slayer that we hope to release later on this year. All of our slayer content is now either in the later stages of development or awaiting QA.
Year of the Witch
The Year of the Witch looks to be the third event of its type, this time centered around Alchemy.
As part of the Year of the Witch event, players will need to seek out particular mobs, killing them to gather ingredients. Ingredients can be used to gain permanent buffs or as currency in the Year of the Witch Shop. Also...we will be adding a Seal Pet with this update!
Lotus Atoll
The Lotus Atoll is our next fishing island and will be accessed via the Ship Navigator. It's going to contain some Trophy...Frogs?
Pet Crafting Changes
One of our upcoming goals is to improve the new player experience of SkyBlock. One of the ways we are planning doing this in the near future is through moving some Pets earlier in collections and making them cheaper to craft. For awhile we've wanted to remove randomized Pet crafting in the game, and we decided to take this opportunity to do that.
All crafted Pets will now always start at Common rarity, but their early Kat upgrades are being changed to be incredibly cheap and quick to compensate. No more waiting for Diana!
As always, we truly hope you will enjoy this update. Thanks for helping us shape the future of SkyBlock. — The Hypixel SkyBlock Team

# Changelog/2025/March 18
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
0.22
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.22 - The Backwater Bayou
Updated: March 18, 2025
Hello,
As of today, Hypixel SkyBlock now has its first dedicated fishing island! The Backwater Bayou brings with it rod parts, fishing hotspots, and a wide array of new sea creatures, adding more depth to the fishing skill.
Read on to find out more!
## ► Backwater Bayou
The Backwater Bayou can be accessed from the Fisherman's Hut upon reaching Fishing Level 5. After helping Fisherman Gerald acquire the last necessary part to get his ship up-and-running, you can speak to Captain Baha to set sail for the Bayou.
The Bayou is the first of what will hopefully be many fishing-themed islands that are accessible via this new Ship system. While the Bayou is static - always available and always accessible, future islands may be more fleeting.
Once you arrive at the Backwater Bayou, there are several key activities for you to partake in:
- Learn about Junk Fishing from Junker Joel
- Apply Rod Parts at Roddy
- Learn about Fishing Hotspots from Hattie
- Fish for Bayou Sea Creatures and Hotspot Sea Creatures!
## ► Junk Fishing
Junker Joel has lived in the Bayou for some time, and dislikes the state that its ended up in thanks to people dumping their trash in the waters of the area. Talking to Joel will give you the Junk Sinker, which you can attach to your rod to replace all Treasure catches with Junk! One man's trash is another man's treasure though - bring junk to Joel and you can trade it in for some goodies!
Be careful as you fish, however - exclusive Bayou Sea Creatures lurk in these waters, and they're not particularly fond of trespassers!
## ► Rod Parts
As part of this update, we have done a pass over fishing rod progression and phased out several existing rods, such as the Sponge Rod and Chum Rod. Now unusable, these rods can be scrapped at Roddy in exchange for a Challenging Rod and their relevant rod part, with this being the Sponge Sinker and Chum Sinker respectively.
When applied to a rod, the Sponge Sinker materializes a Sponge in the player's inventory whenever they fish something, and the Chum Rod does the exact same thing - but with Chum.
As is the case with drill parts, there are three varieties of rod parts, and you can have one of each applied to a rod at any one time:
- Hooks increase your chance to catch certain things.
  - Example: The Common Hook increases your chance to catch COMMON Sea Creatures by 25%
- Lines grant fishing-related stats.
  - Example: The Titan Line grants +2 Double Hook Chance
- Sinkers augment your gameplay in various ways.
  - Example: The Junk Sinker allows you to catch Junk while in the Backwater Bayou, which can be exchanged at Junker Joel.
Players can add or remove rod parts as they please by speaking to Roddy in the Backwater Bayou, or by calling him on their Abiphone. More rod parts will be added in future updates.
## ► Fishing Hotspots
Players can first learn about Fishing Hotspots by speaking to Hattie in the Backwater Bayou.
The general gist of hotspots is that, on public islands (currently limited to the Backwater Bayou, Spider's Den, and Crimson Isle), particle circles known as hotspots will occasionally pop up. Casting your rod into a hotspot will grant you the stat buff listed above it, which can be anything from Fishing Speed to Double Hook Chance.
If that wasn't enough already, each type of hotspot also comes with its own exclusive sea creatures!
## ► 14 New Sea Creatures
This update adds a whopping 14 new Sea Creatures to the game. 6 of these are exclusive to the Backwater Bayou, while the remaining 8 are split evenly between Water and Lava Fishing Hotspots.
You won't find a list of them here- - go get fishing!
## ► Fishing Balancing
A main goal of this update was to make fishing progression more linear and understandable. As such, a large number of QoL and balancing changes have been made, a list of which can be seen below:
### ➥ Treasure Balancing
- Treasure Chance is now an official stat. Sources of it have been adjusted accordingly.
- Treasure Loot Tables have been rewritten.
  - We aim for them to be better than before, but for treasure to be more difficult to reliably catch without a dedicated setup.
- Reduced Seal Talisman's Treasure Chance from 1% -> 0.1%.
- Reduced Seal Ring's Treasure Chance from 1% -> 0.2%.
- Reduced Frozen Amulet's Treasure Chance from 5% -> 0.5%.
- Reduced Golden Bait's Treasure Chance from 5% -> 4%.
- Added a tiered bonus to Diver's Armor that grants 0.5/1/1.5 Treasure Chance while underwater.
- Reduced the amount of Treasure Chance Luck of the Sea grants per level.
- Removed explanation of Treasure Chance from fishing level-up message.
- Adjusted Trophy Hunter Armor's "Odger's Blessing" ability into a Tiered Bonus.
### ➥ Sea Creature Balancing
- Decreased HP of Sea Walker from 1,000 -> 100.
- Decreased Damage of Sea Walker from 60 -> 10.
- Moved Sea Walker unlock from Fishing 2 -> 1.
- Increased Sea Walker Fishing XP from 68 -> 150.
- Decreased HP of Squids from 750 -> 50.
- Increased Squid Fishing XP from 41 -> 75.
- Increased Sea Guardian Fishing XP from 101 -> 250.
- Increased Sea Witch Fishing XP from 338 -> 400.
- Increased Reindrake's rarity from LEGENDARY -> MYTHIC.
- Loot Share now works on Sea Emperor Skulls.
### ➥ Collection Balancing
- Moved Ice Bait to Ice Collection II.
- Added Salmon Hat to Raw Salmon I.
- Moved Minnow Bait to Raw Fish I
- Moved Salmon Armor to Raw Salmon VI.
- Added the Umberella to Umber Collection IX.
- Added Common Hook to Lily Pad Collection V.
- Removed Angler Armor from Raw Fish I.
  - It is now purchased from the Fishing Merchant, as is Angler Equipment.
- Reduced rarity of Challenging Rod to Uncommon and removed it from Lily Pad Collection V.
### ➥ Rod Balancing
With many rods having been replaced with hooks, lines, or sinkers, their methods of obtainment have also seen them replaced with their more modern counterparts. You may claim any rods that have been removed from the museum at your island's Jerry.
- Replaced Phantom Rod in Phantom Fisher's drop pool with Phantom Hook.
- Replaced Shredder in Sea Emperor's drop pool with Shredded Line.
- Replaced Chum Rod in Moby's Shop with Chum Sinker.
- Replaced Ice Rod in Frozen Steve's drop pool with Icy Sinker.
  - This Sinker also currently has a Hook perk on it. It's not a bug and is intentional for the time being.
- Replaced Winter Rod in Sherry's Shop with Festive Sinker.
- Replaced Sponge Rod with Spongy Sinker at Sponge Collection II.
- Replaced Prismarine Rod with Prismarine Sinker at Prismarine Shard Collection IV
- Added 1x Combat Gemstone slot to Soul Whip.
- Added a "Deals 2x damage to Sea Creatures" passive to Soul Whip.
- Added 2x Combat Gemstone slot to Flaming Flay.
- Added a "Deals 3x damage to Sea Creatures" passive to Flaming Flay.
- Removed Polished Topaz Rod's ability & renamed it to Topaz Rod.
### ➥ Miscellaneous Balancing
- Removed the Underground Fishing Speed Reduction.
- Aquamarine Gemstones now give Fishing Speed instead of Sea Creature Chance.
- Adjusted the stats and gemstone slots granted by all fishing rods/fishing-related armors. Some have lost stats/slots and others have gained them.
- Added the ability to break ice near bobber cast to all rods by default.
- Added Loudmouth Bass to Seal's Treat Bag.
- Adjusted the Bobbin' Time recipe to include the new water orbs.
- Added 1M coin NPC sell price to Golden Bounty, A Beginner's Guide to Pesthunting.
- Added Guardian Pet to Experimentation Table loot pool.
  - Squid Pet has replaced it in the Treasure tables.
- Reworked Stereo Pants to now allow you to fish up Music Discs while worn.
- Removed Clay Crystal from the Hub & Wilderness.
  - Clay now drops from Bayou Sea Creatures.
- Removed Frost Walker enchantment.
- Added "Ascending Rarity" and "Descending Rarity" sorts to /scg.
- Removed the ability to move at supersonic speed while in water from Diver Armor.
- Updated Bobbin' Time recipe to include the Bayou Water Orb.
## ► Future Plans
As usual, the list below isn't exhaustive, and is just what we're ready to share at this time.
### ➥ Foraging
We know players have been waiting a long time for this update, and we appreciate your patience. Right now, we’re focused on quality assurance to ensure a smooth experience. If all goes well, we hope to enter the Alpha phase within the next few months!
### ➥ Slayer Content
We're still hard at work on our previously promised slayer content, it shouldn't be long now!
And that's all for today! The Backwater Bayou is playable now, so grab your rods and get fishing!

# Changelog/2025/March 25
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
0.22.1
SkyBlock Patch Notes 0.22.1 - General Changes and Bug Fixes
Updated: March 25, 2025
Hello!
Welcome to Hypixel SkyBlock Patch 0.22.1. Today’s update has a few minor changes and bug fixes.
## ► General Changes
- Added a way to check how many times the Experimentation Table has been refreshed in the day.
- Added a Garden Level requirement to Emissary Sisko.
- Added Dung Dye to all Pest Drops in the Pest Handbook.
- Added Fruit Bowl as a rare reward from Garden Visitors.
- Added Divine Gift II and III to the Bazaar.
- Added Sulphuric Coal to the Nether Sack.
- Book of Stats is now applicable to Wands.
- Changed Gemstone Gauntlet to only loot share gemstones to players who also attacked with a Gemstone Gauntlet.
- Changed Glowing Mushroom Cave name color to aqua.
- The Enderman Slayer Level 9 Perk now grants an increased chance for a Superior Dragon to spawn.
## ► Bug Fixes
### Garden Fixes
- Fixed Garden plot prices for some people who had some of their progress reset due to a past issue.
  - Refunded players who, as a result, paid extra for their plots.
- Fixed visitors, pests, and composter offline progress resetting when getting kicked from the Garden.
### Item Fixes
- Fixed Extremely Real Shuriken description only specifying "when killed by you" instead of any player.
- Fixed Giant Bouncy Beach Balls not popping when hitting a wall.
- Fixed Magma Lord Armor’s Fireproof ability requiring the full 5-set instead of 2 for activation.
- Fixed "Storm/Thunder/Hurricane in a Bottle" all filling when in inventory.
- Fixed the Vanquished Equipment Stat bonuses applying twice.
### Enchanting Fixes
- Fixed an issue where the Experimentation Table was closing if you bought experience.
- Fixed being able to apply the Golden Bounty item to Scavenger Books.
- Fixed being able to buy experience bottles from the Experimentation Table on Stranded/Ironman modes.
- Fixed Experimentation Table’s RNG Meter not displaying accurate odds for Ultra Rare items.
- Fixed Ultimate Wise not reducing the mana cost of the Maniac Miner ability.
### Fishing Fixes
- Fixed coins caught from fishing not showing in the scoreboard.
- Fixed fishing rods with soulbound rod parts not being soulbound.
  - To clarify: Applying a soulbound rod part to an unsoulbound fishing rod will temporarily soulbind the fishing rod. Removing the soulbound rod part will unsoulbind the fishing rod.
### Pet Fixes
- Fixed being able to use some disabled bows with the Skeleton Pet’s "Skeletal Defense" perk.
- Fixed Goblin Pet’s "Fetid Thief" perk incorrectly displaying the name of the perk.
- Fixed Magma Cube Pet’s "Slimy Minions" perk description not mentioning working on Magma Cubes.
- Fixed Reindeer’s "Icy Wind" ability not doubling Ice Essence when killing Winter Sea Creatures.
### Misc. Fixes
- Fixed a double space in the Flaming Fist item description.
- Fixed a typo in Pandora's Box item description.
- Fixed a typo in the Ember Armor full set bonus description.
- Fixed a typo in the Griffin Pet's Odyssey Perk.
- Fixed a typo in the Jerry Box click description.
- Fixed a typo in the Kuudra Believer intro dialogue.
- Fixed a typo in the Skeleton's Helmet Ability message.
- Fixed Arthur's Wheat Minion in the Hub having a Compactor.
- Fixed being able to buy rabbits that you don’t meet the requirements for from Hoppity over the phone.
- Fixed being able to place Enchanted Clay Blocks on Private Islands.
- Fixed being unable to select Mithril Grubbers in the Bestiary Widget in the Dwarven Mines.
- Fixed Fiery Burst Rune not working with Cactus Knifes.
- Fixed Finnegan's GOATed perk not applying to some contests.
- Fixed Ice Minions being 5% faster for no apparent reason.
- Fixed inputting custom prices with commas giving incorrect prices.
- Fixed minion and furniture counts being off by 1.
- Fixed Rhinestone Infusion (Diamond Essence Shop Perk) giving more SkyBlock XP than intended.
- Fixed Sirius' name being the wrong color in Lucius' dialogue.
- Fixed Spooky Festival bits rewards sometimes not being granted.
- Fixed "Starred" items from not counting toward Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X's quest.
- Fixed the particle quality toggle not affecting Gemstone Crystal particles.
- Fixed the SkyBlock Guide’s Mythic Rabbit section showing an incorrect amount of rabbits.
- Fixed the RNG meter re-selected message showing twice in some scenarios.
- Fixed usage of /wikihand with Enchanted Wheat showing a non-existent page.
## ► Hotfixes
These are issues that popped up during the release that we fixed.
- Fixed Loot Share not working.
- Fixed Lava Rods not being able to be cast in lava.
- Fixed Ultimate Wise not working on wands.
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!

# Changelog/2025/November 20
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
ANNOUNCEMENT
MVP++ Improvements and 2025 Black Friday Sale
Updated: November 20, 2025
Today we're rolling some new improvements to MVP++ for SkyBlock along with the start of our annual BLACK FRIDAY SALE. With incredible savings that are up to 85% OFF, there are discounts on Ranks, Boosters, and more! This year we also have two new bundles and a bonus skin when purchasing the 16,400 SkyBlock Gem pack.
## ► MVP++ Improvements
We've made a handful of changes to the MVP++ rank:
- Everyone with MVP++ will now earn 500 SkyBlock Gems each month
- New MVP++ Subscriber Emblems in SkyBlock
  - Progress towards new Emblems is cumulative and based on how many months of MVP++ you have been subscribed to total
  - There are 11 Emblems total, with the last being reached at 60 months
- MVP++ is now subscription-based and can be auto-renewed
  - Prepaid days can still be purchased in the in-game store
  - Active prepaid days will be paused if you purchase a subscription
- Added two new Lobby Statuses exclusive to MVP++ players

  - MVP++ for <duration> months
### ➥ Other SkyBlock Changes
- Added a Wardrobe Slots Account Upgrade which allows you to purchase up to 9 new Wardrobe slots
  - The first tier of this upgrade is free to start, second tier is 250 SkyBlock Gems to start, and the rest are 500 SkyBlock Gems to start.
- Elizabeth will now give all players 75 SkyBlock Gems the first time you talk with her
- Gems are now displayed on the scoreboard while in the Community Center
## ► Black Friday Sale
From now until November 30th we'll also be running our annual Black Friday Sale with incredible savings that are up to 85% OFF! We've got two new bundles this year and a bonus Awakened Wither Helmet Skin when purchasing the 16,400 SkyBlock Gems pack.
### ➥ Black Friday Bundle (2025)

# Changelog/2025/November 4
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
0.23.6
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.23.6 - Mythological Ritual v2 & Vesuvius
Updated: November 4, 2025
Hello!
Today we've released version 0.23.6 of Hypixel SkyBlock, an update which introduces a whole host of new content to the Mythological Ritual along with Vesuvius, a useful NPC for Kuudra enthusiasts. Keep reading for all the details!
## ► Mythological Ritual v2
Designer Note: This content will become available when Diana is next elected with her "Mythological Ritual" perk active. Word around the election booth is that she might be in the running again very soon.
As part of this update, Mayor Diana's Mythological Ritual perk has undergone a massive revamp that brings with it a whole host of new mobs, drops, treasures, and QoL changes. You can read more about the changes below.
### ➥ Mythic Griffin Pet
The Griffin Pet can now be upgraded to its final rarity, Mythic, using the Mythic Griffin Upgrade Stone, which can be crafted using a number of valuable items obtained by playing the Mythological Ritual.
Upgrading your Griffin to Mythic unlocks 2 new mobs for you to take on - the Manticore, and King Minos himself - both of which have some particularly valuable drops.
Firstly, the Manticore can drop its Fated Stinger, which can upgrade the maximum level of the Venomous enchant on a weapon by one level. Not only this, but it can also drop the Manti-core, which can be crafted into the Manticore Claw, a particularly potent piece of combat equipment.
Lastly, King Minos himself might just make an appearance, if you should be so lucky. If you can defeat him and his trusty golden sheep, you might just find yourself left with a piece of Shimmering Wool. This extremely rare drop is used in crafting the Golden Fleece - a legendary cloak that's particularly useful for further increasing your rates during the Mythological Ritual, but also comes with some solid combat stats to boot.
### ➥ New Stat: Tracking
❃ Tracking is a new stat introduced as part of this update. For every 1 tracking you are 1% more likely to find mobs with the ♣ Elusive typing. Examples of mobs with this typing includes Field Mice, Diamond Goblins, Scathas, and Mythic Sea Creatures.
In terms of the Mythological Ritual, having more tracking will make you more likely to find the following ✿ Mythological mobs, which also have the ♣ Elusive typing:
- Minos Inquisitor
- Sphinx
- Manticore
- King Minos
A sizable amount of the new content added as part of this update will involve the Tracking stat, so keep it in mind!
Please note that we do not intend to add Inquisitor pity or anything similar for ✿ Mythological mobs - if you want to find them more often you simply need to increase your Tracking stat!
### ➥ Burrow Changes
Previously, there were always 4 burrows in a chain. Burrow #1 always contained an arrow, burrows #2 and #3 mob or treasure at a 75%/25% split, and #4 was guaranteed to be something from the burrow treasure table. Now, though burrow #1 remains always an arrow, all other burrows maintain the 75%/25% split between mob or treasure.
With this update, though the base burrow length remains at 4, we have added some new ways to extend burrows:
- The Archaic Spade and Deific Spades, upgrades to the Ancestral Spade, grant +2 and +4 burrow length respectively, in addition to allowing you to find more valuable items in burrows.
- The 'Erudite' reforge, which can be applied to any Spade via the Daedalus' Notes item, grants +2 burrow length.
With an optimal build, this means a maximum burrow length of +10 is possible. The benefits of longer chains are less arrows - meaning more burrows containing mobs or treasures - and, if you have a Mythic Griffin, a bonus of up to +10 Tracking on your final burrow in a chain, meaning you're far more likely to find ♣ Elusive mobs.
### ➥ Hephaestus Items
The Hephaestus Anvil can be crafted from 64x Enchanted Gold Blocks, 2x Braided Griffin Feathers, 1x Zog Anvil, 128x Enchanted Ancient Claw, and 64x Mythos Shards.
When placed in a crafting grid alongside 3 of any of the following items, all of the items will be consumed and a Hephaestus item will be created.
- Crochet Tiger Plushie
  - Becomes Hephaestus Plushie. Bonus Attack Speed buff increased from 35% -> 50%.
- Cretan Urn
  - Becomes Hephaestus Urn. Pet Luck increased from 5 -> 10.
- Antique Remedies
  - Becomes Hephaestus Remedies. Strength buff increased from 80% -> 100%.
- Minos Relic
  - Becomes Hephaestus Relic. % buff increased from 33% -> 50%.
- Dwarf Turtle Shelmet
  - Becomes Hephaestus Shelmet. True Defense increased from 10 -> 20.
- Washed-up Souvenir
  - Becomes Hephaestus Souvenir. Sea Creature Chance increased from 5% -> 10%.
### ➥ New Attributes!
Some of the new ✿ Mythological mobs can also be hunted, and will grant you some useful attribute shards for doing so!
- Cretan Bull (COMMON Shard):
  - Mythological Resistance: Grants +4-40 Defense against ✿ Mythological mobs.
- Minotaur (RARE Shard):
  - Mythological Ruler: Grants +0.5-5 Magic Find on ✿ Mythological mobs.
- Sphinx (EPIC Shard):
  - Big Game Hunter: Grants +0.5-5 Tracking on Griffin Burrows.
- King Minos (EPIC Shard):
  - King of Greed: Increases Coin gain from Griffin Burrows by 1-10%.
### ➥ Carnival Shop Perks
Stop right on by Foxy's carnival next time it's in town, for there are some new Mythological Ritual perks to be had!
|Perk Name|Perk Effect|Carnival Token Cost|
|-|-|-|
|Fabled Slayer I-V|Increases damage dealt to ✿ Mythological mobs by +5/10/15/20/25%.|50/100/150/200/250|
|Deadly Greed I-V|Gain +2/4/6/8/10% more Coins from Griffin Burrows.|50/100/150/200/250|
|Diana's Favor I-III|Grants +1/2/3 Magic Find on ✿ Mythological mobs.|100/200/400|
|Elusive Hunter I-II|Grants +2.5/5 Tracking while digging up Griffin Burrows.|100/1,000|
### ➥ Mythological Ritual QoL Changes
As part of this update, we have made a large number of changes to the existing Mythological Ritual in order to improve the overall gameplay experience:
- Added Griffin Upgrade Stones to the Bazaar.
- Added a mechanic to teleport Mythological mobs back faster when they fall into the void.
- Added NPC sell prices to several preexisting items:
  - Enchanted Ancient Claw: 32,000
  - Crochet Tiger Plushie: 250,000
  - Antique Remedies: 250,000
  - Dwarf Turtle Shelmet: 250,000
  - Washed-up Souvenir: 250,000
  - Daedalus Stick: 1,000,000
  - Minos Relic: 5,000,000
- Added itemlore/flavortext to several Mythological Ritual items.
- Added Ancestral Spade to the Museum (2 SBXP).
- Added +10 True Defense to the Dwarf Turtle Shelmet.
- Added a requirement of having a Griffin Pet equipped to damage other player's Mythological mobs.
- Added a new texture to the Zog's Anvil.
- Adjusted Minotaur's Bestiary from 4,000 kills to max -> 1,000 kills to max.
- Added a short Mythological Ritual guide to Mayor Diana's in-office menu.
- Added 50% Magic Resistance to all Mythological mobs that didn't already have it.
- Added "Right-click to view recipes!" to Ancient Claw, Enchanted Ancient Claw, Braided Griffin Feather.
- Adjusted Braided Griffin Feather's Bazaar section from Voidgloom Seraph -> Mythological Ritual.
- Adjusted the Sword of Revelations recipe, making it significantly cheaper.
- Adjusted Diana's Bookshelf, replacing +1 Pet Luck with +1 Tracking.
- Adjusted Beaconmite Attribute description to clarify it does NOT affect Private Island beacons.
- Adjusted Private Island Beacons, replacing Pet Luck Boost with Tracking.
- Adjusted Mythological mob knockback, making most mobs take less.
- Adjusted the Crown of Greed, replacing its ability:
  - Passive: Indulgence
    - Gain 2x Coins from Griffin Burrows and +10 Magic Find on Mythological mobs, but you take 1.25x damage from them.
- Adjusted the Crown of Avarice, replacing its ability:
  - Passive: Overindulgence
    - Take 1.5x damage from ✿ Mythological mobs but gain 5x Coins from them and Griffin Burrows. Coins gained are consumed by the helmet. Grants +0.0015x Damage and +2.5 Magic Find on ✿ Mythological mobs for each digit of coins consumed (Capped at 1B)
      - Designer Note: The damage bonus applies to all mobs, but the Magic Find only applies to Mythological ones.
- Adjusted the rarity of several pet items:
  - Washed-up Souvenir: LEGENDARY -> EPIC
  - Dwarf Turtle Shelmet: RARE -> EPIC
- Adjusted the recipes of the various Griffin Upgrade Stones so as to include the new drops.
- Adjusted Future Calories Talisman, replacing +1 Fishing Wisdom with +0.5 Tracking.
- Adjusted Mythological mob prefixes:
  - COMMON → No prefix
  - UNCOMMON → Blessed
  - RARE → Stalwart
  - EPIC → Venerable
  - LEGENDARY → Exalted
  - MYTHIC → Empyrean
- Adjusted the Blade of Daedalus:
  - Now only copies Combat Stats.
  - Slightly reduced the Magic Find per Mythological Bestiary tier due to new mobs being added to the event.
- Adjusted Griffin Pet's "Odyssey" perk to list how many Mythological mobs it can find at each tier (+2 per tier).
- Adjusted Griffin Pet's "King of Kings" perk to the following:
  - Grants +20 Magic Find on Mythological mobs.
- Adjusted Griffin Pet's base stats:
  - Added +25 Strength & +25 Bonus Attack Speed
  - Removed +10 Intelligence
- Removed Griffin Pet's "Legendary Constitution" perk and rescaled the existing perk unlocks:
  - COMMON: Odyssey
  - UNCOMMON: Odyssey
  - RARE: Odyssey, Sacred Strength
  - EPIC: Odyssey, Sacred Strength
  - LEGENDARY: Odyssey, Sacred Strength, King of Kings
  - MYTHIC: Odyssey, Sacred Strength, King of Kings, Ancient Earth
- Removed the random Minos Follower spawns when killing mobs throughout the game.
- Removed the ability to recombobulate the various Griffin Upgrade Stones.
- Removed mana cost from Ancestral Spade.
- Removed the ability to take knockback from Mythological mobs.
- Removed Diana's "Lucky!" perk, replacing it with the following:
  - Huntress' Intuition -> Grants +10 Tracking.
## ► Other QoL Changes
Quality-of-life changes to other areas of the game!
- Added the Pesthunter Relic to the Pesthunter's Wares. Costs 200 Pests + 1x Pesthunter Artifact.
- Added an Anguish Talisman line, crafted from various Ghost drops.
- Adjusted Ghost Bestiary from 100,000 kills to max -> 40,000 kills to max.
- Adjusted Venomous enchant as a result of adding Venomous VII, reducing the walk speed penalty inflicted and slightly reducing the venom damage per tick.
- Adjusted Enderman Slayer 9's perk from 15 Tracking -> 5 Tracking.
- Adjusted the amount of Soul String needed in several crafts:
  - Venom's Touch: 10x per slot -> 16x per slot
  - Venom's Touch (Fragged): 50x per slot -> 64x per slot
  - Spider Queen's Stinger: 6x per slot -> 16x per slot
  - Spider Queen's Stinger (Fragged): 6x per slot -> 64x per slot
## ► Vesuvius
Ever wanted to deal with all your Kuudra loot chests at once? Fear not, as Vesuvius has arrived. Vesuvius can be found at the Kuudra's Skull. He functions very similar to Croesus in the Dungeon Hub, the only difference being he only shows chests from Kuudra.
You can now queue for Kuudra in the Dungeon Hub. If you're looking for your chests, Croesus has you covered for both Dungeons and Kuudra. The layout of the Dungeon Hub has also been updated for a fresher look.
## ► Other Changes
- Hidden Elections have been removed.
  - We feel that with the minister system, players benefit a lot more from open elections and planning what they can do in the upcoming days than the potential suspense a hidden election provides.
- Fixed Wheel of Fate not working.
## ► What's Next?
As we are going through our backlog of updates, we are getting closer to some of our larger version releases. Everything shown here is subject to change !
### ➥ The Greenhouse
Before the year ends, the Garden is getting its most ambitious growth spurt yet. We’re introducing an entirely new system centered around the Greenhouse , alongside major changes to:
- Farming Tools
- Adjustments to both Crop and Visitor Milestones .
- A brand-new Farming currency , letting you invest in and upgrade Farming Chips that strengthen every aspect of your Garden.
Rather than one big grind, this update is designed to make Farming progress unfold more naturally, with meaningful upgrades waiting at every step.
If you haven't seen it yet, check out our previous sneak peek of the Greenhouse HERE!
## ➥ Galatea's Second Island: Torrhus Canyon
Still planned for next year, this new island is currently in early development.
If you encounter any bugs or unexpected behavior while playing this update, please report it HERE!
Thanks for your continued support, and see you next time!

# Changelog/2025/October 15
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
0.23.5
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.23.5 - Dungeonbreaker, Pity System, Ironman W's, and more!
Updated: October 15, 2025
Hello!
Today we've released version 0.23.5 of Hypixel SkyBlock, which includes the Dungeonbreaker pickaxe, a new /pity system to help out particularly unlucky players, various improvements to Ironman profiles, lots of quality of life (QoL) changes, and a few bug fixes! Keep reading for all the details!
## ► Dungeonbreaker
Where Dungeonbreaker?
The Dungeonbreaker is a new pickaxe that can be purchased at Ophelia for 500 Wither Essence. It allows you to mine through most blocks in the Catacombs.
The Dungeonbreaker has a couple of limitations:
- The number of blocks you can break is limited by how many Charges you have available
  - Charges regenerate at 2 blocks per second
  - You also regenerate up to 5 Charges when collecting a Secret with the new Echoes of the Lost perk
    - This perk can be found in the Wither Essence shop
- You cannot break blocks around room boundaries
- Crypts can't be broken with the Dungeonbreaker. They will still require Superboom TNT or an equivalent explosion effect, as previously
With the release of the Dungeonbreaker, you will no longer be able to "stonk" with previous methods. This allows us to enable anticheat measures that were previously disabled due to having to allow "stonking" as a mechanic.
The Dungeonbreaker is an important step in moving towards a fully latest version experience, as stonking was a vanilla exploit only found in Minecraft 1.8. While the Dungeonbreaker is designed for the latest version functionality in mind, it should be fully functional in older versions. However, we still encourage you to update your Minecraft version (and mods!) as soon as possible since a lot of future content will most definitely require the latest version.
## ► /pity
RNGesus not on your side? Say hello to /pity! This new menu keeps track of various new and pre-existing pity across the game. The addition of /pity does not mean we are discontinuing RNG Meters - this system just allows us to add hard pity for smaller systems that aren't large or complex enough to warrant a dedicated meter. The Pity menu can also be accessed through the /rng menu, or by typing /pity directly.
New Pity
- Hoppity's Hunt Pity
  - Every 500th Egg you open is guaranteed to contain a MYTHIC rabbit you don't already have
  - Every 2,500th Egg you open is guaranteed to contain a DIVINE Rabbit you don't already have
    - Designer Note: Rabbits from the Hitman count towards pity. Pity replaces the previously teased Hoppity's Hunt RNG Meter.
    - Designer Note: Upon pity release, players will receive pity progress of up to 1 new free divine and mythic rabbit based upon lifetime eggs opened.
- Garden Pity
  - Every 3,000th Visitor who visits once you are Garden Level 15 is guaranteed to be the Spaceman
    - Designer Note: Pity only increments if you are at the necessary Garden level to spawn the Spaceman.
- Moonglade Marsh Pity
  - Every 2,500th Treasure fished is guaranteed to be Fish The Fish
  - Every 2,500th mob killed is guaranteed to drop Mob The Fish
  - Every 1,000th Tree Gift is guaranteed to have Tree The Fish
Pre-Existing Pity
- Gold & Diamond Trophy Fish on the Crimson Isle
  - Every 100th Trophy Fish of a specific type is guaranteed to be GOLD
  - Every 600th Trophy Fish of a specific type is guaranteed to be DIAMOND
- Glacite Mineshaft spawning in the Glacite Tunnels
  - Base chance of 1/2,000, mining blocks decreases the 2,000 until you get a Mineshaft
## ► Ironman W's
There's a handful of improvements we've made to Ironman Profiles with this update.
### ➥ Ironman Shen's Auctions
Ironman profiles will no longer be bidding against Normal profiles in Shen's Auctions to help level the playing field a bit more. There will be about half the regular quantities of items available in Shen's Auction and Special. We anticipate the numbers will need tweaking, but we want to see how it plays out first.
Keep in mind that purchasing any items from Shen's Auction or Special will Co-op Soulbound the item.
### ➥ Convert To Normal Profile ("De-iron")
Tired of the Ironman experience? Solo and Co-op Ironman profiles now have the ability to convert to a Normal profile, although the process is a bit different for both.
If you're on a Solo Ironman profile, you can choose to start the conversion in the Profile Management menu. In most cases, you'll be required to wait a certain amount of time (up to 35 days) in case you change your mind. Once the timer is up, you'll be asked one last time if you are absolutely sure you want to de-iron, as the process is permanent!
For Ironman Co-ops, the process is a little different. Once someone in the Co-op wishes to convert, it will start a vote that lasts 3 days. During this time, if any player in the Co-op votes "NO" or fails to vote, the conversion process will stop. If the Co-op unanimously votes "YES", then the countdown starts (up to 35 days). If during this time any player in the Co-op has second thoughts, they are able to cancel the conversion process in the Profile Management menu.
### ➥ Cosmetic Auction House
Ironman profiles are now able to purchase most cosmetics listed on the Auction House (they are still not able to sell any). When opening the Auction House menu, they'll now see a custom browser with filters for various cosmetic types.
For Normal profiles, we've replaced the Blocks tab in the Auction House with a Cosmetics tab so that players can more easily find cosmetics. There wasn't much going on in the Blocks tab previously, and most of its items can now be found in the Tools & Misc tab instead.
On a side note, Ironman profiles can now purchase a custom amount of Booster Cookies from the Ironman Bazaar.
### ➥ Ironman Guild
Found above spawn in the Dwarven Mines, the Ironman Guild is an in-universe Guild with Ironman Dwarves. If you talk to Ian of Ironmen, he'll explain Ironman profiles, and he'll even open a menu for you to create an Ironman profile right there if you want.
## ► QOL Changes
Over the past few months, we've been gathering many of the most popular community suggestions.
### ➥ Dungeons
- Thorn Shard Changes
  - Now drops exclusively from Master Mode Floor IV 100% of the time in Emerald Chests and above as a bonus
  - Is now a LEGENDARY Shard with the ID of L48
- Added a Scarf Shard
  - Drops exclusively from Master Mode Floor II 100% of the time in Emerald Chests and above as a bonus
  - Grants 2-20% more Dungeon Class experience
- All of Livid's clones are now Humanoid/Arcane mob types rather than a bunch of different ones
### ➥ Farming
- Replaced the Mosquito Pet's 'Fool Me Once' perk with a new one
  - Perk: Bloodsucker's Betrayal
  - Effect: Pest Traps work 0.2-20% faster.
- Doubled the Pest damage buff provided by the Praying Mantis Shard
- Added the Elusive typing to the Field Mouse
- If you participated in multiple Farming Contests, you'll now receive the best medal out of the crops you farmed
  - Previously, it was simply given to the crop you farmed the most in, but this could occasionally result in you receiving a worse medal than you would've otherwise gotten
  - 10/15/2025 21:00 EST -> This change, as well as the 10 year event claim limit, has been reverted due to issues with claiming rewards.
### ➥ Fishing
- Bobbin' Time Ultimate Enchantment Changes
  - 10 ➜ 5 max players
  - +0.3/0.4/0.5% ➜ +0.6/0.8/1.0% stats per player
  - In effect, you get the same stats as before, but with half as many people
- The Totem of Corruption now counts as a Deployable, and it can only corrupt Sea Creatures that you fish up
- Chumcap Buckets now only allow their owner to fish up Agarimoo
- Ember Armor's 'Nether Lord' ability is now disabled near Fishing Hotspots
- Added Raw Cod to the Fishing Merchant
### ➥ Foraging
- Added Wet Water, Gill Membranes, Flexbones, Stretching Sticks, Sturdy Bones, Starlyn Prizes, Deep Roots, Bamboo, and Sticks to the Foraging Sack
- Added Wet Water, Gill Membranes, Sturdy Bones, Starlyn Prizes, Deep Roots, Warty, Lushlilacs, Candycombs, Oceandies, and Bonbons to the Bazaar
- Shellwise, Pandas, and Joydives now respawn faster
### ➥ Mining
- Mythic Armadillo Pet
  - Upgraded using a 'Frozen Scute', which is dropped rarely from Umber, Tungsten, and Vanguard Corpses
  - New perk 'Well-worked', which consumes up to 10% less Energy while tunneling
  - Mythic Armadillos have 400 Energy
- Added +2.5 Gemstone Spread to the Pendant of Divan's "Legacy" Tiered Bonus.
- The Travel Scroll to the Crystal Nucleus no longer has an MVP+ requirement
- Mithril Powder Buffs
  - Raffle
    - Participation Bonus: 5,000 ➜ 10,000 Mithril Powder
    - Per Ticket: 10 ➜ 25 Mithril Powder
  - Goblin Raid
    - Participation Bonus: 5,000 ➜ 10,000 Mithril Powder
    - Per Goblin: 20 ➜ 50 Mithril Powder
  - Mithril Gourmand
    - Participation Bonus: 2,000 ➜ 10,000 Mithril Powder
    - Removed the cap on Tasty Mithril
  - Commissions
    - ALL Dwarven Mines Commissions now give 500 Mithril Powder
    - ALL Crystal Hollows Commissions now give 1,000 Gemstone Powder
    - ALL Glacite Tunnels Commissions now give 1,500 Glacite Powder
    - Each of these are doubled if you are HOTM 10

- All Glacite Mineshafts now spawn with at least 10 mobs

  - They have a 50% chance on their 2nd, 4th, and 6th appearance to get that perk early
- Reduced the minimum weight needed to get a Skeleton the Fish from 1,000 LB Loudmouth Bass to only 50 LB
- Oops the Fish, Spook the Fish, and Chill the Fish can now be auctioned
- Golden Jerries are now elusive
Spoiler: NPC Sell Price Changes

## ► Other Changes
There are a handful of other changes being made with this update to help balance the game.
- Scorpion Bow Changes
  - Renamed to 'Spider Shortbow'
  - Made crafting recipe significantly cheaper
  - Deals x2 Damage to the Tarantula Broodfather's Egg Sacs
  - Is now a Shortbow
  - Reduced stats to compensate
- Mosquito Bow now deals x3 Damage to the Tarantula Broodfather's Egg Sacs
- Egg Sac Changes
  - Tier 3: 2 Egg Sacs -- 3 Health each
  - Tier 4: 3 Egg Sacs -- 6 Health each
  - Tier 5: 4 Egg Sacs -- 9 Health each
    - Designer Note: This has been reduced down from 5/10/15 Health each on Alpha.
- T5 Tarantula Broodfather's 'Til Death Do Us Part' ability now has 'Noxious Paralysis' ability and can Backstab
- T5 Tarantula Broodfathers now always have Magic Resistance instead of only being after its 'Bat Barrage' ability
- Points gained for Hunting during Agatha's Contests are now per-mob instead of per-shard to prevent players from getting way more Points than intended
- The Starborn Shard is now a Water shard instead of a Combat Shard
- Reduced the scaling of enchantments that increased damage to specific mob types
- Renamed 'Explorer' achievement to 'Wanderer', changed description to more clearly indicate that it requires you to visit every zone in the Hub, except beyond what's required for the 'Explorer' quest (except the Regalia Room)
- The Soul Eater Ultimate Enchantment is now capped at 1m Damage outside of Dungeons
- Removed Smite VII from Tomioka's shop
- Capped the 'Maniac Miner' Pickaxe Ability to 500 Mining Fortune
  - Designer Note: We anticipate this being a 10%~ cut to hourly rates from our testing. Mining pure ores in an early-game mining area was never intended to be the best money-making method for mining, and we will look into making adjustments to Gemstones in the future.
- Reduced the drop rate of Glacite Shards to 0.1% from various mobs
- Nerfed Enchanted Hoppers to only give 70% of an item's NPC sell price
- Unequipping Reaper or Final Destination Armor now takes away stats from their abilities if they were active
- Fossil item descriptions no longer mention that they give Fuel, as it's not their primary purpose
  - They can still be used for Fuel, but it's a "secret" feature
## ► Bug Fixes
As usual, we have fixed some more bugs with this release.
- Fixed Gauntlet of Contagion's damage scaling when it's just supposed to be a percentage of mob health
- Fixed Primordial Jockey's doing way more damage than intended
- Fixed Ragnarock's recipe being in the shape of an axe, despite now being a sword
- Fixed the Hollow Wand's 'Ichor Pool' damage buff persisting even after leaving radius
- Fixed the Great Spook Staff's 'Spooker' ability not working on Private Islands
- Fixed Vampire Witch Mask being able to damage certain immune Galatea mobs
- Fixed an issue with the 'Front Loaded' HOTM perk
- Fixed a rare issue when claiming items from The Forge
- Fixed Angry Archaeologists flying when touching water
- Fixed Bobbin' Time Ultimate Enchantment not applying to Magic Find
- Fixed Cooled Forges perk not rolling odds properly per each item
- Fixed anvil menu saying it will remove the enchantment when upgrading an Ultimate Enchantment
- Fixed typo in reclaim menu
- Fixed typo in Accessory Bag upgrade with Elizabeth
- Fixed being unable to complete these quests in Hina's Chapters:
  - Reach 50 Sweep
  - Reach 200 Sweep
  - Get at least 100 Pressure Resistance
- Added the Dark Red level bracket to the Century Party Invitation item
## ► Great Spook
The Great Spook event has been enabled and will last until November 5th.
We've also scheduled bonus Spooky Festival events towards the end of the month in celebration of Halloween.
- Friday, October 31st 3:35am EDT (regularly scheduled event)
- Friday, October 31st 11:35pm EDT
- Saturday, November 1st 7:35am EDT
- Saturday, November 1st 3:35pm EDT
- Saturday, November 1st 11:35pm EDT
- Sunday, November 2nd 3:35pm EDT
## ► What's Next?
Here is a quick update on the next major update coming to SkyBlock, along with a peek at what is to come next year. Keep in mind that this list is definitely not comprehensive; there are many things not mentioned here that we are working on, some even planned to come out later this year!
### ➥ The Greenhouse
Later this year, we're planning to release the biggest addition to the Garden since its initial release in February 2023. This update includes the main flagship feature of the Greenhouse, a revamp to Farming Tools, a nerf to Crop Milestones and Visitor Milestones, and a new powder-like currency for Farming (retroactive by the way!). You'll be able to spend this new currency on earnable and upgradable Farming Chips, which will provide a boost to all Garden-related content. Our goal with this is to make Garden progression feel smoother and more rewarding through regular upgrades as you play.
If you haven't seen it yet, check out our previous sneak peek of the Greenhouse HERE!
### ➥ Next Year: Second Foraging Island
We've begun pre-production of the second foraging island, which is slated to come out next year. We'll provide more updates on the project as we get closer to release.
### ➥ Next Year: Latest Version Content
Going into 2026, we have 2 goals: new combat content and more latest version features. We're really excited about all the cool new things we can bring to you guys in latest, so we want to remind everyone to move over to 1.21+ if you haven't already! All of our major content updates planned for 2026 are latest version only, don't miss out!
In the near future, we plan on only supporting the 2 latest content drops in addition to 1.8.9. This will allow us to utilize even more cutting-edge features for updates. You can read more about it in our announcement HERE!
If you encounter any bugs or unexpected behavior, please report it HERE!
That's all for today!

Howdy folks!
