It can now be fished up while using the Icy Sinker.
- Fixed a few other miscellaneous issues.
That's all folks! Thanks for the feedback, and if you find any more issues please be sure to report them HERE!

# Changelog/2025/September 16
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
ANNOUNCEMENT
Regarding Stocks of Sheep Firesales from last week
Updated: September 16, 2025
Hey Guys, I'm dropping in with some unusual bit of information regarding last week's fire sale. As many pointed out, we did sell more sheeps than the originally intended amount.
The whole situation is a bit of an accident, which I'll gladly explain. However, I want to begin by mentioning that this is not the result of a player exploit. As such, none of the extra skins will be deleted or wiped from the game. Everyone who spent gems to obtain the skins gets to keep them; nothing will be reverted or rolled back.
Now that that's out of the way, let's explain what happened.
## ► Understanding digital sales
Because of the way Hypixel Servers work, and how there are multiple instances on multiple servers at any point, when you make a transaction happen, there can be a few milliseconds of delay between server communications as servers report their actions to each other. Fire Sales are a unique system on the network that relies on a maximum quantity of items to sell, so every time a player purchases one, the server has to report that one must be removed from the stock.
As we approach the final few purchases, it is possible (and always has been possible) that multiple players go on to purchase the last item at the same time. This can cause the server to sell more items than are in stock. Historically speaking, ever since fire sales began, we have never experienced more than a few items sold over the limit.
Thankfully, those are digital sales, so we don't technically have a maximum we can sell. We choose the amount we want to sell with the intent of creating value for these items, as you are all already aware. This means that when we oversold by a few, there was no reason to cause any sort of errors; it's was best to just provide the purchased items to everyone who spent. On physical orders in other stores, this happens frequently, and usually leads to a lot of frustration from buyers being told that their order is actually out of stock, even though their money was taken. We did not want to do that; we preferred to keep things smooth and functional for everyone. Even if we ended up with a few bonus items sold, it was never enough to really affect the economy in any way.
## ► The Sheep Sales
Last week, a lot of things aligned on the sheep sales that caused a much higher amount of hype over this Firesale. It was the anniversary of fire sales, the sheep were coming back with new colors, there were multiple skins on the sale (so it needed to have lower limits per skin), it was accompanied by another mythic skin (who did not oversell) and due to the anniversary and the hype, we decided to sell the same amount of sheep we did on the original 6 colors - 250 each.
When the fire sale opened up, the whole stock of sheep sold out within two seconds. As explained above, it can take a second or two for sales to be reported to other servers, but the sheer volume of purchases that happened during those 2 seconds essentially all went through. These are the amounts of each color that sold:
- Blue Sheep: 1743
- Gray Sheep: 1712
- Red Sheep: 1552
- Green Sheep: 1472
- Orange Sheep: 1398
- Yellow Sheep: 1265
To put things into perspective, I have two examples of other fire sales:
- The Goblin Jinn, with a limit of 4000, only oversold by two copies (4002 items sold)
- The Black Chick chicken skin, with a limit of 3000, only oversold by 8 copies (3008 items sold)
## ► What now?
As mentioned earlier, we do not plan to remove, wipe, or delete extra sheep skins that sold. Everyone gets to enjoy what they paid for. We've identified solutions to the issue so this doesn't happen again, and we will be implementing them before the next fire sale. Following this change, it should no longer be possible under any circumstances to buy past the stock limit.
Furthermore, we won't be planning for all of these extra factors to align again (the firesale anniversary and the low quantity of multiple hyped items). We do believe players are happier when their purchases hold greater value, and while we will continue to make each fire sale moving forward interesting, it is very important for us that players know how many of these items are entering the economy.
## ► TLDR PLEASE???
Sure. To recap:
Low stock divided through multiple colored skin caused the stock to be much lower than usual. Coupled with extreme hype, too many clicks led to way more sales than intended. Nothing will be deleted or wiped, and everyone gets to keep their purchased goods.
We'll be implementing failsafes to prevent selling over the stock limit.
We apologize for the confusion and intend to maintain the value of Fire Sales while ensuring that the buying experience remains enjoyable for our players.

# Changelog/2025/September 19

ANNOUNCEMENT
Taylor's Autumn 2025 Bundle
Updated: September 19, 2025
Hey there!
It's that time again, the changing of seasonal content in Taylor's shop.
This bundle will be available from September 20th at 12pm Noon (EST)
## ► Taylor's Autumn Bundle
Taylor's Autumn 2025 Bundle is this year's introduction of our Autumn Cosmetics bundle. It features 12 new items that bring lovely fall colors to SkyBlock. This bundle will be available starting from September 20th up until December 20th.
Here's a full list of items being added:
Ultimate Rarity (1800 Gems)
- Campfire Power Orb Skin (15 options to choose from, displays the total amount of players fluxed by this orb)
Mythic Rarity (1350 Gems)
- Wolf Pack Werewolf Skin (18 options to choose from)
- Biome Frog Skin (15 options to choose from)
Legendary Rarity (950 Gems)
- Grand Leafing Rune (cherry blossom + buffed effects on foraging islands!)
- Turkey Barn Skin (suspiciously turkey shaped)
Epic Rarity (650 Gems)
- Sugar Glider Bat Skin
- Onyx Goblin Skin
Dyes & Other (375 Gems)
- Marine Dye
- Spooky Dye (with particles!)
- Cauldron Backpack Skin
## ► Availability
Here's the warm usual reminder regarding availability of Taylor's Shop item:
- Starting with the next bundle, Taylor's Winter 2025 Bundle, items from the Winter 2024 bundle will be returning. We will also include new cosmetic items along with the returning ones.
- Items from Taylor's Collection will be available for the foreseeable future. Items within Taylor's Collection may eventually rotate but you can expect them to be made available one way or another.
Thank you for your support over the years and we hope you enjoy these cosmetics.
Have a Spooky Autumn!

# Changelog/2025/September 2
Categories: 2025 Updates, Updates
0.23.4
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.23.4 - Primordial Broodfather Changes, Abiphone Keynote Event, and more!
Updated: September 2, 2025
Hello!
Today we're rolling out update 0.23.4 for Hypixel SkyBlock, which includes improvements to T5 Spider Slayer, the latest Abiphone Keynote Event, improvements to the stats menu, and much more! Keep reading to learn all the details!
## ► Primordial Broodfather Changes
We've made a variety of adjustments in response to community feedback from the Primordial Broodfather update.
### ➥ Boss Changes
- Enabled the Spider Slayer RNG Meter
- Enabled Ananke Feather usage on the Spider Slayer RNG Meter
- Tarantula/Primordial Broodfathers now deal damage in a consistent AOE every 1s
- Added Spider Catalyst to the Tier II loot table (125 weight)
- Increased the weight of the Shriveled Wasp from 10 → 20
- Tarantula Catalysts can now drop upon reaching Spider Slayer 4 (was 5)
### ➥ Item Changes
- Spider Sword/Recluse Fang/Tarantula Fang/Sting damage bonus against Spiders is now multiplicative
- Shriveled Bracelet's ability now activates on all Ж Arthropod mobs instead of just in the Spider's Den
- Mosquito Shortbow Recipe Changes
  - Replaced x128 Enchanted Emerald with x2 Tarantula Catalyst
  - Replaced x64 Enchanted Gold with x1 Digested Mosquito
- Recluse Fang Changes
  - Damage: 120 → 60
  - Crit Damage: 20% → 10%
  - Combat Wisdom: 30 → 0 (against Spiders)
  - Rarity: RARE → UNCOMMON
  - Requirements: Spider Slayer 3 → 2
- Tarantula Fang Changes
  - Damage: 130 → 90
  - Strength: 40 → 45
  - Crit Damage: 30% → 20%
  - Combat Wisdom: 45 → 15 (against Spiders)
  - Rarity: EPIC → RARE
  - Requirements: Spider Slayer 6 → 4
  - Item: Iron Sword → Stone Sword
- Scorpion Foil Changes
  - Adjusted recipe to now require 32x Tarantula Silk, 8x Tarantula Catalyst, and 1x Tarantula Fang
  - Adjusted Dungeonization/upgrade requirements to be the same as Sting's weapon line
  - Added new passive - Deal +250% damage to Spiders and gain +30 Combat Wisdom against Spiders
  - Added "Webcutter" ability
  - Removed "Heartstopper" ability
  - Damage: 80 → 120
  - Strength: 65 → 60
  - Crit Damage: 0% → 30%
  - Vitality: 5 → 0
- Sting Changes
  - Strength: 60 → 75
  - Increased passive damage to Spiders from +250% → +300%
- Wood Singularity Changes
  - Now applied to Axes instead of Wooden Swords
  - Grants +25 Foraging Fortune, can only be applied once
## ► Abiphone Improvements
### ➥ Upcoming Keynote Event
Tune in on September 9th at 1pm EDT for the next Abiphone Keynote Event, live from the Crimson Isle, where Udel will unveil the long-awaited Abiphone Flip+!
### ➥ New Store Opening
A questionable new company, Abiphones & Co., have set up a location in The Hub. This store currently only sells the Abiphone Basic model, which comes with 6 contact slots. Please see store associate Alda for a help on adding new contacts. Udel is currently pursuing legal action against this company for their theft of his original creation.
### ➥ /call
Coming in today's abiOS 441 update is a brand new feature: /call ! This feature is accessible to all customers with an Abiphone in their inventory and an active Cookie Buff. Need to reforge something? Use /call blacksmith! Checking what Dyes are in rotation this year? Use /call vincent! The command works for all NPCs that currently have a contact, so long as you have them added.
### ➥ Other Improvements
Over the past year Udel has been working on additional improvements for his loyal customer base.
- Simplified our product offering menus so you can find what you want faster
- Renovated the store in the Crimson Isle.
- Adjusted the pricing and features of older Abiphone models in response to changes in market demand
- Reached a trade agreement with the Barbarian Faction to unite against Abiphones & Co.. As such, Abiphones will no longer be sold at an upcharged rate from Barbarian vendors.
## ► Stat Categories
We've overhauled the stats menu and sorted all existing stats into different categories: Combat, Mining, Farming, Foraging, Fishing, Miscellaneous, Hunting, and Wisdom Stats. Several items from across the game have been rebalanced to buff specific categories now instead of "all" or "most" stats.
- Item Changes
  - Superior Dragon Armor: Only buffs Combat Stats and Magic Find
    - Designer Note: While we understand Pet Luck not being increased by things such as Superior Dragon Armor theoretically makes Ender Dragon pets rarer, we have listed some weight changes to Dragons further down the thread that should remedy things.
  - Blaze Pet: Only buffs Combat Stats and Miscellaneous Stats on the Crimson Isle
  - Ender Dragon Pet: Only buffs Combat Stats and Magic Find
  - Mithril Golem Pet: Only buffs Combat Stats on Mining Islands
  - Dragon Horn: Only buffs Combat Stats and Magic Find
  - Legion Enchantment: Only buffs Combat Stats and Magic Find
  - Bobbin' Time Enchantment: Only buffs Fishing Stats and Magic Find
    - As a result of it now increasing Treasure Chance and Double Hook Chance, Bobbin' Time's values have been reduced from 0.48%/0.64%/0.8% to 0.3%/0.4%/0.5% per bobber.
  - Giant Fishing Rod: Reduced Double Hook Chance from 20 -> 10
    - Designer Note: We feel like the Double Hook Chance given by the Giant Fishing Rod is excessive, especially now that Bobbin' Time also increases your DHC. It was unfair for a single, incredibly expensive, limited-quantity item to be so integral to fishing progression so we're nerfing it and looking to reintroduce the DHC elsewhere in future fishing content.
  - Mayor Jerry: Only buffs Combat Stats and Miscellaneous Stats
- Base stats on items are now colored based on which category they fall into.
- When you unlock a new stat in SkyBlock a chat message will be sent about it explaining what it does.
- Simplified the item description for the stats menu button to make it less overwhelming.
- True Defense now shows Damage Reduction % in the stat menu similar to regular Defense
## ► Other Changes
### ➥ Item Reclaim System
Over the 6 years since SkyBlock has released certain items have seen changes that make them either unusable or outdated. In the past we would handle reclaims on a case-by-case basis, but our goal now is for all reclaims to happen through the new /reclaim command. If you have an item that has available reclaim there will be a notice for it on the item's description.
To coincide with this change, the following items are no longer dungeonizable:
- The Primordial
- Halberd of the Shredded
- Reaper Scythe
- Reaper Falchion
- Revenant Falchion
- Undead Sword
Master Stars and Dungeonization costs can be reclaimed from these items using /reclaim. As mentioned earlier in the post, Wood Singularities are now applied to Axes instead of Wooden Swords. Any Wood Singularities you have that are on Wooden Swords can also be refunded through this system.
### ➥ Axe Runes
Axe Runes are a new cosmetic type which change the particle trail of Axes when they are thrown. The Fading White and Fading Green Axe Runes can be found rarely in Fig and Mangrove Tree Gifts respectively. In the near future we plan on adding more Axe Runes, stay tuned!
### ➥ Miscellaneous
- Hina's Chapters Changes
  - Added the /chapters command which will allow you to access any Chapters you've unlocked
    - e.g. /chapters moonglade for Hina's Chapters
  - Island Chapters can now be accessed through the Quest Log
  - Improved the item descriptions of the Chapters menu
- Taylor's Shop Changes
  - Players can now use /warp taylor to teleport directly to her shop (available by default)
  - Taylor now advertises in chat to players in The Hub when she releases a new Seasonal Bundle
  - Modernized the item description of SkyBlock Gems
- Vincent Changes
  - Clicking on a Fire Sale Dye in Vincent's menu now teleports players to Elizabeth
  - Global Sales of unlimited Fire Sale Dyes are now shown in Vincent's menu
- Dragon Weight Table Improvements
  - EPIC Ender Dragon Pet: 450 → 449 Weight
  - Aspect of the Dragons: 450 → 447 (except Superior Dragons)
  - Dragon Horn: 452 → 448 (only Superior Dragons)
  - Dragon Claw: 451 → 446
  - Dragon Scale: 295 → 296 (only Young Dragons)
- Mining Changes
  - Added a 'Claim All' button to The Forge
  - /warp forge now makes players face towards Fred the Foreman
  - Uncapped the amount of Suspicious Scrap you can get in a Glacite Minehsaft
    - Note: Each Suspicious Scrap you drop still halves the drop rate for you to get the next
  - Added +5 Mining Fortune to each piece of Glacite Armor
  - Simplified the HOTM item description
- Fishing Changes
  - Moved certain Backwater Bayou items into the Backwater Bayou section and out of the Mob Drops section of the Bazaar
  - Adjusted the Junk Sinker to only function while in the Backwater Bayou
  - Capped Chomp Bonus from the Kuudra Mandible to -20% Health
  - Added an easter egg to Odexar's dialogue if you are holding something specific
- Other Changes
  - Various buttons in the SkyBlock Menu now show what command you can run to quickly access them
  - Crafted Minions button now shows how many unique crafted Minions are in the game
  - Shards now list their Hunting requirement
  - Added Enchanted Charcoal and Match-Sticks to Nether Sack
  - Enchanted Hoppers now use Budget Hoppers in their crafting recipe
  - Increased the rarity of Enchanted Golden Carrots, Baked Potatoes, and Blaze Rods to RARE (from UNCOMMON)
  - Increased the rarity of Giant Cleavers to LEGENDARY (from EPIC)
  - If Mayor Nancy is somehow in office she now has a special skin
- Name Changes
  - Updated the names of various vanilla items in SkyBlock so that they match the names of their latest version counterparts (i.e. Clownfish → Tropical Fish)
  - Renamed the Mini Fish Bowl (Carnival Reward) to 'Aquarium Diorama' to minimize confusion with the accessory line
  - Renamed Anomalous Desire HOTM perk to 'Tunnel Vision'
  - Renamed 'Spooky Hook' in Carnival Shop to 'Spooky Savant' to minimize confusion with actual Hooks
## ► Bug Fixes
- Fixed cocooning deleting boss drops from the T5 Primordial Broodfathers
- Fixed Enderman Pet's 'Enderian' perk not working
- Fixed Kelp/Tall Grass removing Diver Armor's 'Deep Sea Diver' bonus
- Fixed Melody's Hair not being purchasable at Rusty despite having 90% completion
- Fixed Personal Harp not being purchasable from Melody despite having 90% completion
- Fixed Bane of Arthropods VII still being obtainable in the Experimentation Table
- Fixed Fig Armor having inconsistent requirements
- Fixed all Benefactor Bonus time from Starborn Temples being double what it should be
- Fixed Ricochet HOTF perk targeting trees that can't be broken
- Fixed Fig Hew and Figstone Splitter not having mirrored recipe variants
- Fixed Liz's dialogue saying Diaz isn't in office even when she is
- Fixed the Lapis Creeper (L12) Fusion not requiring a Legendary Shard
- Fixed Voidgloom Seraph bosses being able to spawn/get stuck in blocks
- Fixed Spider Artifact having an inconsistent unlock and usage requirement (they are both now Spider Slayer 6)
- Fixed Premium Flesh not using the Undead mob type
- Fixed Lava Flames not having the Infernal mob type despite being Blazes
- Fixed Cleave being able to kill Baby Pandas D:
- Fixed being able to apply Recombobulators to Reforge Stones
- Fixed Galatea missing its tier from the warp menu
- Fixed Bal's Health being displayed incorrectly in the Bestiary
- Fixed Lushlilac Collection recipes missing from the Recipe Book
- Fixed Beach Balls from overriding Shard drops while using Wooden Bait
- Fixed an entire stack of Boosters being consumed when applying them in an Anvil
- Fixed certain mob types not listing what enchantment boosted your damage towards it
- Fixed Huntaxes allowing you to bypass item requirements on the weapon you put in it
- Fixed various minor typos and coloring issues
If you encounter any bugs or unexpected behavior, please report it HERE!
That's all for today!

# Changelog/2026/April 21
Aliases: Changelog/2026/April 27
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[April 21] Fossil Essence Shop, Farming Toolkit, Harvest Feast Changes
Updated: April 27, 2026
27th April Changes
The following changes were rolled to Alpha over the weekend:
- Added all Fossils to the Bazaar
- Added more information on how to use the Farming & Hunting Toolkits
- Added the ability to salvage Glacite Chunks & Fossil the Fish for 25 Fossil Essence
- Added +100 Fossil Essence to Dr Stone's rewards for donating a Fossil
- Increased Fossil Block Fossil Essence yield from 1 -> 4-8
- Increased Fossil Muncher's Fossil Essence reward from 50 -> 100
- Reduced "Sleight of Hand" from 5000 → 2000 Fossil Essence
- Reduced price of the Farming Toolkit
- Reduced "Prehistorian" from 100*tier -> 50*tier Fossil Essence
- Reduced "Resourceful" from 5000*tier -> 2500*tier Fossil Essence
  - These changes mean the Fossil Essence Shop goes from 109,500 -> 66,250 Essence to max
- Adjusted "Cut Loose" effect:
  - Old: Killing a ❆ Glacial mob inside a Glacite Mineshaft will reduce your Cold by 1*tier.
  - New: Killing a ❆ Glacial mob inside a Glacite Mineshaft grants +0.5*tier Cold Resistance. (Stacks up to 10x)
- Fixed not being able to fill glass bottles with water
- Fixed Fossil Essence amount being incorrect for Fossil Excavator fossil tiles
Hello!
Today the Harvest Feast update is back on the Alpha Network with some changes from last time. If you are unfamiliar with the new Harvest Feast event, you can read more about it HERE! In addition to this, we also have some new content hitting the Alpha Network for the first time:
## ► Fossil Essence
Fossil Dust is being revamped into Fossil Essence, and its functionality is being expanded beyond just being able to buy Suspicious Scrap with it.
### ➥ Fossil Essence Shop
The Fossil Essence Shop is accessed through Researcher Beryl, a new NPC that can be found in the top floor of the Fossil Research Center.
Perks:
- Sleight Of Hand I: Start the Fossil Excavator with an additional charge
- Chilled to the Bone I-X: Increases your chances of fishing up Skeleton Fish in the Crimson Isle by 2-20%
- Dwarven Expertise I-X: Grants +2-20 Dwarven Metal Fortune
- Cut Loose I-V: Killing a ❆ Glacial mob while inside a Glacite Mineshaft reduces your Cold by 1-5
- Prehistorian I-X: Increases the chance of a Fossil Dye spawning in the Fossil Excavator by 1-10%
- Resourceful I-V: Grants a 1-5% chance to not consume keys when looting a Frozen Corpse
- Additionally, you are now able to purchase a Chisel through this menu for 5 Fossil Essence. Suspicious Scrap is now purchased here for 100 Fossil Essence as well.
### ➥ Fossil Muncher
Meet the Fossil Muncher, a new NPC found in the Glacite Tunnels. Every 24 hours he'll ask for a random Fossil. Giving him this Fossil will reward you with 25,000 Glacite Powder and 100 Fossil Essence.
### ➥ Other Changes
- The loot table of the Fossil Excavator has been updated
- Citrine Gemstones now prioritize higher value treasures in the Fossil Excavator when highlighting slots
  - Fossil Dye (Highest Priority)
  - Fossil the Fish
  - Paleontologist I Enchantment
  - Fossils
  - Glacite Powder / Essence
  - Gemstones/Glacite/Umber/Tungsten (Lowest Priority)
- Improved the zone entry message for the Glacite Tunnels and the Fossil Research Center
- You can now salvage the following items:
  - Suspicious Scrap: 5 Fossil Essence
  - Caged Wisp: 500 Fossil Essence
  - Frosted Scute: 500 Fossil Essence
  - Shattered Locket: 1,000 Fossil Essence
  - Prehistoric Egg: 25 Fossil Essence
- Halved the Cold Resistance given by the Frozen Skin perk in the Ice Essence Shop
## ► Farming Toolkit
One of the most popular suggestions we've gotten recently is for a farming equivalent to the Hunting Toolkit. It's finally here! You can purchase one for 2,500 Copper from SkyMart and it'll store your Farming Tools for all crops. Consuming the item now grants access to the /farmingtoolkit menu, where you can store your Farming Tools. You can also access this menu by shift right-clicking the Farming Tool you are currently holding, just like the Hunting Toolkit.
## ► Harvest Feast Changes
- Made RARE CROP Chance into a stat named "Overbloom"
  - +1 Overbloom = +1% to RARE CROP drop rates
- Celestial Mason Jar Changes
  - Donation Tiers now reward varying amounts of "Stew Flasks" instead of the Mason Jar directly
  - x4 Stew Flasks are needed to make a Celestial Mason Jar
  - The Leftovers Carnival Perk now rewards +1/+2 Stew Flasks at the final Donation Tier
  - Doubled the duration of the Celestial Mason Jar's effect so that it is in line with other Mixin durations again
  - Designer's Note: We are concerned that there will simply be too many Celestial Mason Jars in the economy at the current rate they were being given out, so we are making Donation Tiers effectively give you a portion of a Celestial Mason Jar instead of a full one. To compensate for this we've re-buffed the duration to 36h, the default for all other Mixins.
- Moondew Changes
  - Now guarantees Lunar Moth spawns in Pest Traps
  - Halved Crystalized Moonlight in crafting recipe
- Revamped "Moonlit" Ultimate Enchantment for Farming Armor
  - Renamed to "Sunset"
  - Now grants +1/2/3/4/5 Overbloom during the day and -1/2/3/4/5% Visitor Cooldown during the night
- Added all of the new Harvest Feast RARE CROPS to the Garden Sack
- Significantly increased the amount of Sack of Sacks slots given via the Clownfish Collection
  - Small: 3→8 Slots
  - Medium: 6→12 Slots
  - Large: 9→16 Slots
  - Greater: 12→20 Slots
  - Giant: 15→24 Slots
  - Massive: 18→28 Slots
- Large Walnuts now have a Reforge Bonus that gives +5% Sowdust
  - Designer's Note: With the changes made to Farming Tools in this update it kind of left Large Walnuts in a spot where they didn't really do anything special anymore. As such, we're giving it a Reforge Bonus that let's you earn a little bit more Sowdust.
- Nerfed MYTHIC Elephant's 'Abundant Harvest' perk to only give +15% Sowdust
  - Designer's Note: We're essentially moving a quarter of the original buff over to the Large Walnut Reforge Stone in order to make it more accessible early on and also to provide more competition between Farming Reforges.
- Thermodynamic Armor Changes
  - Each piece now only gives +5 Attack Speed (down from +20 Attack Speed)
  - Quadrupled the amount of Enchanted Sugar Cane in the recipe
  - Added a Combat Level XXX requirement
  - Designer's Note: We want to maintain the wild fun this armor set brings to the game, but we also don't want it to invalidate existing Combat progression.
- Rather than setting your Speed Cap to 500, Racing Helmets now grant +100 Speed Cap
- Added a Pickup Button to the menu of Buzzybee’s Fantabulous Disco Destinations
- Renamed the "Bonus Attack Speed" stat to just "Attack Speed"
- Fixed RARE CROP Chance not working on Burrowing Spores
- This item's drop rate has been increased to 1/350,000 to help maintain its value
- Fixed having more than 500 Speed not actually making you any faster (this change has been rolled to the main network)
- Fixed Turbo Gourds missing a "Right-click to view recipes!"
- Fixed the cost to upgrade MYTHIC Bee Pets being wrong
- Fixed the Floating Crystals within the Hub Farm not having the correct texture
That's all for today! :hypixel_smile:
## April 27th Changes
The following changes were rolled to Alpha over the weekend:
- Added all Fossils to the Bazaar
- Added more information on how to use the Farming & Hunting Toolkits
- Added the ability to salvage Glacite Chunks & Fossil the Fish for 25 Fossil Essence
- Added +100 Fossil Essence to Dr Stone's rewards for donating a Fossil
- Increased Fossil Block Fossil Essence yield from 1 -> 4-8
- Increased Fossil Muncher's Fossil Essence reward from 50 -> 100
- Reduced "Sleight of Hand" from 5000 → 2000 Fossil Essence
- Reduced price of the Farming Toolkit
- Reduced "Prehistorian" from 100*tier -> 50*tier Fossil Essence
- Reduced "Resourceful" from 5000*tier -> 2500*tier Fossil Essence
  - These changes mean the Fossil Essence Shop goes from 109,500 -> 66,250 Essence to max
- Adjusted "Cut Loose" effect:
  - Old: Killing a ❆ Glacial mob inside a Glacite Mineshaft will reduce your Cold by 1*tier.
  - New: Killing a ❆ Glacial mob inside a Glacite Mineshaft grants +0.5*tier Cold Resistance. (Stacks up to 10x)
- Fixed not being able to fill glass bottles with water
- Fixed Fossil Essence amount being incorrect for Fossil Excavator fossil tiles

# Changelog/2026/April 30
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[April 30] Assorted QoL Changes
Updated: April 30, 2026
Day 2 Changes
The following changes are now live on alpha.hypixel.net:
- Adjusted Hellwisp and Demonlord shards:
  - Demonlord changed from LEGENDARY -> EPIC, replaces Hellwisp’s old ID
  - Hellwisp changed from EPIC -> LEGENDARY, replaced Demonlord’s old ID
    - Designer Note: This is a better solution than our previous changes to the Demonlord shard, and creates less frustrating paths towards fusions that maintain our original intent of keeping people playing Blaze Slayer as intended. In order to prevent players from losing attribute levels as a result of the the rarity adjustment, the attribute levels of these two have been swapped, so if you had Level 10 Demonlord and Level 7 Hellwisp, you should now have Level 10 Hellwisp and Level 7 Demonlord.
- Huntraps placed in the “Matriarch’s Lair” zone will now catch Hellwisp Shards
- As a result of the Hellwisp swap, Inferno Demonlord shards now once again drop 1 at a time instead of 4x
- Reduced the Grinch Sea Creature to 3 HP
- Buffed Magma Lord Armor’s “Lord’s Blessing” bonus:
  - 4/5: Increased to +20* Magic Find on Magmatic mobs
    - *currently says 25 ingame
  - 5/5: Increased to +30 Magic Find on Magmatic mobs
    - Designer Note: These changes have been made to ensure the Magma Lord Necklace is preferable to the Rift Necklace for Magic Find in its intended area of the game. This is a temporary change as we await the Magic Find rework, which is currently in development and will adjust both items.
- The Bingo Pet's “Mountain Climber” perk now applies to Heart of the Forest, and has been renamed to “Climber”.
- Removed the upgrade path of Lava Sea Creature -> Thunder -> Magma Lord.
  - Designer Note: This change raised some larger issues both with starring and with old-system attributes persisting through upgrades and then being salvageable for better rewards. As a result, we will not be going forward with this upgrade path for the time being. Please note that Magma Lord Necklace remains a Necklace but does NOT* build from the Thunder Necklace, rather than being reverted to a Gauntlet.
- Fixed St. Jerry and Hoppity visiting The Garden outside of their respective events
- Fixed the SkyBlock guide showing Harp songs as incomplete despite achieving their 90% requirement
- Fixed being able to plant crops in the Greenhouse that you haven't unlocked yet
- Fixed some sand not being harvestable in the Mushroom Desert
- Fixed the XP gain Autopet rule not triggering when Taming XP is selected and gained.
- Fixed Arachne and Broods being able to exit Arachne's Sanctuary.
Day 1 Changes
The following changes are planned, and will be visible on Alpha soon:
- Increased Magma Lord Necklace stats:
  - Sea Creature Chance: 1.5% -> 2.5%
  - Defense: 35 -> 45
- Increased Personal Sea Creature Cap from 5 -> 10
- Adjusted Inferno Demonlord Shards to drop 1 -> 4 at a time
- Renamed "Maximal Torment" Attribute -> "Unlimited Torment"
- Renamed "Paramount Fortitude" Attribute -> "Unlimited Fortitude"
  - Both Attributes are now buffed by "Almighty" Attribute due to these naming changes
- Removed Helix Fossils from the Fossil Excavator
More changes are currently in the works to target some key issues with Lava Sea Creature/Thunder/Magma Lord Armor starring and the Magma Lord Necklace's lack of Magic Find when compared to the Rift Necklace.
We'd also like to formally state that Ender Pearls being disabled within Kuudra is not intended, can be classified as a bug, and that we are aware of it.
Hi all!
Today we're opening up the Alpha Network with an assortment of quality-of-life changes targeting a wide variety of different areas of the game. Have a quick read before you get to testing!
## ► Combat Changes
- Added a 2x coin multiplier to Crown of Avarice upon reaching a 1B counter
- Increased cooldown of the "Life Recovery" Attribute from 0.2s -> 0.5s
- Moved several items from slayer "extra" tables to main:
  - Festering Maggot
  - Enchanted Blaze Powder (Weight reduced to 1500)
  - High Class Archfiend Dice
- Added a floor-wide drop message for more rare Dungeons items obtained from end-of-floor chests. Also added a more exciting variant for particularly desirable drops, such as the Necron's Handle, which also shows up in the Dungeon Hub
- Adjusted Jerry Boxes to now go straight into the inventory instead of being left on the floor
- Adjusted Soulflow Engine to work with Catalysts
- Adjusted the Horse Pet and Skeleton Horse Pets into skulls instead of ridable mobs. Also gave them new perks
- Adjusted Tormentor:
  - Replaced 2x Sapphire Gemstone Slots -> 2 Combat Gemstone Slots
  - Added +30 Strength
  - Reduced "Angered" extra damage taken from 2x -> 50%
  - Added new ability named "Chain of Agony"
    - Each melee hit arcs with cursed energy, chaining to up to 5 nearby mobs dealing 100% damage (arcs jump from mob to mob, max range of curse jumps are 3 blocks)
    - Cost: 200 Mana
    - Cooldown: 0.5 seconds
    - Togglable
  - Adjusted Burningsoul Shard -> Inferno Demonlord Shard, now only drops from the Inferno Demonlord (250 Weight T2, 500 Weight T3, 1000 Weight T4)
    - Designer Note: This change looks to address complaints that this Shard dropping from Blaze Slayer's "Burningsoul Demon" miniboss was encouraging players to mass-AOE mobs in the Smoldering Tombpeople only to spawn low-tier bosses, kill the minibosses, and then restart the slayer without even attempting the boss.
    - Designer Note #2: For the time being we will make it so you drop 4x Demonlord Shards at a time, as we are aware that some fusions are currently dependent upon this shard.
- Adjusted Huntraps in the Smoldering Tomb to now catch Cinderbat Shards instead of Burningsoul Shards
- Reduced Final Destination Armor's crafting requirement from Enderman Slayer 4 -> 3
## ► Farming Changes
- Added Tal Ker as a Garden Visitor
  - Designer Note: As a result of his excessive ramblings he missed the boat for both the Greenhouse and Grand Feast, but he got there in the end! He is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]]-tier visitor who will ask for Moonflowers.
- Renamed "Garden" section of /pity to "The Garden"
## ► Fishing Changes
- Added the functionality of your current bait being slown in slot #9 while fishing
- Updated the quiver arrow to display the current arrow's stats in its itemlore
- Added the Personal Sea Creature Cap from the Crimson Isle to every island in the game
  - Designer Note: Individual players hoarding large amounts of Sea Creatures on certain islands was causing grievous amounts of lag and preventing others from fishing due to the island-wide Sea Creature cap previously being shared by all players
- Adjusted Lava Fishing Armors to now build into each other (Lava Sea Creature -> Thunder -> Magma Lord)
- Adjusted Magma Lord Gauntlet to now be a Necklace
  - Designer Note: This change allows for the item to build from the Thunder Necklace, inheriting its modifiers, and also now allows players to run 3/4ths Gillsplash + Magma Lord Necklace, which they previously couldn't do as a result of Magma Lord Gauntlet and Gillsplash Gloves both being Gloves.
- Removed all unrelated Shards from Thunder & Lord Jawbus lootpools
## ► Hunting Changes
- Added indicative text in the Attributes menu to show when one Attribute is being buffed by another
- Added a visual indicator of how many more Shards are needed to max out an Attribute in /attributes
- Improved the Attribute level-up message
- Adjusted Mass Syphon button's itemlore to now list all Shards that will be syphoned if pressed
- Renamed "Big Game Hunter" attribute -> "Mythological Tracker"
## ► Mining Changes
- Added the ability to salvage some items for Fossil Essence:
  - Claw Fossil (25 Fossil Essence)
  - Helix Fossil (25)
  - Footprint Fossil (50)
  - Clubbed Fossil (50)
  - Webbed Fossil (50)
  - Ugly Fossil (50)
  - Spine Fossil (50)
  - Tusk Fossil (50)
- Adjusted how Gemstones are displayed on items - there is now a specific "Gemstones:" line in itemlore
- Adjusted "Crystal Serendipity" Attribute to work on all Crystal Hollows Treasure Chests - reduced values from 2-20% -> 1-10% as a result
- Adjusted Jotraeline Greatforge to now ask for an Abiphone Basic as part of her Abiphone Contact quest
- Adjusted Nest Endermites to now consider their spawner their owner for Bestiary/Lootshare purposes
- Removed Claw Fossils and Helix Fossils from the Fossil Excavator
## ► Spider Slayer Changes
- Added the ability for the "Piercing" enchantment to damage multiple Tarantula Broodfather egg sacs at once
- Added a message for when The Primordial / Bloodshot Reforge activates
- Added a prompt in tier 3 telling players to shoot Tarantula Broodfather's egg sacs when the phase begins
- Adjusted the Tarantula Broodfather's "Web of Lies" animation to now be significantly faster
- Adjusted Tarantula Broodfather's "Backstab" in accordance with the mob dealing AOE damage
- Adjusted Cocooned mobs to now belong to the player who cocooned them
- Adjusted Scorpion Bow so that the item's internal ability cooldown is now explicitly stated on the item
- Adjusted Egg Sacs to be affected by Swing Range
- Adjusted Tarantula Broodfather's "Noxious Paralysis" to now reduce your Vitality & Speed by a flat amount
- Reduced HP of Primordial Bats
## ► Other Changes
- Added a feature to Mayor Voting menus that will show upcoming Special Mayors ahead of time
- Added an Area Discovery message for the Mob Types House
- Added Smite 7, Ender Slayer 7, and Bane of Arthropods 7 support to /legacytrades
- Prevented Dyes from appearing back-to-back in Vincent
- Adjusted Century Party Invitation and Fruit Bowl to now be consumed upon completion
- Adjusted nametags of several NPCs to display their job/title above their name instead of below
- Adjusted the color of the Farming for Dummies modifier from DARK GREEN -> LIGHT GREEN
- Adjusted the color of the Foraging Booster modifiers from LIGHT BLUE -> DARK GREEN
- Recategorised some Attributes:
  - Lapis Creeper: Combat -> Mining
  - Thyst: Combat -> Mining
  - Star Sentry: Combat -> Mining
  - Fungloom: Combat -> Mining
  - Spike: Foraging -> Combat
  - Hideoncave: Global -> Mining
  - Silentdepth: Global -> Mining
  - Shellwise: Global -> Combat
- Unsoulbound some items:
  - Pre-digestion Fish
  - Future Calories Talisman
And that's all for today! If you encounter any issues while testing, please report them HERE, ensuring you select alpha.hypixel.net!

# Changelog/2026/August 25/0
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[August 25th] 0.27.1 Release Candidate
Updated: August 25, 2026
Today we have two separate updates on the Alpha Network: the 0.27.1 Release Candidate (which this thread is for) and The Minister Update (which you can find on this thread). We plan on releasing 0.27.1 to the network soon, we don't think it will need that long on the Alpha Network. We want to keep the Minister Update on the Alpha Network longer so that we can polish it to the point where all the Ministers are happy, so it will come out after 0.27.1. Now back to your regularly scheduled patch note reading.
Hello!
Today we have a smaller update on the Alpha Network with Mob Level Improvements and various QOL changes focused on smoothing out progression at various points in the game. There's also some new content for the Chocolate Factory and a handful of bug fixes.
## ► Mob Level Improvements
The Mob Level Improvements mentioned in the 0.27 Release Thread have arrived, and are now ready for testing!
### ➥ What's Changed?
Mob Levels have historically not been very accurate within SkyBlock. For example, both the Zealot Bruiser and the Kalhuiki Tribe Member are currently Level 100, despite the Tribe Member having 15,384× more Health and dealing 320× more damage per hit.
Using a formula that takes into account a mob's Damage and Health, in addition with several contextual factors, such as:
- If the mob is a Boss, Slayer Boss, Miniboss, or Slayer Miniboss
- If the mob is a Sea Creature and, if so, what its rarity
We can now automatically generate not only a mob level for every mob in the game that is far more indicative of its threat to the player, but we can also automatically generate the following rewards in a manner that makes the following rewards received scale with the level of the mob you just defeated:
- Skill XP
- Coins
- XP Orbs
What this should mean for mob-centric skills, such as Combat and Fishing, is that you now gain significantly more XP from mobs that previously gave you far too little, though outliers in either direction (those that gave either too little or too much XP) will have been adjusted.
### ➥ What Should I Test?
For this part of testing, simply play areas of the game where Skill XP is integral, such as:
- Fishing
  - Has early game Fishing XP gain improved?
- Combat
- Slayer
  - How are Slayer spawn times? Faster, slower, the same?
We will be keeping a close eye on feedback to these improvements, so please leave anything you notice in the replies. This content will not necessarily release as part of 0.27.1 if significant further adjustments are needed to prevent disruption to the meta.
## ► QOL Changes
### ➥ Fishing
- Reduced the number of Rusty Coins needed to purchased a Challenging Rod from 16x to 8x
- The Travel Scroll to the Backwater Bayou is now earned in Chapter II for the Backwater Bayou (it is no longer in Junker Joel's shop)
  - More scrolls can be purchased from Rusty for 25,000 Coins
  - Researcher Gilbert now asks for a Busted Belt Buckle instead of a Travel Scroll to the Backwater Bayou
- The Travel Scroll to the Lotus Atoll is now earned in Chapter II for the Lotus Atoll (it is no longer a Trophy Fishing reward)
  - More scrolls can be purchased from Rusty for 50,000 Coins
- Corruption Enchantment changes
  - No longer drops from Corrupted Sea Creatures
  - Corrupted I books are now crafted using 64x Corrupted Fragments and 3x Enchanted Paper
- Rebalanced how Sea Creatures on the Lotus Atoll drop Lotus
  - The Atoll Croaker and Lotus Guardian now only drop 1x and 2x Lotus respectively (nerf)
  - gorF now drops x8 Lotus (buff)
  - The Drowned Captain now drops 32x Lotus with a 20% chance to also drop 1x Silver Lotus (buff)
  - Increased the drop rate of Gold Lotus from Puddle Jumpers from 0.5% to 1%
  - The Frog Prince now always drops 8x Silver Lotus (buff)
  - Increased the drop rate of Gold Lotus from the Frog Prince from 2.5% to 5%
- You now only need to reach Lotus Collection VII for Chapter V in the Lotus Atoll
- Increased the drop rate of Frogcoins from Lotus Guardians from 1% to 5%
### ➥ Combat
- Capped the bonus from the counter on the General's Medallion to 5 digits instead of 6
  - You'll now only need 10,000 Secrets to max it out instead of 100,000
- Reduced the requirements needed for Chili Pepper Collection V-IX
  - You'll now only need 10,000x Chili Peppers to max out this collection
- Increased the drop rate of Flaming Fists from Barbarian Duke X from 0.2% to 0.5%
- The Bingo Combat Talisman now gives +5 Global Wisdom
  - It has also been renamed to the Bingo Wisdom Talisman
### ➥ Other
- Reduced the number of Hemobombs needed to craft the Blood Donor Artifact from 128x to 32x
- Reduced the number of Nearly-Whole Carrots needed to craft Exportable Carrots from 2x to 1x
- Tripled the drop rate of Rock the Fish from Dark Monoliths
- Rescaled the number of logs needed to max out the Absorb enchantment
  - You'll now only need 25,000,000 Logs to max it out instead of 50,000,000
- Added Arachne's Calling, Arachne Fragments, and Soul String to the Combat Sack
- Reworked the Pig Brain Mixin to now just give a flat 10% chance to receive an extra item from Shiny Pigs
## ► Chocolate Factory Improvements
### ➥ Rabbit Factions
Starting at Chocolate Factory V you'll unlock the Faction Tower, where you're able to select one of four Factions: the City Rabbits, the Mountain Rabbits, the Country Rabbits, and the Beach Rabbits. Your can change which Faction you are aligned with for 1B Chocolate.
Being part of a Faction means you can find new Rabbits that are exclusive to that Faction. Finding these Rabbits are special, as they don't get automatically added to your collection. Instead, they'll drop as an item that you can trade with other players or buy/sell on the Bazaar. Right-clicking on them consumes the item and adds them to your collection permanently.
Each Faction has 4 levels of dedication that you can level up using Chocolate, the higher the level, the higher the rarity of Faction Rabbits you can potentially find! Each Faction has 5 COMMON, 4 UNCOMMON, 3 RARE, 2 EPIC, and 1 LEGENDARY Chocolate Rabbit to find. Note that Faction Rabbits are twice as rare as Chocolate Rabbits of the same rarity.
### ➥ Chocobits
Starting at Chocolate Factory VI you'll unlock the Chocobit Tower, which allows you to find SPECIAL Chocobit Rabbits that no one else in SkyBlock can have at the same time! There are 33,333 Chocobits total and they drop twice as frequently as MYTHIC Rabbits, and they each add 0.2x to the multiplier of your Chocolate Factory! Note that Chocobits are not stored in your normal Chocolate Rabbit Collection and they do not take up space.
...but there's a catch! You must pay 1M Coins each SkyBlock Year per Chocobit you own, or else it'll be released back into the pool for anyone to potentially get! When a Chocobit gets released, they'll give you two SkyBlock Years worth of Chocolate production that they would've made otherwise as a thanks!
If you already have every DIVINE Chocolate Rabbit, the pity for it will be replaced with pity for Chocobits.
### ➥ Other
- Doubled the drop rates of MYTHIC and DIVINE Rabbits
  - With this change their Pity amounts have been reduced to 250 and 1,250 respectively
- Added a new Chocobun Shard that can be fused into with Bunbun and any Crime Family shard
  - Adds +1 Level Cap to Rabbit Bro, Cousin, and Sis
- You can now buy 3 Dark Cacao Truffles per SkyBlock Year instead of only 2
- Buffed the Rabbit Crew Attribute from 2 ➜ 20 to 5 ➜ 50 Chocolate per second
## ► Other Changes
- Added a DPS Meter above Training Dummies
- Improved the responsiveness of F7 Terminals for players with high ping ("pingless terminals")
- Arrow cost changes
  - Reinforced Iron Arrow: 24x Iron Ingot ➜ 1x Enchanted Iron Ingot
  - Gold-Tipped Arrow: 40x Gold Ingot ➜ 1x Enchanted Gold Ingot
  - Redstone-Tipped Arrow: 24x Block of Redstone ➜ 1x Enchanted Redstone Dust
  - Emerald-Tipped Arrow: 16x Block of Emerald ➜ 1x Enchanted Emerald
  - Bouncy Arrow: 24x Slime Block ➜ 2x Enchanted Slimeball
  - Icy Arrow: 64x Packed Ice ➜ 2x Enchanted Ice
  - Explosive Arrow: 24x Block of Coal ➜ 2x Enchanted Gunpowder
  - Glue Arrow: x64 Tarantula Web ➜ 2x Enchanted String
  - Regular Arrows are now cheaper from the Weaponsmith and Ophelia, only costing 5 Coins per
  - Increased the amounts given for each Arrow in their more trading options menu by x5
- Reduced the drop rate of Ananke Feathers by 25% from Kuudra
- Added the Greenhouse to the Enchanted Clock
- The Coin cost for removing Rod Parts now scales with the Rod Part's rarity
  - COMMON: 10,000 Coins
  - UNCOMMON: 25,000 Coins
  - RARE: 50,000 Coins
  - EPIC: 100,000 Coins
  - LEGENDARY: 250,000 Coins
  - MYTHIC: 500,000 Coins
  - DIVINE: 1,000,000 Coins
  - Along with this change the rarities of many Rod Parts have been changed
  - You can also no longer Recombobulate Rod Parts as a result of this change
- Added text to the item lore of Flexbones explaining where to obtain them
- Mango Dye is now dropped from Tree Gifts
  - 1/1M from Fig Tree Gifts
  - 1/500k from Mangrove Tree Gifts
  - 1/100k from Helix Tree Gifts
- Added 10 Crit Damage to the base stat of Manticore Claws
- The Bestiary entry for Shiny Pigs now displays Year of the Pig loot
  - We also added Enchanted Cooked Porkchops as a rare drop from them (2500 weight)
- Improved shift-clicking items out of the Crafting Table
- Improved shift-clicking items out of the Brewing Stand
## ► Bug Fixes
- Fixed Lumber Jack, Kelly, Ryan, Melody, and Molebert all accepting stacks of logs for their quest only if you had the exact amount in your inventory
- Fixed a way to bypass the Inferno Slayer boss's Magic Damage reduction
- Fixed the Tormentor ability using the damage of the original hit instead of recalculating for each chained mob
- Fixed the Brewing Stand not allowing item stacks to be dragged within the inventory
- Fixed a typo in the Burning Vengeance ability's description
- Fixed Aspect of the Dragon working on the Conjoined Brood
- Fixed Ice Spray working on the Conjoined Brood
- Fixed the Knockback enchantment working on the Conjoined Brood
- Fixed being able to damage the Conjoined Brood without first unlocking Tier 5 Spider Slayer
- Fixed Tier 5 Spider Slayer boss loot not being properly loot shared
- Fixed Duplex Arrows not correctly damaging the Spider Boss's egg sacs
- Fixed Pumpkin Minions overproducing while offline
- Fixed waterlogged blocks disappearing in the lobby when filling a bucket
- Fixed players falling through farmland when jumping on it
Feel free to leave any feedback on this update in the comments below. If you have feedback in regards to the Minister Update, please leave it on its respective thread instead.
If you encounter any bugs or unexpected behavior, please report it HERE! That's all for today! :hypixel_smile:

# Changelog/2026/August 25/1
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[August 25th] The Minister Update Alpha Testing
Updated: August 25, 2026
Today we have two separate updates on the Alpha Network: the Minister Update (which this thread is for) and the 0.27.1 Release Candidate (which you can find on this thread). We plan on releasing 0.27.1 to the network relatively soon - we don't think it will need that long on the Alpha Network. However, we wish to keep the Minister Update on the Alpha Network longer so that we can polish it to the point where all the Ministers are happy, so it will come out after 0.27.1. Now, back to your regularly scheduled patch note reading:
Hello again!
Today on the Alpha Network we have the long-awaited Minister Update. Since Aura ended, we have worked on the perks outlined by Ministers as part of their election campaigns, trying to preserve proposed perks as much as feasible while ensuring they would impact players in the most positive yet unintrusive way possible.
This alpha thread will get a little more technical than usual - altered perks will be suffixed with a "Designer Note" that will outline the vision, context, and process surrounding each perk and the alterations made. So, with that out of the way, we'd like to present to you, in chronological order of election, the results of the Minister Elections! Please note that not all perks are fully complete yet and that the perks we are presenting today are subject to change.
With that said, let's begin!
## ► Precursor Drone (thirtyvirus)
The Precursor Drone has received some changes! Now being base Legendary, players may collect pet items known as "drone parts" from various areas of the game and attach them to the Precursor Drone in order to grant it new base stats and perks. There are currently 3 drone mods but, who knows, more might be added with time:
- Drone Mod: Grungle -> Forest Essence Shop, 2,500x Forest Essence
- Drone Mod: Contraband -> Crafted using various Junk items sourced from the Backwater Bayou
- Drone Mod: Mining off Camera -> Diamond Essence Shop, 2,500x Diamond Essence
Designer Note: The Precursor Drone didn't release in an ideal state so we took some time to revisit it, looking to both better deliver on the customizable aspect of it and find a futureproof design that would allow for the pet to be further adjusted in the future. We saw a great suggestion on the forums (you can expect a Creative Mind in your Jerry after release!) that introduced "Drone Mods" - items that could be applied directly to the Drone to grant it base stats and perks, allowing for players to swap perks in and out depending upon their activity.
## ► Blood God Sigil & Potato Ring (Technodad)
Technodad's perk started a Year of the Pig event while he was in office - along with buffs to Potato Minions, and introduced two new upgrades to the Potato Talisman and Blood God Talisman. Players can craft both these upgrades - which have been gracefully bestowed upon SkyBlock by the Great Potato King himself - from items gathered from Shiny Pigs.
Designer Note: This Minister Perk was already in the game and hasn't been adjusted as part of this release.
## ► Kuudra's Kidney, Lung, and Heart (Future77)
Crafted from Kuudra Tentacles, which can be purchased from paid chests after defeating Kuudra, the Kuudra's Kidney, Lung, and Heart accessory line grants some useful quality-of-life features for those looking to take on the lava kraken.
Designer Note: This Minister Perk was already in the game and hasn't been adjusted as part of this release.
## ► Mining Fiesta Rework (zj2)
When Mayor Cole is elected with his Mining Fiesta perk, said Fiesta will now span his entire mayorship! Though the buffs have been toned down a bit as a result, you can purchase a few Fiesta Flasks (as well as a brand-new Attribute) from Brynmor that stack additively with the base fortune buff from the fiesta, allowing you to either consume them when you're sure you have the time in order to maximize your profits, or store them up to go all-out during a future Fiesta.
Designer Note: Fiestas were previously extremely strong but balanced out by the fact that most players could never attend all of them. However, with fiestas actually being quite important to progression, this has become very frustrating for players who struggle to attend. As a result, we thought about the Minister's proposal and decided to weaken fiestas a bit, making them +25% Mining Fortune, but to allow them to span the entire duration of Cole's mayorship. We will see how this pans out and know that some players who attended entire Fiestas may be upset at their weakening, but the main goal here is for more players to be able to participate in fiestas and for less players to end up progression locked by them.
## ► Hotspot Talisman, Ring, and Artifact (Derailious)
Located in the Clay Collection (Clay being the token drop of Water Hotspot Sea Creatures), the Hotspot Talisman line grants you even more useful buffs when fishing in a Hotspot, and is crafted out of materials gathered from various Hotspot Sea Creatures and junk found in the Bayou.
Designer Note: This Minister originally wanted a Tracking Mixin to help out new players, though this was delivered as part of the Harvest Feast update. As such, it was agreed upon that a new talisman line, which ended up being located in the Clay Collection. would be useful for early/mid-game fishers who spend a lot of their time fishing in Hotspots, regardless of whether they're treasure fishing or sea creature fishing.
## ► Dungeons Terminal Changes (fear5s)
Some particularly dastardly Dungeons Terminals have been made a little easier/less time-consuming thanks to the work of this generous Minister, who has dropped the amount of inputs needed to complete each of them:
- Reduced the amount of rows on the "Melody Terminal" from 4 -> 3
- Reduced the amount of numbers on the "Numbers Terminal" from 14 -> 10
## ► Bestiary Accessory Line (Intrests)
The Bestiary Talisman can be claimed from Grandma Wolf upon reaching Bestiary Milstone 50. The accessory evolves as you progress through the Bestiary, increasing its stats and upgrading its rarity:
- Milestone 100 ->
  - Increases to UNCOMMON
  - Stats: Strength: +2, Intelligence: +1
- Milestone 150 ->
  - Increases to RARE
  - Stats: Strength: +3, Intelligence: +2, Crit Damage: +1
- Milestone 200 ->
  - Increases to EPIC
  - Stats: Strength: +4, Intelligence: +3, Crit Damage: +2, Ability Damage: +1
- Milestone 250 ->
  - Increases to LEGENDARY
  - Stats: Strength: +5, Intelligence: +4, Crit Damage: +3, Ability Damage: +2, Tracking: +1
- Milestone 300 ->
  - Increases to MYTHIC
  - Stats: Strength: +6, Intelligence: +5, Crit Damage: +4, Ability Damage: +3, Tracking: +2, Magic Find: +1
Designer Note: Though we have capped its rarity at Mythic due to not wanting to add Divine accessories to the game just yet, we were on board with the Minister's vision to have a strong accessory that scales with your Bestiary progress. Giving respectable amounts of some of the strongest combat stats in the game, we hope this is an adequate reward for getting so deep into your milestones.
## ► Hunt (Dra2oon)
[REDACTED]
## ► Eagle Pet (Kuvdra)
The Eagle Pet is a brand-new pet that helps players who are hunting mobs in Galatea. It's obtained from the Lushlilac Collection and helps with Combat Hunts in particular, though it also grants general Hunting Wisdom while on Foraging Islands.
Designer Note: Admittedly, this has been one of the more difficult perks to make work. The proposed design was perhaps a bit too strong - granting Hunting Fortune for each Eagle Pet on the island and having a chance to double the Shards output from fusions but, as a result of our attempts to prevent simply adding a pet that would be used to Hunting Fortune swap once Magic Find swaps are removed, the Eagle struggles to find a particularly strong niche.
However, since we initially started work on this pet the Critter Safari has released, so, if the Minister so desires, we could perhaps look to move this pet into the Safari Essence Shop and try to make it a strong Safari option (higher chance of Sparkling Critters, More loot from Floor Drops, unconditional Hunting Wisdom, etc.).
## ► Dungeons Yapping Nerfs (Celestial_Milk)
All Bosses from Dungeon floors 1-6 now spend significantly less time talking, and cutscenes have also been sped up to remove the prolonged periods that players spend standing around waiting for the next phase of the fight to begin. The Watcher also now has less dialogue, talks faster, and spawns his summons at a faster rate than before if you have beat the floor at least five times.
Designer Note: Though the Minister initially proposed a consumable item that would speed up dialogue for the player, we decided to just make this a global QoL change for everyone to enjoy, and to speed up animations/waiting time as well as dialogue.
## ► Capybara Sanctuary (CandyPat)
Designer Note: We're still finishing up cooking up this perk and you can expect to see it on Alpha soon. Though the Minister eventually suggested a Capybara Pet, we struggled to find an iteration of its proposed perks, which did things such as having a chance to double Dye drops - that felt good for the player. Imagine you drop a Dye but don't have your Capybara Pet equipped - suddenly you feel a twinge of regret rather than excitement over having dropped a dye - sort of similarly to how the Blessing enchantment used to interact with Aquamarine Dye. Our current concept is for the Moonglade Marsh to house a Capybara sanctuary, where you can feed Capybaras to power them up and eventually receive the Capybara's Blessing, which would grant you some strong foraging buffs for a while (timber, fortune, wisdom, etc.).
## ► Refractive Paint Palette (Refraction)
The Refractive Paint Palette is a new item that, like Necron's Handle and Diana's Bookshelf, can be purchased from the Regalia Room for 75m coins. It tracks unique Dyes you've obtained with the item in your inventory and, upon completion, gives you some nice stats to boot! The palette even starts off blank and gains a new color each time you obtain a new Dye!
Designer Note: This Minister sought to add a new item similar in function to Necron's Ladder/Diana's Bookshelf that awarded Magic Find and Combat Wisdom upon being maxed out. While the original proposal was for it to track Crazy Rare Drops from Slayers, we discussed with the Minister and ended up settling on Dyes, as they're a more unified system that's more intuitive for the player to track.
## ► Powder of the People (Alaawii)
This Minister sought to add the Powder of the People accessory line to the game, obtainable through the Dwarven Forge.
- Powder of One -> Increases Mithril Powder gain by +2.5%
  - HOTM Requirement: 2
  - Material Requirement: 1x Refined Mithril, 1x Refined Titanium
  - Forging Process: 1 Hour
- Powder of The Few -> Increases Mithril Powder and Gemstone Powder gain by +5%
  - HOTM Requirement: 5
  - Material Requirement: 1x Powder of One, 4x Gemstone Mixture, 32x Refined Mineral
  - Forging Process: 1 Hour
- Powder of The Many -> Increases Mithril Powder, Gemstone Powder, and Glacite Powder gain by +7.5%
  - HOTM Requirement: 8
  - Material Requirement: 1x Powder of the Few, 4x Glacite Amalgamation, 32x Glossy Gemstone
  - Forging Process: 6 Hours
- Powder of the People -> Increases Mithril Powder, Gemstone Powder, and Glacite Powder gain by +10%
  - Obtained via combining Powder of the People with Divan's Powder Coating in an Anvil
Designer Note: While the original concept for this accessory also buffed Sowdust, Essence, and Whispers, that was a little on the strong side. However, we were able to work with the Minister to reach the compromise of an accessory that boosted all three types of Powder gain while also maintaining the defining behavior of the accessory being able to be upgraded to its final tier through the use of Divan's Powder Coating, which was quite a unique concept. We also decided to use Refined Minerals & Glossy Gemstones in the Forge recipe due to them becoming easier and more reliable to obtain as we move to Yearlong Mining Fiestas.
## ► Scatha in a Bottle (Toadstar0)
Craft yourself a Nothing-in-a-bottle upon reaching Living Metal Heart 3! What does it do, you may ask? Nothing! However, if you take the bottle out of the Rift and into the overworld, you may feel a strange compulsion to use it to capture a Scatha. Upon doing so, you'll find yourself with a brand-new Mixin that grants +0.1 Pristine!
Designer Note: While the original concept for this perk was to add a Mythic Scatha upgrade, the proposed perk was to prevent drills from consuming fuel. Not only do we already have concrete plans to add a Mythic Scatha upgrade in the future, but drill fuel is an important aspect of mining that we don't feel comfortable outright invalidating. However, it is important to question why such a perk would be proposed - mining can be difficult for higher-ping players, and server-side mining ticks + drills constantly updating their itemlore have been known to cause issues, which is what this minister sought to address. We have some changes coming in the future to help players in some ping-intensive areas of the game, such as with the Dojo, Tiny Dancer, and Vampire Slayer, so we will have a look at what we can do to make mining more friendly to players with less stable/higher ping connections.
## ► Returning Player Buffs (Endthr)
Designer Note: We're just finishing cooking up this perk and you can expect to see it on Alpha soon. The long and short of it is that, once a player has been offline from SkyBlock for a month or more, when they next log in they'll be visited by a new NPC on their Private Island. The player can then claim limited-time buffs from said NPC - buffs that give coins, increase Skill XP gain, etc., to help them get back into the game a little faster.
## ► Secret Bingo #2 (arithenmonkey)
This Minister desired a second Secret Bingo event, which we decided to release in April 2026 to coincide with April Fools. We were very happy with how it went - goals were discovered at a good pace, participation was high, and it seemed like players were having an all-round good time. You can expect another Secret Bingo in the future, though we don't have a concrete timeline for it just yet.
## ► HOTF/HOTM Loadouts (Luamon)
This Minister wanted to add Loadouts that allowed you to swap around your HOTM/HOTF trees freely instead of having to constantly reset and rebuild them. The official "Loadouts Update" was added to the game a little over a month ago as part of Hypixel SkyBlock 0.26, and contained both the desired loadouts.
## ► Folf Shard & Dungeons Improvements (F22_Raptor)
- Added the Folf Shard (RARE):
  - Description: Increases your Speed Cap by +5-50 while in The Catacombs.
  - Obtainment: Fuse Soul of the Alpha + Epic or higher
- Increased the pickup range of Wither Keys and Blood Keys in the Catacombs by 5 blocks
- Increased the activation range of Fels by 5 blocks.
Designer Note: We liked the initial concept this Minister had, of adding a new Attribute to help players speed through Dungeons. Though the Minister later suggested adding a passive pet, now that we have attributes we are trying to move away from passive pets, and would like to adjust all existing ones in the future. Because of this, we simply added two of their suggested passive perks as innate features for everyone to enjoy.
## ► Dark Auction Changes (lejimbo)
It has been made easier to obtain the Seal of the Family accessory line:
- Reduced Dark Auction purchases required to obtain the Shady Ring from 5 -> 1
- Reduced Dark Auction purchases required to obtain the Crooked Artifact from 10 -> 3
- Reduced Dark Auction purchases required to obtain the Seal of the Family from 15 -> 7
The following changes have also been made to the Dark Auction pool:
- Added Prosecute VI
- Added Execute VI
- Removed Sharpness VI
- Removed Protection VI
Designer Note: We also made some server size improvements to the Dark Auction back in January as part of this perk.
## ► Infinipot (rembutquaglet)
The Infinipot is a new accessory, purchasable for 5,000 Wither Essence, that will automatically consume dungeon potions from your inventory when a new Dungeons run begins. If the player does not have a Dungeon Potion, the accessory will automatically purchase their desired tier of potion using coins from their purse or bank.
Designer Note: We believe this iteration to be identical to the Minister's vision, introducing some useful QoL that synergizes with Jellyfish Pet petrules. If this isn't the case and the accessory is triggering at the wrong time, please let us know.
## ► Frog's Spirit (KeepAura)
Purchased from the Forest Essence Shop for 5,000 Forest Essence, the Frog's Spirit is an Ability Scroll that can be used to apply the "Leapfrog" ability to Fig and Helix Boots. Once they have the ability scroll applied, players will be able to double jump while Foraging!
Additionally, we have added permanent Jump Boost IV to the Frog Pet's "Hop" perk.
Designer Note: Though this Minister initially wanted some new pieces of Foraging equipment that granted a Double Jump effect, we had already decided upon our final upgrade lines for Foraging (David's Cloak, Honeycomb Necklace, Veilshroom Bracelet, Safari Belt), so there wasn't much room for an additional piece, especially if it wanted to be BiS. However, we did really like the idea of it allowing the player to Double Jump, so we transformed the equipment piece into an ability scroll instead. Since no other pieces of Foraging armor/equipment have abilities right now, perhaps there is room for more scrolls in the future.
## ► More Pity (MashClash)
The following pity meters have been added to this game as a result of this Minister's efforts:
- Special Zealots
- Vanquishers
/pity also no longer requires a Booster Cookie to use.
Designer Note: We didn't add the initially proposed Shattered Locket pity due to the Frozen Corpse RNG Meter releasing shortly after the campaign. As for the Phoenix Pet, we're not sure where the Phoenix pet's place in the game should be, but don't want to add it to a pity in its current state of obtainment.
## ► End Aura (End_Aura)
True to their word, this Minister decided that the next election would be Aura's last. What a power move!
## ► Weather (ALAND_)
The following islands within SkyBlock may now experience periodic bouts of weather!
- Backwater Bayou
- Crimson Isle
- Crystal Hollows
- Dwarven Mines
- Glacite Tunnels
- Jerry Island
- Lotus Atoll
- Moonglade Marsh
- Spider's Den
- The End
- The Garden
Mild weather occurs every 20 minutes, and grants thematic stats to all players on the island. Every 3rd instance of weather will instead of EXTREME, granting more stats alongside an exclusive bonus.
To see a list of all the types of Weather and their accompanying buffs, you can climb the mountain in The Hub and speak to Professor Wynd who, due to a recent crash landing, has taken up residence inside what was previously Scoop's house. He's been studying these strange bouts of weather and would be glad to show you his research.
Designer Note: Though this Minister initially campaigned to add a "Quest Master" of sorts to The Hub, we already have some plans for improving the opening stages of the game and were slightly worried that a Quest Master could distract players from a future "tutorial" of sorts. However, we noticed that the Minister's backup perk - weather - was very fleshed out, and the concept really appealed to us. We thus worked with the Minister to add weather to several islands of the game in accordance with their vision.
## ► Parts of Aura Will Stay (Aura_STAY)
Designer Note: Though this Minister was unable to actually ensure that Aura would stay thanks to a previous Minister using their perk to ensure this would be Aura's last election, we saw how much players enjoyed the sped-up progression that Aura offered throughout her mayorship and, as you may have noticed, have recently been trying to reduce some of the game's longer grinds:
- Bestiaries were reduced across the board as part of the Torrhus Canyon & Critter Safari update
- The Magic Find rework is on the Alpha horizon, and will particularly help newer players progress faster
## ► Dungeons Tick Changes (FlameOfWar)
Even more Dungeon changes come courtesy of this Minister, who has made changes to secrets that will lead to faster clears on all floors as well as some more specific changes to the "Simon Says" terminal:
- Reduced the amount of time it takes to pick up a secret from 11 -> 5 ticks
- Removed one set of lights from the "Simon Says" terminal in F7/M7
## ► Other Changes
- Improved itemlore of Refined Minerals
- Improved itemlore of Glossy Gemstones
- Improved itemlore of Magical Milk Bucket
- Adjusted Spinosaurus Pet's "Primordial Fisher" perk, and renamed it to "Adaptive Fisher":
  - Old: Increases this pet’s base stats by x% during rain.
  - New: Increases this pet’s base stats by x% during Weather.
- Adjusted Quartzfang Shard:
  - Old: Mining Speed from Events is increased by +10-100%.
  - New: Grants +10-100 Mining Speed on Mining Islands while Weather is active
- Adjusted category of Implosion, Shadow Warp, Wither Shield from DUNGEON ITEM -> ABILITY SCROLL
- Adjusted the location of recipes in the Clay Collection to make room for the Hotspot accessory line
- Adjusted the Blizzard in a Bottle to now increase the effects of weather on players by 10%
- Adjusted Fishing Speed stat scaling to be more linear
  - Designer Note: These changes look to make Fishing Speed gains more noticeable for early-game fishers instead of ramping up more noticeably as you approach the cap. Nobody at any game stage gets weaker here.
- Adjusted Creative Minds:
  - New Creative Minds are Soulbound
  - Itemlore can now be customized by Staff to input a reason for awarding
  - Going forward, itemlore for new Creative Minds will state they were awarded by "The Hypixel Team"
    - Designer Note: The goal here is for us not to effectively be dumping several billion coins on a player when awarding them a Creative Mind. Like the original Wiki items, CM's are intended to be a commemoration of an achievement, not a marketable asset.
Feel free to leave any feedback on this update in the comments below. We will especially be keeping an eye out for feedback from Ministers themselves. If you have feedback in regards to the 0.27.1 Release Candidate, please leave it on its respective thread instead.
If you encounter any bugs or unexpected behavior, please report it HERE!

# Changelog/2026/August 3
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[Aug 3] 0.27 Alpha Changes 2
Updated: August 3, 2026
Hello! We have one more round of fixes and fusion changes, as follows:
### ➥ Bug Fixes
- Fixed claiming a Safari Belt from the Safari Manager not completing the Chapter task.
- Fixed a few Shards having the same ID numbers.
- Changed Puck from Animal to an Airborne mob type.
- Fixed the Logger perk not showing up passed Foraging Level 54.
- Changed the Torrhus Springs Travel Scroll recipe to use Ruby Veilshrooms.
### ➥ Farming Fortune Changes
- Buffed Cropshot Chip from +1/+2/+3 Farming Fortune per chip level → +3/+4/+5
- Buffed Harvesting enchantment from +5/10/15/20/25/30/35 → +12.5/25/37.5/50/62.5/75
- Increased Pesthunter Philip’s Potion effect from +2.5 Farming Fortune per pest (capped at 200) to +5 Farming Fortune per pest (capped at 200)
That's all for today! Please remember to report any bugs you encounter while testing HERE, being sure to select "alpha.hypixel.net". Thank you!

# Changelog/2026/February 18
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[February 18] SkyBlock 0.24.2 Release Candidate
Updated: February 18, 2026
Hello!
Today on the Alpha Network we have a large update containing the following:
- Hub Revamp & Museum Restructure
- The Year of the Witch
- Frozen Corpse RNG Meter & Frozen Corpse Milestones
- Various QOL Changes
- Stranded EOL
- Bugfixes
Since this is a pretty large update, we've sectioned the Alpha Thread into various sections for your viewing pleasure.
Please note that the minimum version for joining this Alpha is Minecraft 1.21.9.
### ► Hub Revamp & Museum Restructure​
Also part of this release candidate are the Hub Revamp and Museum Restructure from the January 30th Alpha Session. For more information on those, take a look at the linked thread.
### ► The Year of the Witch​
The Year of the Witch is the third event of its type, following on from the Year of the Pig and Year of the Seal. During this year, talk to Alcina in The Hub to view the Witch's Stew. During this year, mobs throughout SkyBlock will drop various ingredients, the type of which depends upon their Mob Type.
Mobs of multiple types are eligible to drop multiple ingredients, with the tasty list including, but not being limited to...
- Waterborne Lice
- Dirty Fingernails
- Void Ooze
- Festering Flies
- Funny Bones
- Frogs Legs
You can use these delightful ingredients to craft various stews that will reward you with permanent Magic Find buffs when consumed, or to purchase useful items - and even a pet - from the Year of the Witch Shop.
As you may notice, two items that can be purchased from the Year of the Witch Shop are mixins to help you out during the Year of the Pig and Year of the Witch. The long-term goal for this year is for it to be something that helps improve your performance in other "Year of the" events in a manner not all too dissimilar from how Alchemy as a whole is used to improve your stats/efficiency in other areas of the game, so expect more mixins to be added to the YOTW Shop as more "Year of the" events are added.
92,80,-85 bread bowl??
### ► Frozen Corpse RNG Meter & Corpse Milestones​
The Frozen Corpse RNG Meter has been added to the game, making it easier for particularly unlucky looters to get their hands on particularly desirable items, such as the Shattered Locket. Not only this, but Grandpa Wolf has also been spotted loitering around the upper levels of the Glacite Tunnels, plotting some shady scheme.
#### ➥ Frozen Corpse RNG Meter​
A Frozen Corpse RNG Meter has been added to the game, accessible through /rng or by talking to Grandpa Wolf in the Glacite Tunnels. The meter is 10% retroactive and can be progressed by opening Frozen Corpses (higher tiers grant more Meter XP) or through the use of Ananke Feathers.
As part of these changes, the Frozen Corpse tables have been rewritten with the intent of making higher-tier corpses more profitable overall (though variance is still intended). Open the spoiler below to take a look!
