#### ➥ Frozen Corpse Milestones​
Grandpa Wolf can be found in the top-left section of the Glacite Tunnels. If you can loot enough Frozen Corpses, he will reward you for your time in the form of several useful perks and a new talisman line. All Frozen Corpses Milestones are retroactive, meaning you won't have to get frostbite once again if you've already put the work in.
#### ➥ Other Mining Changes​
- Added the ability to salvage the Frosted Scute for 100 Ice Essence
- Added a 5,000,000 NPC sell price to the Frosted Scute and Shattered Locket
- Added a Worm/Scatha Tablist Widget to the Crystal Hollows.
- Added +2.5 Gemstone Spread to the Pendant of Divan's "Legacy" Tiered Bonus.
- Adjusted Mining Fiestas start times, spreading them more evenly throughout the SkyBlock Year.
  - Fiestas now occur throughout the 2nd, 4th, 6th, 8th, and 10th months of the year.
    - [!] Designer Note: This is not a Minister Perk - those are still to come.
- Adjusted Mort to now ask for a Spirit Bone instead of a Spirit Mask as part of his Abiphone Contact quest.
- Improved clarity of the Gifts from the Departed HOTM Perk's itemlore.
- Adjusted Dead Man's Chest HOTM Perk from +1-50% to +0.5-25%.
- Added a line of "Dwarven Lantern" mining deployables:
  - The Dwarven Lantern can be purchased from Lumina.
  - The Dwarven Lantern can be upgraded to the Mithril Lantern in The Forge at HOTM 2.
  - The Mithril Lantern can be upgraded to the Titanium Lantern in The Forge at HOTM 5.
  - The Titanium Lantern can be upgraded to the Glacite Lantern in The Forge at HOTM 8.
  - The Glacite Lantern can be upgraded to the Will-o-wisp in The Forge at HOTM 10.
    - Crafting the Will-o-wisp requires a Caged Wisp, which is a new drop from Tungsten/Umber and Vanguard Frozen Corpses.
### ► Various QOL Changes​
Today we have a number of high-impact quality-of-life changes to several areas of the game.
#### ➥ Combat​
- Adjusted the unlock order of the Kuudra Pet's perks. The new order can be found below:
  - COMMON -> Wither Bait
  - UNCOMMON -> Trophy Bait
  - RARE -> Crimson
  - EPIC -> Kuudra Fortune
  - LEGENDARY -> Kuudra Specialist
    - [!] Designer Note: WA key goal here is for the "Trophy Bait" perk to be more accessible, since +20 Trophy Chance is a massive amount to be locked behind the Epic tier of the pet.
- Adjusted the rarity of the Wolf Talisman from COMMON -> UNCOMMON.
- Adjusted the purchase requirements for the Blood God Crest:
  - Can now be purchased infinitely instead of once per profile.
  - Now capped at +7 Strength.
#### ➥ Dungeoneering​
- Added the Spirit Mask to the Master Mode Floor 4 drop table and removed it from the Dark Auction.
  - Can only drop from the Obsidian Chest, costs 15m to open.
  - Has 4x less weight than a Spirit Sword.
- Added a chat message that displays whenever you level up a Boss Collection.
- Adjusted Golden Heads and Diamond Heads to now be purchased from Ophelia upon being unlocked.
  - [!] Designer Note: With this change, Diamond Heads now carry over upgrades from Golden Heads.
- Added 250 Gold Essence to Boss Collection tiers where Golden Heads were previously awarded.
- Added 1,000 Diamond Essence to Boss Collection tiers where Diamond Heads were previously awarded
- Adjusted the default dungeons class from Berserk -> Archer.
- Removed the ability to activate Berserk's Throwing Axe by sneaking while in The Catacombs.
- Adjusted the Shadow Assassin Cloak's speed stat:
  - Base: 3 -> 10
  - Fragged: 5 -> 15
- Removed the ability to activate Berserk's "Throwing Axe" via sneaking.
#### ➥ Fishing​
- Added a Golden Fish Tablist Widget to the Crimson Isle.
- Adjusted the speed of earlier-tier Fishing Minions to ensure the speed increase was more gradual between tiers.
- Adjusted Fishing Baits to only be consumed upon successful catch.
- Adjusted Sea Creatures to no longer attack players aside from the spawner unless provoked.
- Removed the mechanic that would cause you to catch nothing if you didn't move for a while when fishing.
#### ➥ Miscellaneous​
- Added the ability for players to retrieve perishable minion fuels (e.g Hyper Catalysts) from minions.
- Added reminder text to all Accessories to inform the player that they still function while in the Accessory Bag.
- Added the ability for Hoppity to take coins from Bank if you have insufficient funds in your Purse.
- Adjusted items within the 20s can't-buy period on the Auction House to be hidden until the 20s has elapsed.
- Removed the ability to Recombobulate several types of items:
  - Wisp Upgrade Stones
  - Enchanted Bookshelves
  - Bejewelled Handles
  - Soul String
  - Griffin Feathers
- Removed NPC sell prices from several special items:
  - Creative Mind
  - Quality Map
  - Dead Bush of Love
  - Game Breaker
  - Game Fixer
  - French Bread
  - The Cake
### ► Stranded EOL​
Back in August, we started work on an end-of-life update to the Stranded mode, working with the community to gather feedback and address some of its most pressing issues. Today we're presenting you with our final set of proposed changes to Stranded:
- Added the following NPCs to Stranded, they have no requirements unless otherwise stated:
  - Alchemist
  - Fishing Merchant
  - Einary
    - Einary only appears during the Winter.
  - Terry
  - Malik/Blacksmith
  - Roddy
  - Rusty
- Removed the Builder's collection requirement.
- Halved the HP and damage of Mobs on Stranded profiles.
- Added Legacy Treecapitator to Stranded.
  - You can convert your existing Treecapitator to Stranded version by placing it in the middle slot of the crafting GUI in the Workbench.
- The Weaponsmith now sells Arrows.
- All Fortunes now work on your Stranded island.
- Fixed Melody's Harp not working on Stranded.
- Enabled access to /mobtypes without visiting Ike while on Stranded.
- The Hex can be accessed on Stranded with the /hex command while a Booster Cookie is active.
- Added the following items to Villager trades:
  - Small Pocket Black Hole: 16 Emeralds, T1 Villager Trade
  - Medium Pocket Black Hole: 2 Enchanted Emeralds, T3 Villager Trade
  - Soul String: 32 Emeralds, T2 Villager Trade
  - Ancient Claw: 64 Emeralds for 16x, T3 Villager Trade
  - Enchanted Ancient Claw: 2 Enchanted Emeralds, T5 Villager Trade
  - Recomboulator 3000: 10 Enchanted Emeralds, T5 Villager Trade
  - Living Metal Heart: 5 Enchanted Emeralds, T5 Villager Trade
  - Titanium: 64 Emeralds, T3 Villager Trade
  - Challenging Rod: 32 Emeralds, T1 Villager Trade
  - Legend Rod: 4 Enchanted Emeralds, T3 Villager Trade
  - Rod of the Sea: 16 Enchanted Emeralds, T5 Villager Trade
  - Magma Rod: 32 Emeralds, T1 Villager Trade
  - Inferno Rod: 4 Enchanted Emeralds, T3 Villager Trade
  - Hellfire Rod: 16 Enchanted Emeralds, T5 Villager Trade
- Added a /trade for Oak Saplings for 250 coins per.
- Resync'd the Stranded Treasure Fishing loot pool with the regular loot pool.
  - Name Tags are still obtainable as a Good Catch, but will not be nearly as common as before.
- Significantly increased the obtainability of Golden/Diamond Goblins and Mithril/Gemstone Powder.
  - Increased the spawn chance of Golden/Diamond Goblins by 10x.
  - Increased the drop chance of Goblin Eggs dropped from eligible mobs by 2x.
  - Increased the powder drops of Golden/Diamond Goblins as follows:
    - Golden Goblin: 10,000 Mithril Powder
    - Diamond Goblin: 40,000 Mithril and Gemstone Powder
- Adjusted the following Villager item to emerald trades: (trade values may vary in practice as there's 11% variance implemented with villager trades)
  - Raw Mutton: 214 → 18 per emerald
  - Wool: 36 → 18 per emerald
  - Raw Rabbit: 33 → 9 per emerald
  - Wood (all types): 70 → 40 per emerald
  - Lapis Lazuli: 173 → 153 per emerald
  - Flint: 33 → 23 per emerald
  - Ghast Tear: 16 → 5 per emerald
  - Iron Ingot: 51 → 30 per emerald
  - Blaze Rod: 25 → 15 per emerald
  - Magma Cream: 46 → 25 per emerald
  - Slimeball: 59 → 25 per emerald
  - Glowstone Dust: 103 → 40 per emerald
  - Clay: 105 → 35 per emerald
  - Spider Eye: 118 → 27 per emerald
  - Coal: 60 → 30 per emerald
  - Gold Ingot: 40 → 25 per emerald
  - Gunpowder: 31 → 20 per emerald
  - Diamond: 29 → 20 per emerald
  - Nether Quartz: 38 → 15 per emerald
  - Ender Pearl: 26 → 15 per emerald
  - End Stone: 33 → 20 per emerald
  - Redstone: 120 → 88 per emerald
  - Bone: 33 → 22 per emerald
- Fixed being unable to obtain the Mosquito Pet and Mushroom Armor on Stranded.
- Removed the HoTM requirement for Beacons on Stranded.
- The HoTM task in the SkyBlock guide now displays Powder SBXP progress.
- There is now an End of Life indicator/warning when creating a new Stranded profile and hovering over your Stranded profile in the profile selector.
### ► Bug Fixes​
- Added some collections that were missing from the SkyBlock Guide.
- Fixed being unable to use Lesser Orb Of Healing on The Farming Islands.
- Fixed being able to apply pet skins to hidden pets.
- Fixed enemy health sometimes being formatted incorrectly.
- Fixed Market Info in the /viewitem menu not showing the correct auction prices.
- Adjusted Pet entities to display properly in latest versions
- Fixed Museum displays floating too high above their pedestals in latest versions
As usual, you should report any bugs at https://hypixel.net/bug-reports/create, making sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
We look forward to hearing your feedback about the Hub changes - hop on now and take a look around!

# Changelog/2026/January 20
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[January 20] SkyBlock 0.24.1 Release Candidate
Updated: January 20, 2026
Hello everyone!
Today we are opening alpha with the Release Candidate for SkyBlock 0.24.1. This patch mostly consists of bugfixes, with one new feature - Swappable Pet Items.
Spoiler: 1/26 Garden Changes
- Changed the Rosewater Flask to cap at 4 Growth Stages and work on the Greenhouse Plot the player is standing in
- Added Decay Time to Diagnostic Tool
- Added Water Lasting Time to Diagnostic Tool
- Added message in Diagnostic Tool Menu to show if a crop has enough water to fully grow
- Improved time displays in Diagnostic Tool Menu
- Added the item amount to the message given when dropping rare items from pests
- Added a message to the Plot Limit upgrade icon, clarifying that new plots come with the slots already unlocked
- Added Sunflower Oil and Bachelor's Rose to the Bazaar
- Added lore to the Voter's Badge accessories to show progress towards the next tier
- Fixed Sunflowers and Wild Roses planted in the Barn plot not growing and popping off as melon stems
- Fixed Jake's Plushie saying "Invalid reward item" in chat when trying to open the claim menu from chat
- Fixed Pig Pet being unable to trample Wild Rose, Sunflower, and Moonflower
- Fixed Pig Pet being unable to trample any crops sometimes
- Fixed Walk Speed cap being lower than expected with the Sundial and Rancher's Boots when interacting with Cactus Knives and Young Dragon Armor
- Fixed being unable to give a Bachelor's Rose to the current mayor
- Other mayor candidates will now say they are not eligible for a rose
- Fixed the Warty drop rate being lower than expected
- Fixed Sunflowers and Wild Roses not dropping Rarefinder Chips
- Fixed visitors giving low amounts of Farming Experience for Wild Rose, Sunflower, and Moonflower
- Fixed Voter's Badge counting all mayors served instead of unique mayors served
## ► Swappable Pet Items
Starting today, you can now freely replace your pet’s held item without deleting the old item. You will be refunded the old item once it's replaced:
The cost of the swap process depends upon the rarity of the item being replaced :
- COMMON : 25,000
- UNCOMMON : 50,000
- RARE : 100,000
- EPIC : 250,000
- LEGENDARY : 500,000
- MYTHIC : 1,000,000 (Futureproofing)
- DIVINE : 2,500,000 (Futureproofing)
### ► Other Pet Item Changes
- Added the Super Scrubber to Plumber Joe’s Shop for 25,000 Coins.
  - The Super Scrubber can be used to retrieve a pet item from a pet without applying another. The swap cost still applies.
- Adjusted the Minos Relic & Hephaestus Relic to now only increase Combat Stats.
  - Designer Note: We will delay this change for the time being as it puts both the Minos & Hephaestus Relics in an awkward spot when compared to the Antique & Hephaestus remedies. In a manner akin to the Bal change from a few years back, we will bench this for the time being and revisit it in the future as part of some larger Magic Find changes that we look to have a design thread out for soon.
- Adjusted the first line of text on all Pet Items to improve clarity.
- Adjusted rarity of All Skills Super-Exp Boost from COMMON → UNCOMMON.
- Adjusted rarity of Quick Claw from EPIC → LEGENDARY.
- Adjusted rarity of Brown Bandana from RARE → EPIC.
- Adjusted rarity of Washed-up Souvenir from LEGENDARY → EPIC.
- Adjusted rarity of Dwarf Turtle Shelmet from RARE → EPIC.
- Adjusted the rarity of all mythic-upgrading pet items (e.g Scuttler Shell) to MYTHIC.
- Disabled the Recombobulation of Pet Items.
## ► Changes and Bug Fixes
This patch contains many changes and bug fixes from over the holidays. View the full list below!
### ➥ General Changes
- Added a tool level requirement to the Overclocker 3000.
- Added shapeless crafting recipe for Enchanted Cookies.
- Added Sparrow and Seagull Shard Recipes to the Recipe Book and Quick Crafting.
- Added support for Bal in the boss spawn pet rule.
- Added Wet Books to the Hex.
- All 10 Hot Potato Books now must be applied before applying any Fuming Potato Books.
- Changed Pet Luck description to be more clear.
- Expanded the Oasis zone in the Farming Islands to include the other pools of water.
- Gave Royal Resident better names to avoid confusion.
- Increased the Jungle Pickaxe Heart of the Mountain requirement from level 3 to level 4.
- Pests now have 2 stamina bars when lassoing since kill credit is granted.
- Removed Mushroom Desert travel pet rule.
- Stereo Pants music discs no longer count as a treasure catch, and now give a custom message when fished up instead.
### ➥ UI Changes
- Improved the Flaming Fist description to be more clear on how it deals damage.
- Removed the "Unknown Map" label under an icon in the SkyBlock Guide.
- Updated some dye sources in Vincent's menus with mob types.
### ➥ Item Fixes
- Fixed Adaptive Blade lore mentioning an outdated Enchantment name.
- Fixed being able to open power scrolls like books and write in them.
- Fixed Builder's Wand being able to place blocks over minions.
- Fixed Enchanted Shears not working while offline.
- Fixed Experience Artifact not giving extra experience from XP orbs.
- Fixed Gauntlet of Contagion giving more drops than intended when killed (multiple of) certain mobs at the same time.
- Fixed Jerry Helmet being craftable outside of The Honk and Seek Adventure event.
- Fixed Sorrow Armor giving permanent invisibility in some cases.
- Fixed Tarantula Silk showing it needs Spider Slayer 2 instead of 7.
- Fixed Vampire Mask damaging Heavy Pearls and Tonsil/Pigs in Matriarch's Lair.
- Fixed items such as the Soul Esoward and Apples being deleted when dropped.
### ➥ Combat Fixes
- Fixed Auto Armor and Autonull mobs sometimes spawning impossible blocks past the barrier wall.
- Fixed Auto Armor mobs failing to find any suitable locations for their blocks when standing in certain spots.
- Fixed being able to stack Ferocity by attacking other players using the Fatal Tempo enchantment.
- Fixed bonuses that increase Vanquisher spawn chance sometimes reducing the chance for it to spawn.
- Fixed Cocoons spawning slayer bosses on the player.
- Fixed Empyrean Harpy intelligence debuffs not resetting after dying.
- Fixed Ferocity procs damaging Shellwise.
- Fixed Fishing Rods unable to damage Spooky mobs.
- Fixed knockback resistance not being properly removed in some cases, such as when unequipping the Slime Hat.
- Fixed Tarantula Broodfather having infinite health regeneration.
- Fixed the Headless Horseman despawning when the Spooky Festival ends.
- Fixed the Lifeline Attribute dealing more damage than intended.
- Fixed the Siamese Lynx not always counting kills for Bestiary.
- Fixed the Water Hydra and Revenant Slayer despawning when in water in the Galatea.
### ➥ Farming Fixes
- Fixed being able to break Sunflowers and Rose Bushes with weapons.
- Fixed being able to paste Cocoa Bean plots for free.
- Fixed being unable to break the bottom half of custom crops with replenish
- Fixed being unable to use the Moonflower and other Moonflower variants at the Composter in the Garden.
- Fixed certain crop hoes unlocking at wrong Garden levels.
- Fixed custom crops not being loaded properly along plot borders.
- Fixed Delicate enchantment not working on custom crops
- Fixed Enchanted Mushroom Blocks counting for less mushrooms than it’s supposed to for Garden plot pasting.
- Fixed some pests not giving shards.
- Fixed Sunflower minions not being categorized as Farming type.
- Fixed Vargul showing up as a Garden Visitor even when the Great Spook is not active.
### ➥ Fishing Fixes
- Fixed Worm Bait sending "unusable" message for every slot it’s in.
### ➥ Foraging and Hunting Fixes
- Fixed Azure Fish having the Combat category instead of Water.
- Fixed Black Hole and Lasso not granting loot and Bestiary credit in certain cases.
- Fixed Black Holes spawning slayer bosses on the player.
- Fixed chopping logs with Sweep 1 not giving any XP or drops.
- Fixed Hunting recipes not being shown in the Recipe Book.
- Fixed Fighting Boosters giving a higher damage boost than intended.
- Fixed Forest Training perk only being applied outside of Foraging Islands instead of only on them.
- Fixed Shellwise giving less shards to Loot Sharers.
### ➥ Mining Fixes
- Fixed an issue with Filet O' Fortune resetting when switching servers.
- Fixed Diamond Goblins not counting towards "Kill Goblins" commissions.
- Fixed Crystal Serendipity Attribute not working on Crystal Hollows lock-pickable chests.
- Fixed Gemstone Gauntlet's forge time reduction ability having a hidden 2 second cooldown.
  - EDIT : This fix has been reverted but the cooldown has been reduced to only 1 second.
- Fixed the Better Together event sometimes reducing your Mining Speed.
- Fixed Titanium Equipment having the incorrect item IDs while in the Forge menu.
- Fixed Top Cobblestone Slabs being unbreakable in Glacite Mineshafts.
### ➥ Pet Fixes
- Fixed the Snail pet's speed reduction would not work correctly on the Spider's Den.
### ➥ Misc. Fixes
- Fixed /call not supporting call recipient names with multiple words.
- Fixed an issue with the "Click to Claim" chat message for Mob Types.
- Fixed Attribute Shards in Bestiary saying "Shard" twice.
- Fixed being able to feed the Sven Packmaster.
- Fixed being able to use the Overload enchantment without unlocking it first.
- Fixed being unable to add more than 10 Hot Potato Books to the Shimmersparkle Chestplate if you close The Hex menu before maxing out.
- Fixed being unable to craft Lushlilac bonbons.
  - Recipe changed.
- Fixed being unable to craft Epic Griffin Upgrade Stones.
  - Recipe changed.
- Fixed being unable to reset Do Not Disturb status for some NPC contacts (Abiphone).
- Fixed candy from Primal Fears during the Great Spook not being put in your Trick or Treat Bag.
- Fixed Carrolyn's menu saying it gives Farming Fortune instead of Crop Farming Fortune.
- Fixed consuming Travel Scrolls not showing SkyBlock XP gained.
- Fixed David's Cloak costing a different amount of coins to reforge at the Blacksmith and The Hex.
- Fixed duplicate rabbits from Hoppity’s Easter Eggs incorrectly displaying "+0 chocolate".
- Fixed Enchanted Books, Shards and Pet recipes only showing 1 recipe even if you can craft multiple different types with the items in your inventory.
- Fixed Enchanted Coal Block NPC sell price being inaccurate.
- Fixed Enchantment Guide displaying unobtainable level 3, and 4 versions of Counter-Strike enchantment.
- Fixed Enchantment Guide displaying Experiments as a source for Venomous enchantment.
- Fixed Enchantment Guide showing Smite level 8.
- Fixed Enchantment Guide showing Smite upgradeable by End Stone Idol (replaced with Severed Hand).
- Fixed Haste given from Alixer during Bingo being removed when swapping servers.
- Fixed Item Value in /show incorrectly displaying gemstones.
- Fixed some Minions with Enchanted Hoppers that were still using the old Sell Price.
- Fixed some overlapping zone boundaries for the Void Sepulture and Zealot Bruiser Hideout.
- Fixed the Cow Minion Tier XII recipe requiring extra Enchanted Leather to upgrade.
- Fixed the lore on Shovels saying they can be reforged when they cannot.
- Fixed the maximum teleportation height for Wither Impact in the Hub.
- Fixed The Hex incorrectly showing that Master Stars can be applied to certain items.
- Fixed Trivia Quiz Puzzle not showing the username of the player that failed it.
### ➥ Typo Corner
- Fixed a formatting inconsistency with flipping items on the Bazaar.
- Fixed typo in Rusty Spoon’s Tetanus message.
As usual, you should report any bugs at https://hypixel.net/bug-reports/create, making sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
We look forward to hearing your feedback!

# Changelog/2026/January 30
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[January 30] Hub Revamp Alpha
Updated: January 30, 2026
Hello!
Today we have an Alpha testing session for SkyBlock that aims to test our revamped Hub world, SkyBlock in latest Minecraft versions, and a Museum Restructure. Please note that the minimum version for joining this alpha is 1.21.9.
These are both pretty major changes, so feel free to read more below!
Hub Revamp & SkyBlock in 1.21.9+
The Hub has moved to latest version and now has an entirely new look! As we mentioned towards the end of last year, we are looking to move SkyBlock to the latest versions of Minecraft throughout 2026. The Hub Revamp plays a key part in facilitating this and allowing us to utilize latest-version features all throughout the game, both on new and existing islands.
Moving the Hub to the latest version of the game is an important first step in our plans for SkyBlock in 2026. In order to best utilize latest features across the entirety of SkyBlock, we need to enforce all players joining with a minimum Minecraft version as soon as possible. This allows us to focus our development efforts solely on a much smaller subset of MC versions instead of attempting to continue supporting 1.8.9 and 1.21+ worlds and features simultaneously.
Over the coming months, we plan to release content in SkyBlock that takes advantage of the new blocks, mobs, and features offered in latest Minecraft versions. Additionally, existing islands will gradually be upgraded to take advantage of these features!
Museum Restructure
As part of this update we have also reorganized the museum, creating several new categories and adding a number of new items. You can find an exhaustive list of the new museum's various sections below:
- Combat
- Dungeoneering
- Farming
- Fishing
- Foraging
- Hunting
- Mining
- Misc.
This new categorization of the museum should be far more intuitive when browsing for both old and new players alike - let us know what you think of it!
Latest Islands Testing
For this Alpha session, we are testing all of the islands listed below in latest worlds. It is important to note that we may NOT release 1 or more of the listed islands due to performance issues, gameplay concerns, or any other unforeseen circumstances.
- Hub
- Spider's Den
- The End
- Gold Mine
- Farming Islands
The Hub is our primary focus of this testing session due to the substantial changes to its looks and gameplay. Other islands only contain visual upgrades, for now. Islands not listed remain unchanged in this initial phase but will be upgraded to use latest blocks and features over the coming months!
As usual, you should report any bugs at https://hypixel.net/bug-reports/create , making sure you select alpha.hypixel.net !
We look forward to hearing your feedback about the Hub changes - hop on now and take a look around!

# Changelog/2026/July 10
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[July 10] Torrhus Canyon, Safari Zone, and SkyBlock OST
Updated: July 10, 2026
Hello all!
Today on the Alpha Network we have, for you, two brand-new islands. Before you begin with your testing please be sure to read through the thread as, aside from providing you with the necessary details for informed testing, we also have a list of focus questions that we are would like your feedback on.
## ► Torrhus Canyon
Torrhus Canyon is the second Foraging Island comprising the region known as Galatea. Accessible from a launch pad in the Moonglade Marsh upon reaching Heart of the Forest 4, there is much to do and see here!
Upon entering the island for the first time, look for Helia just down Torrhus Path. She will introduce you to the Torrhus Chapters before directing you to Miria - the second Starlyn sister and host of this island's Starlyn Contests!
Upon talking to Miria, continue on and use Helia's "Torrhus Chapters" to inform your exploration.
### ➥ SkyBlock OST
While exploring Torrhus Canyon you'll get to hear the gorgeous soundtrack for the island composed by Oscar Garvin (you may know him as the composer for Hytale). He has also composed other songs that have been added for the Hub, the Garden, and the Moonglade Marsh. These are not note block songs, the recently added SkyBlock Resource Pack has allowed us to add fully orchestral songs to SkyBlock!
### ➥ A Note on Textures
In an attempt to gather as much public feedback as possible - given that we have not one, but two islands to test - we have opened the Alpha Network a bit earlier than usual.
As a result, you may find that some of Torrhus Canyon's items and icons are currently using placeholder assets. Don't fret - these placeholders will be replaced with their final textures in due course - but, for the time being, please don't file any bug reports on the matter. Thanks!
## ► Safari Zone
The Safari Zone is a dedicated Hunting Island consisting of four biomes - Icy, Forest, Canyon, and...Haunted? Accessible via the Safari Zone Entrance on Torrhus Canyon - so long as you have a ticket - the Safari is home to almost 40 unique critter species that cannot be found anywhere else in SkyBlock.
To get a ticket to enter the Safari, you'll need to perform in Miria's Contests.
- UNCOMMON Bracket ➜ 1x Basic Safari Ticket
- RARE Bracket ➜ 1x Economy Safari Ticket
- EPIC Bracket ➜ 1x Premium Safari Ticket
- LEGENDARY Bracket+ ➜ 1x First-Class Safari Ticket
A vanilla experience, while in the Safari Zone all of your stats are reset to the default and you'll be armed with nothing but a handful of Critter Capsules. Explore the various biomes, catching Critters, finding secrets, and collecting items as you go. Please note that you can enter the Safari alone or with up to 3 friends - simply use /party (but note that you will each need a ticket of your own to enter). Please also note that some critters do not spawn naturally - you will need to use your wits and the items you found strewn about to uncover the secrets of the Safari Zone.
## ► Questions for Testers
Please have a read of these questions before you begin your testing session and keep them in mind as you go about your playthrough, which should be informed through the Helia NPC by the entrance to the island and her "Torrhus Chapters" system.
### ➥ Torrhus Canyon
1. When cutting them using the correct technique, how did you feel about the strength of Helix Trees?
2. How does Tree density feel across the island? Too few, too many?
3. When it comes to cutting Trees, how does the pathing compare to the Moonglade Marsh?
4. How far into the Torrhus Chapters did you get? Did any particular task feel too early/hard for its Chapter?
5. Miria's Contests. How did you find them and their point gain compared to the Moonglade Marsh?
6. What did you think of the 3 new Collections?
7. There are 32 mob species in Torrhus Canyon. How many of them did you encounter? Which ones did you like most?
  1. Designer Note: Some Torrhus Canyon & Safari Zone Bestiaries are a bit inaccurate right now, needing a lot more kills than intended (e.g Beeheemoth, Grizzly Bear). Please give us some time to fix the brackets!
### ➥ Torrhus Springs
1. How deep into Torrhus Springs could you get in a single dive? What was your Respiration & Pressure Resistance?
2. How easy was it to reach the bottom when you used the higher-up rooms to break and refill your Respiration etc.?
3. What did you think of the Torrhus Sea Creatures?
4. What did you think of the various rooms underneath the springs (you have to dive down and swim to them)?
5. Did you reach the Temple? If so, how far did you get with the two puzzles?
### ➥ Safari Zone
1. There are 37 Critter species in the Critterdex. How many did you find on your first visit? (Use /critterdex or /be to check)
2. Following on from Q1, how many players did you play alongside in that session?
3. How did you feel about the Floor Drop system?
4. If you played in a group, how did you feel about the Lootshare system? Is it too strong compared to solo play?
5. Did you encounter any Bosses? If so, how did you feel about them?
6. What did you think of the Safari Essence Shop? Did it feel like anything was missing? Too cheap/expensive?
7. Any opinions on the Safari Milestones (found above the Safari Zone Entrance in Torrhus Canyon)?
Thanks in advance to everyone who answers the focus questions listed above. To reiterate, this will be an extended period of Alpha Testing, as was the case with the Lotus Atoll, so take your time.
And that's all we have for you today! We hope you have a great time testing - please remember to report any bugs you encounter HERE, being sure to select "alpha.hypixel.net".

# Changelog/2026/July 15
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[July 15] 0.26.1 Release Candidate
Updated: July 15, 2026
Hello!
Today we're putting the release candidate for the 0.26.1 update on the Alpha Network. This update includes a handful of improvements to the new player experience (plenty more to come in the future!), some changes/additions to the Harvest Feast, and another iteration of the Healing Revamp.
## ► New Player-Focused QOL
### ➥ Beginner Sacks
Beginner Sacks are a new tier of Sack beneath "Small". They only have a capacity of 640 items, and most of them only hold a smaller "beginner-focused" pool of items so that they don't overwhelm new players (for example, the Mining Sack only holds vanilla resources, so not Mithril).
Beginner Sacks are found in Tier II of certain collections but they can also be purchased for 250 Coins from the corresponding merchant.
Additional Sack Changes:
- Revamped their descriptions so that they are less overwhelming
- Sacks now have their own item type
- Moved some items around in the Pufferfish collection
### ➥ Accessory Changes
- Players now have 9 Accessory Bag slots unlocked by default
- Condensed Redstone Collection down to 9 tiers and rebalanced the slots they give across the tiers
- Reduced the Redstone needed to craft all the tiers of Personal Compactors
- Renamed Magical Power to Accessory Power so it is more clear where it comes from
- Accessories now list the Accessory Power that they give
- Reworked the descriptions of Power Stones to better explain what to do with them
- Added a dismissible chat message the first time a player finds an accessory
- Added an alert if you put an Accessory or an Equipment piece on your head slot
- Added an info button to the bottom of the Accessory Bag which lets you know how to unlock more slots
### ➥ Farming Changes
- Farming Armor Progression Changes
  - Farm Suit Armor
    - Renamed to Farmhand Armor
    - Swapped Farmhand Armor and the Harvesting Discount in Wheat Collection
    - Got rid of both abilities and gemstone slots
    - Changed some stats
    - Redid the colors
    - Added a tip that explains how Farming Fortune works to all pieces
  - Farm Armor
    - Renamed to Haymaker Armor
    - Changed Helmet to a Haybale
    - Not upgraded directly from Farmhand Armor
    - Removed both abilities and gemstone slots
    - Now unlocked at Wheat VI and made significantly cheaper to craft
    - Reduced rarity to COMMON
  - Pumpkin Armor
    - Renamed to Sprout Armor
    - Now an upgrade from Haymaker Armor
    - Found in Carrot Collection VIII now
    - Each piece now gives +15 Farming Fortune, removed existing ability
  - Melon Armor
    - Renamed to Tater Armor
    - Now an upgrade to Sprout Armor
    - Found in Potato Collection IX now
  - Rabbit Armor
    - Helmet is now a skull
    - Removed Farming Fortune and current Full Set Bonus
    - Each piece now gives Speed and Defense
    - New Full Set Bonus gives the player permanent Jump Boost III if they are sneaking
    - Moved to Raw Rabbit II and made significantly cheaper
  - Designer's Note: This new version of farming armor progression should be a major improvement over the old which required you to farm tons of Melons just to get started with Garden progression, only for you to then be asked to go back to farming Wheat/Carrot/Potatoes for Cropies, only for you later needing to go back to Melon for Squash.
- Added a new Husbandry Merchant to the Barn that sells "Basic" versions of the Husbandry Axes
  - Costs 100 Coins each
  - Deal +10 Damage and buff respective Animal Drops
- Existing Husbandry Axe changes
  - Renamed to "Advanced <animal> Axe"
  - Now deal +20 Damage
  - Changed to the Sword item type
  - Updated their descriptions
  - Purchasing them now costs the Basic tier of that Axe
- Rookie Hoe Changes
  - Added +5 Farming Fortune to the Rookie Hoe
  - Removed ability
- Added a description that explains what Farming Fortune does
- The Farmer NPC now gives a Rookie Hoe upon completion of his quest
- Added a Rookie Axe to the Farm Merchant for 10 Coins
- Buffed Basic Gardening Hoe/Axe to +10 Farming Fortune
- Buffed Advanced Gardening Hoe/Axe to +15 Farming Fortune
- The Farmhand NPC's quest now rewards the player with a COMMON Chicken Pet and tells the player how to equip it
### ➥ Other Changes
- Players are now directed to the Bartender's quest first instead of Fisherwoman Enid
  - He now also gives players an Undead Sword when starting the quest
- Removed Bookshelf Power from the game, no enchantments require it anymore
  - Made Enchanted Bookshelves a legacy item
  - Added a new Pet Item to Sugar Cane VII: Magic For Pets In a Hurry
    - Grants +20 Speed and +50 Intelligence
- Renamed the "Your SkyBlock Profile" button in the SkyBlock Menu to "Stats & Equipment"
- Buffed various pieces of gear that Rosetta sells
  - Each piece of Squire and Mercenary Armor now gives +5 and +10 Strength respectively
  - Increased the Health and Defense given by Celeste Armor
  - Significantly increased the base Ability Damage of the Celeste Wand (but reduced its ability scaling) and reduced its mana cost to 25
  - Significantly increased the base Ability Damage of the Starlight Wand (but reduced its ability scaling) and reduced its mana cost to 100
- All Minion items now show what type they are in dark gray text under the item name
- Placed a button shortcut to the Enchantment Guide in the Librarian's menu
- Uncapped the NPC Buy Limit on Experience Bottles
- Players now get sent a dismissible message when they get their first Rune, explaining how they work
- Added a "Right-click to equip!" prompt to Equipment pieces
  - This comes with a new dismissible message the first time you do this
- You now get a warning if you try to place a Crafting Table on a public island
- Added a line to Dusk's dialogue regarding the existence of the Rune Sack
- Added a "Right-click to view recipes!" prompt to the regular Compactor
- Rephrased some things in the description for the Crystal Hollows in Rhys' guide menu
- Added /warps as an alias to /warp
- Added /island and /privateisland as aliases of /is
- Renamed the Adventurer NPC to the Combat Merchant
- Changed Superboom TNT's description to say "Cracked walls" instead of "Weak walls"
- Using /warp jerry outside of Late Winter now specifies you need to wait until Late Winter to go, rather than just "Winter" generically
## ► Harvest Feast Changes
We've been gathering a lot of feedback regarding the Harvest Feast event since its release a couple of months ago. Today we are releasing a large batch of additions and changes to the Alpha Network in response to feedback.
### ➥ New Items
- Orchid Mantis Pet
  - Grants base Overbloom and Speed
  - First perk is "Swift Sickles", which grants +1 Farming Fortune for every 3 Speed you have
  - Second perk is "Intelligent Specimen", which grants +20% more Farming Tool Exp
  - Third perk is "Orchid Nectar", which gives you a 0.02% chance to find Jelly or Plant Matter when farming crops
  - Crafted and upgraded with Floral Gelatin
  - Designer's Note: This Pet is intended to give players earlier in progression more access to Overbloom and a way to upgrade their Farming Tools a bit faster if that is wanted. The third perk is also something entirely new, giving players a way to get more Sprayonator items independently of Pests if they so choose.
- Blooming Thorns
  - Applies the "Thorny" Reforge to Equipment
  - Grants Farming Fortune and Overbloom
  - Thorny Bonus grants additional Overbloom per tier of Thorns you have on your armor
  - Crafted with Cactus Flowers, Thorn Fragments, Thornshade, and Compacted Wild Roses
  - Designer's Note: Thorns IV is a bit of a meme enchantment, maybe it'll be reworked someday. For the time being it'll have a small legitimate purpose in the game: giving you a bit more Overbloom if you are using this equipment reforge.
- Feast Burger With A Side of Deepfries
  - Can be consumed up to 5 times, each time granting a permanent +1 Overbloom
  - Crafted with Enchanted Hay Bales, Deepfries, Feastfungus, and Cornucopias
  - Designer's Note: Gives another use to some of the less desirable Feast RARE CROPS.
### ➥ Feast Changes
- There are now 4 crops per season instead of 3 during Harvest and Grand Feasts
- Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination Changes
  - Has been completely revamped
  - Added a new "Juicy Nozzle" to Melon Slice Collection which upgrades the Sprayonator to the "Juicy Sprayonator"
    - Upgrades spray duration to 45m
    - Allows you to spray x3 materials for x2 base effects
  - Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination has turned into the "Groovy Nozzle" which upgrades the Juicy Sprayonator to the "Groovy Sprayonator"
    - Upgrades spray duration to 60m
    - Allows you to spray x5 materials for x3 base effects
  - Nerfed based Sprayonator a bit to account for these two new upgrades
    - Note that the "Groovy Sprayonator" is better than the old base Sprayonator was, so this is not an overall nerf
  - Left-clicking the Sprayonator now opens a menu that lets you easily select which material and nozzle you want to use
  - Designer's Note: This Disco Destination has caused a multitude of issues since its original release. It was too easily abused by cheaters and quite literally took the "hunting" out of "pesthunting". Pesthunting is intended to be something to engage players more actively while farming, and the item in its current state worked directly against that. We strongly believe this design to be healthier for the game long term, and the Sprayonator has always felt like it needed an upgrade. This seemed like a good opportunity to kill two birds with one stone.
- You now only need x2 Feastfungi to craft "Fragranced" Brown Mushroom "Paste"
  - Designer's Note: This item was previously far too expensive for the value it provided.
- Added x5 Carnival Tickets to each Donation Tier for both Harvest and Grand Feasts
- Buffed the NPC Sell Price of all Feast RARE CROPS to 50,000 Coins
  - Designer's Note: The NPC Sell Price buff here is mainly targeted at Ironman players who have maxed out specific RARE CROPS but of course are still dropping them while farming/pesthunting.
- You can no longer fuse into the Cocoaleech Shard
  - Designer's Note: Bypassed the intentional way of obtaining this item. We want for Mutations to be used in more recipes, but effectively no one crafted this Shard so it was as if we never added a recipe to it in the first place.
- Significantly buffed the amount of Copper earned from the Fungal Tip Jar
  - Old: 25% chance to tip 2 or 3 Copper
  - New: 75% chance to tip between 2 and 5 Copper
  - Designer's Note: This item felt very underwhelming for how much it cost. Hopefully it feels better now for the price you pay.
- The Lucky Clover and Poignant Lucky Clover now give even more Overbloom, +7 and +13 respectively
  - Designer's Note: As a reminder, the Magic Find given by these items will be removed at some point in the future with the Magic Find Rework. You can read more about the Magic Find Rework here.
- Jacob's Participation Medal Changes
  - Made a legacy item, it can still be used as a Minion Fuel but it can no longer be obtained
  - Added the "Super Jacob Entertainment System" Minion Upgrade item which generates Jacob's Tickets at the same rate
  - Crafted with Chirping Stereos, a PlantBoy Advance, Botroot, Enchanted Paper, and Enchanted Iron Blocks
  - Designer's Note: The original item, while thematically charming, was far too expensive to ever be worth using, especially given that it was temporary.
- Rarefinder Chip now gives +1.5/+2/+2.5 Overbloom per level instead of +2/+2.5/+3
  - At max level it now gives +50 Overbloom instead of +60 Overbloom
  - Designer's Note: The Rarefinder Chip gives such a large portion of the Overbloom in the game. With this update we are redistributing the amount it gave to other sources.
- By shift right-clicking you will consume the entire stack of Locust Larva in your hand
- Overpriced Drinks are now crafted with x8 Aggourdians and x8 Designer Coffee Beans instead of x14 Aggourdians and x64 Enchanted Sugar
  - Designer's Note: Mainly a thematic change but also balances out the usefulness of both of these RARE CROPS a bit more.
- Increased the difficulty from Easy to Medium for the Feast Brothers contacts in the Contacts Directory
### ➥ Pet Changes
- Elephant Pet Changes
  - MYTHIC Elephant's "Abundant Harvest" perk has been buffed to give +20% Sowdust (up from +15%)
  - Added +50 Farming Fortune to the base stats of MYTHIC Elephants
- Mooshroom Cow Pet Changes
  - Removed "Efficient Mushrooms" perk
"Mushroom Eater" is now the first perk "Farming Strength" is now the second perk
  - Added a new third perk "Bovine Blessing" which gives you a 0.02% chance of find Cheese or Dung when farming crops
  - Base Farming Fortune now scales from 1 → 100 instead of 11 → 110
  - Refreshed item lore
- Bee Pet Changes
  - Replaced "Weaponized Honey" with a new perk "Honey Harvester", which gives you a 0.02% chance to find Honey Jars when farming crops
  - Halved the amount of Floral Gelatin needed to craft Royal Jelly
  - Bea now sells Honey Dippers to upgrade your COMMON Bee Pet instead of the higher rarities directly
  - Changed the default skin to better resemble the Bee from vanilla Minecraft
- Designer's Note: We wanted to give players (especially Ironman players) a way to grind more Sprayonator items directly if they so choose. Previously the only real way of getting most of these items was from killing Pests, meaning there was never really a way to stockpile them. The only way to have a chance at getting one was to spend one in the first place.
## ► Healing Revamp
Healing Revamp is back on Alpha with a ton of adjustments since the last testing session.
A quick summary for the uninitiated:
- Healing Revamp reworks Vitality as a resource stat that governs how often you can cast healing abilities.
- We don't intend for existing content to be harder as a result of these changes.
  - We have since done a ton of damage adjustments that should in theory, make the current healing amount feel adequate.
This thread will contain a list of changes for Healing Revamp since the initial Public Alpha.
We would once again like to invite you all to test the Healing Revamp changes on Alpha, let us know if healing feels adequate in Combat content compared to the live network, including:
- Dungeons
- Slayers
- Crimson Isle
- Kuudra
- Fishing
- Mythological (Diana should be active during this Alpha session)
For those of you interested in the reasoning behind various changes in Healing Revamp, you should check out this design thread. Feedback regarding Healing Revamp can also be discussed there.
### ➥ Changelog
- Sea Creature Adjustments
  - Nessie
    - Nessie’s “charge” attack: “50%+ True Damage” → 2x the damage stat (4k)
    - Base damage: 2,500 → 2,000
  - Wiki Tiki
    - Wiki Tiki Totem-Beams: 1.5k True Damage → 8,000 flat damage.
  - Thunder
    - Leap Ability: 50% True Damage → 15,000 Damage
    - Eye Beam?: 12.5% True Damage → 2,000 Damage
  - Lord Jawbus
    - Laser: 75% True Damage → 1x Base Damage
    - Fireballs: 1x Base Damage → 0.15x Base Damage
    - Base Damage: 15,000 → 10,000
  - Ragnarok
    - Base Damage: 20,000 → 10,000
- Mythological Event Adjustments
  - King Minos
    - Rage mechanic reworked
    - OLD: changing regular damage into true damage based on times hit
    - NEW: increases regular damage based on time alive, similar to Inquisitor
    - Bobber stat reduction cap: -80% → -50%
    - Bobber damage: 20% Max Hp True Damage → 5,000 Regular Damage
    - Slam damage: 15% Max Hp True Damage → 4,000 Regular Damage
  - Manticore
    - Damage: 8,000 → 5,000
  - Sphinx Damage (Based on Griffin Rarity)
    - Legendary 4,000 → 3,500
    - Mythic 7,000 → 5,000
  - Gaia Lightning Ability (Based on Griffin Rarity)
    - Rare 20% Max HP True Damage → 5,000 Regular Damage
    - Epic 30% Max HP True Damage → 7,500 Regular Damage
    - Legendary 40% Max HP True Damage → 10,000 Regular Damage
    - Mythic 50% Max HP True Damage → 15,000 regular Damage
  - Harpy
    - Int and Vit stat reduction capped at -50%
  - Cretan Bull Damage (Based on Griffin Rarity)
    - Common 5% Max HP True Damage → 1,000 Regular Damage
    - Uncommon 10% Max HP True Damage → 2,000 Regular Damage
    - Rare 15% Max HP True Damage → 3,000 Regular Damage
    - Epic 20% Max HP True Damage → 4,000 Regular Damage
    - Legendary 30% Max HP True Damage → 6,000 Regular Damage
    - Mythic 40% Max HP True Damage → 10,000 Regular Damage
  - Inquisitor Damage (Based on Griffin Rarity)
    - Legendary 750 → 700
    - Mythic 1000 → 900
- Other
  - Littlefoot
    - Base Damage: 15,000 → 10,000
- Dungeons
  - Dungeon stat scaling has been adjusted.
  - Basically everyone that’s not Cata 50 is now stronger.
  - Dungeon Mob damage has been adjusted specific to each mob
  - There is 1 Health nerf, but there are 2 damage nerfs that combine.
  - The comparison of current mob stats versus alpha mob stats (both adjustments applied) can be found HERE.
  - Nerfed HP and Damage of regular Dungeon Mobs
    - Mobs on E0 to F6 deal less damage on top of the mob-specific adjustments This is mostly intended for the new player experience.
      - Toggle: “Damage Multipliers”
        - Entrance: 0.70×
        - Floor 1 : 0.50×
        - Floor 2 : 0.45×
        - Floor 3 : 0.50×
        - Floor 4 : 0.45×
        - Floor 5 : 0.70×
        - Floor 6 : 0.80×
      - Reduced some big damage numbers in Dungeons:
        - Fire Traps: 50% HP Damage → 15% HP Damage
        - Arrow Traps: 50% HP Damage → 15% HP Damage
        - Crusher: 100% HP Damage → 30% HP Damage
        - Tripwire Traps: 33% HP Damage → 15% HP Damage
        - Lava Damage (applicable to F7, M7):
        - F7: 40% HP → 8,000 Regular Damage
        - M7: 40% HP → 60,000 Regular Damage
  - Berserk
    - Now awards 0.75% Melee Damage increase per level up (multiplicative).
    - Melee Hits are 5x more effective at scaling the Lust For Blood damage bonus.
  - Mage
    - Now awards 5 Ability Damage + 1 Ability Damage every 5 levels.
  - Healer
    - Now awards 10 + 0.2 Vitality per Class Level
    - Now awards 10 + 0.5 (flat) Mending per Class Level
    - Mending now affects Healer’s self healing.
  - Tank
    - Now awards 10 + 0.5 Vitality per Class Level
- Slayers
  - Adjusted Tarantula Slayer’s Vitality Reduction
    - T2: 15%
    - T3: 30%
    - T4: 50%
    - T5: 75%
    - The Webbed Cells perk now works against this Vitality reduction.
  - Adjusted Enderman Slayer damage
    - Base Damage
      - T1: 1,200 → 1,000
      - T2: 5,000 → 3,500
      - T3: 12,000 → 9,000
      - T4: 21,000 → 15,000
    - Dissonance
      - T1: 720 → 600
      - T2: 3,000 → 2,000
      - T3: 7,200 → 5,000
      - T4: 12,600 → 9,000
    - Malevolent Hitshield
      - T3 & T4: 3,000 → 2,000
    - Nukekubi Fixations
      - T3: 800 → 600
      - T4: 2,000 → 1,500
  - Blaze Slayer miniboss damage:
    - Flare Demon: 7,000 → 2,500
    - Kindleheart Demon: 12,000 → 5,000
    - Burningsoul Demon: 30,000 → 9,000
    - Smoldering Polarization’s immunity effect will last for 10s after you kill the boss.
    - You can now destroy Blaze Pillars without a Wisp pet.
      - You can destroy them faster by using a Wisp Pet, and having the Coldfused reforge.
- Crimson Isle
  - Minibosses have been adjusted as follows:
    - Damage requirement for loot reduced to 250,000.
    - Ashfang
      - Ashfang’s Gravity Orb now deals 5,000 damage per second (instead of 1-tap)
      - Reduced Ashfang’s healing per blaze absorbed to 250k
      - Reduced Ashfang’s Blaze HP to 2m.
    - Bladesoul
      - Reduced Bladesoul’s base damage to 3,000
      - Reduced Bladesoul’s Wither Plume to 7,500 regular damage.
    - Mage Outlaw
      - Attempted to make DoT effects not nuke you against Mage Outlaw.
    - Barbarian Duke
      - Reduced Barbarian Duke X’s damage to 2,500.
      - Reduced the stun duration of Barbarian Duke X’s leap by 50%.
    - Magma Boss
      - Reduced the HP of Magma Boss and its minions by 20%.
- Kuudra mob damage has been halved.
  - This practically does nothing for newer players, the goal is for end-game players to never struggle with healing in Kuudra, and this should accomplish it.
- Added Plasma to Mithril Grubbers loot pool
  - This lets you obtain Plasma without killing Ghosts.
  - It’s likely even more efficient than killing ghosts, and that’s fine.
    - This is assuming you have very minimal fishing stats, too.
- Vitality is now permanently displayed after you first use an item that costs Vitality.
- Changed Refrigerate cost from Einary to require less Volcanic Rocks.
- Removed healing from Werewolf Armor.
- Explosive Arrows no longer damage yourself.
- Vampire Dentist Relic now has a 20s cd on proc.
  - Considering letting this proc more frequently, but healing for less.
- Flaming Flay no longer heals when hitting non-Sea Creatures.
- Adjusted items that have health costs into flat health costs, as follows:
  - Gloomlock Grimoire: 25% -> 800 (Life Tap)
  - Flower of Truth: 10% -> 100
  - Bouquet of Lies: 10% -> 250
  - Sword of Bad Health: 5% -> 100
  - Staff of the Volcano: 50% -> 1,000
- Removed the Overflow Mana cost on Gloomlock Grimoire's Extreme Measures ability.
If you encounter any bugs or unexpected behavior, please report it HERE! That's all for today! :hypixel_smile:

# Changelog/2026/July 20
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[July 20] 0.26.1 Alpha Changes
Updated: July 20, 2026
Hello! Today we are rolling some changes to the 0.26.1 on the Alpha Network in response to player feedback.
7/21 Changes
- Added textures for all the Honey Dippers
- Fixed Pests not being able to spawn on non-full blocks
- Updated the visuals and sounds for the Salty Sprayonator
### ➥ Farming Changes
- Re-added Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination but it now only works on offline Pests
  - Removed all of the Salted Sunflower Seeds from the recipe and slightly reduced the amount of Botroot used
  - The Groovy Nozzle is now only crafted with Salted Sunflower Seeds, renamed it to the "Salty Nozzle"
  - Buffed the Salty Nozzle to x3 chance for Pests to spawn in sprayed plots
  - Slightly reduced the cost of the Juicy Nozzle
  - Designer's Note: While we still want to maintain the integrity of active Pesthunting, we acknowledge that effectively removing the Disco Destination from the game was not ideal. As such we are bringing the item back, although it will only work on Pests that spawn offline (something we got a lot of suggestions for). We're still keeping the Nozzles around as we think they are cool rewards to go for.
- Added textures for the following items:
  - Blooming Thorns
  - Deepfries
  - Feastburger With A Side of Deepfries
  - Feastfungus
  - Jacob's Entertainment System
- Pests can now spawn in Greenhouse plots
- Swapped Swift Sickles and Intelligent Specimen perk on the Orchid Mantis Pet
### ➥ Healing Revamp Changes
- Adding some healing when you kill a slayer boss.
  - Healing per second is determined by your Slayer Tier
    - T1: 40
    - T2: 80
    - T3: 120
    - T4: 160
    - T5: 200
  - Duration of healing scales with your Slayer Level
    - Base: 6s
    - Slayer Level 2: 7s
    - Slayer Level 4: 8s
    - Slayer Level 6: 9s
    - Slayer Level 8: 10s
  - e.g if I'm Slayer Level 7 and doing T4 Enderman, I'll regenerate 160 health per second for 9 seconds.
  - Does not work with Vampire Slayer.
- Reduced the Vitality cost of Gloomlock Grimoire from 50 to 45.
- Reduced the cooldown of the Drain enchant from 0.5s to 0.25s.
- Re-increased the damage of Lord Jawbus, as follows:
  - [x] Fireballs: 0.15x Base Damage → 0.25x Base Damage (2,500)
  - [x] Jawbus Laser: 1x Base Damage → 3x Base Damage (30,000)
  - This should remain manageable for players with weaker gear, while punishes stronger players who insist on standing still.
- Enderman Slayer Damage Adjustments
  - Base Damage
    - T1: 1,000 → 900
    - T2: 3,500 → 3,200
    - T3: 9,000 → 8,000
    - T4: 15,000 → 13,500
  - Dissonance
    - 0.4x Base Damage
  - Nukekubi Fixations
    - T4: 2,000 → 1,500
- Blaze Slayer Damage Adjustments
  - Base Damage (nerfed around ~15%)
  Demonlord: Tier 1, Phase 1: 45, Phase 2: 65, Phase 3: 115; Demonlord: Tier 2, Phase 1: 65, Phase 2: 130, Phase 3: 190; Demonlord: Tier 3, Phase 1: 80, Phase 2: 160, Phase 3: 320; Demonlord: Tier 4, Phase 1: 385, Phase 2: 575, Phase 3: 955
- Buffed the Demonslayer Gauntlet
  - Increased the True Defense from +5 to +20.
  - Now increases damage against Infernal mobs by 15%.
- Fixed an issue where Mosquito Bow healed for more than intended.
- Removed the Venom Shot ability of Arachne's Keeper.
  - It should now deal damage equal to its base damage of 200, making it twice as threatening as your average spider.
- Catacombs Damage Changes
  - Watcher Summons
    - Livid

          - (This used to deal 400,000 x 8 = 3.2m damage)
  - Floor 2 — Scarf (Note that the base damage of Scarf’s minions have already been nerfed by 20%, which affects the scaling of their damage).

# Changelog/2026/July 22/Alpha
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[July 22] Torrhus Canyon Alpha Testing #2 + Bestiary Reductions
Updated: July 22, 2026
Hello again!
Today we are back once again with a second round of testing for Torrhus Canyon and the accompanying Safari Zone. If you missed the initial round of testing, please consult THIS THREAD to get yourself up to speed with what was included in the original update, as this thread will only be going over the changes made following the feedback from the initial Alpha session.
## ► Foraging Update Changes
### ➥ Torrhus Canyon
- Increased Queen Ant spawn chance from 1% -> 2.5%
- Improved Queen Ant Chapter text to convey that they are more likely to spawn from Anthills
- Added forced Lootshare to any mobs that you spawn from Tree Gifts
- Reduced Honeycomb used in Fun-Sized Pot of Honeycomb from 1x Enchanted Honeycomb -> 64x Honeycomb
- Added forced Lootshare to any mobs that you spawn from smearing Honey on Trees
- Renamed and retextured Condensed Honeycomb -> Enchanted Honeycomb Block
- Tree Protection Order holograms now say "Critter" instead of "Mob"
- Prevented abilities, such as Homing Axe, from targeting Protected Trees
- Renamed Acceleration talisman line -> Accretion
- Forager Daniel and Forager Rowan now hold Helix Choppers
- Adjusted Hivethief to use standard Lasso mechanics instead of requiring 2+ players
- Improved zone entry message for the Spring Shallows
- Reduced Drybark's backstab attack from 25% Max HP damage -> 500 damage
- Added Large Pocket Black Hole to Ruby Veilshroom Collection 8
- Adjusted the effect of the Rubber Snorkel:
  - Grants +5 Respiration, doubled while on Foraging Islands.
- Removed the Rubber Snorkel from Sprawl drop table - sole method of obtainment is through Miria now
- Removed RARE Foraging XP Boost from Tree Gifts, added to Drybarks at a 0.1% drop rate
- Increased Honeycomb NPC sell price from 20 -> 100 coins
- Added Bottled Mischief as a 0.1% drop from Pucks. Used to craft the Torrhus Belt
- Rewrote all Torrhus Canyon critter drop tables
- Reduced Solar Shard from +1-10% more Starlyn Contest points -> +0.5-5% more
### ➥ Safari Zone
- Leaving the Safari Zone now spawns you at /warp safari instead of /warp torrhus
- Increased Critter catch rates:
  - COMMON Critters: 100% -> 100% (Unchanged)
  - UNCOMMON Critters: 50% -> 75%
  - RARE Critters: 25% -> 50%
  - EPIC Critters: 10% -> 25%
  - LEGENDARY Critters: 5% -> 5% (Unchanged)
- Reduced the invisible hard pity values that guarantee a catch after a certain number of attempts (takes less attempts to hit pity now)
- Improved the Wumpa fight
- Reduced amount of Shining Coins that drop at a time from 3x -> 1x
- Increased Scrappy damage from 10 -> 25
- Adjusted mentions of the Safari Belt to be dark green
- Added indicative text in front of the biome that is selected as your Hunting Hotspot
- Added Booster slots to the Safari Belt
- Added a new Secret Achievement
- Lootshared kills now count towards Safari Milestones
- Added a Hunting 15 requirement to access the Safari Zone
- Added Hunting XP to the Safari Zone Floor Drop pool
- Added a perk to the Safari Essence Shop that gives Hunting Wisdom while in the Safari Zone
- Improved Safari Zone Reward Summary
- Added a message when throwing a Critter Capsule at an NPC
- Added varying amounts of Hunting Wisdom to the various Safari Zone tickets:
  - Basic Ticket → +10 Hunting Wisdom
  - Economy Ticket → +25 Hunting Wisdom
  - Premium Safari Ticket → +50 Hunting Wisdom
  - First-Class Safari Ticket → +100 Hunting Wisdom
    - The goal is for Safari Zone Critters to provide the most Hunting XP/hour of all methods, given that a ticket is required for entry and Hunting 50 has been added, meaning players need a lot more XP to max the skill than before. We will continue to monitor.
- Co-op Soulbound Rainbow Feathers
- Removed some levers from the Cavern Biome
- The entry cutscene now plays faster for players who have already seen it
### ➥ General Foraging
Adjusted how Flexbones are caught:
  - Flexbones now have a 25% chance to replace a Treasure Catch when fishing in the Tomb Floodway. Amount of Flexbones received scales with catch tier:
    - GOOD: 1x
    - GREAT: 2-4x
    - OUTSTANDING: 8x
- Fixed the Throwing Axe Penalty applying incorrectly
- Added Desert Whispers, which are used for HOTF4+ and COTF4+
  - Players who currently have over 12.5m Forest Whispers will have all whispers in excess of that amount converted to Desert Whispers at a 1:1 ratio
  - Desert Whispers are gained from Torrhus Canyon, Forest Whispers are gained from the Moonglade Marsh
- Replaced Fading Green Rune in Helix Tree Gifts with Fading Blue Rune
- Reduced Tree Gift Milestones for all Tree types:
  - I: 10 -> 10
  - II: 50 -> 25
  - III: 250 -> 100
  - IV: 500 -> 250
  - V: 1,000 -> 500
  - VI: 2,500 -> 1,000
  - VII: 5,000 -> 2,500
- Adjusted Helia's Chapters tasks requiring you to reach x bracket to complete instantly instead of requiring contest reward claim
- Replaced 1x Helixis in Helix Chopper recipe with 8x Flexbone
- Added Stretching Sticks to the Moonglade Marsh section of the Bazaar
- Adjusted HOTF XP awarded by each Tree Gift Type:
  - Fig: 10 -> 10
  - Mangrove: 10 -> 15
  - Helix: 10 -> 20
- Ruby Veilshrooms and Sea Lumies now give Foraging XP
- Adjusted Jade Dragon Cost:
  - Figstone: 100 -> 64
  - Mangcore: 100 -> 64
  - Helixis: 0 -> 64
    - Added Common Hunting Wisdom Boosters to Rosemary's Shop for 250 Forest Essence
Adjusted Tree toughness:
  - Fig: 7 -> 10
  - Mangrove: 50 -> 50
  - Helix: 200 -> 200
- Adjusted Tree NPC Prices in accordance with Toughness:
  - ig: 7 -> 3
  - Mangrove: 8 -> 15
  - Helix: 9 -> 60
    - The goal here is, as we wish to do with Mining, to make later-game areas more profitable to NPC than early-game ones. Fig being meta dissuaded new players from exploring deeper into Galatea - we will continue to monitor how these changes affect the Foraging MMM meta.
Adjusted Foraging XP in accordance with Toughness:
  - Fig: 1,000 -> 100
  - Mangrove: 1,250 -> 500
  - Helix: 1,500 -> 2,000
- Adjusted Sweep of various items:
  - Seriously Damaged Axe: 7 -> 10
  - Fig Hew: 7 -> 15
  - Figstone Splitter: 24 -> 25
  - Fig Armor: 6 -> 10 per piece
  - Helix Armor: 10 -> 20 per piece
  - Helix Chopper: 40 -> 50
- Removed some sources of % Sweep:
  - Core of the Forest 2: +5% Sweep -> +1 Axe Ability Level (Moved from COTF1)
  - Core of the Forest 4: +10% Sweep -> +5 Tracking on Tree Gifts
  - Lottery HOTF4: +5% Sweep -> +10 Sweep
  - Agatha's Power I-V: +1-5% Sweep -> +5-25 Sweep
  - Miria's Power I-V: +1-5% Sweep -> +5-25 Sweep
### ➥ General Hunting
- Critters no longer show their HP stat as there is no way for players to interact with it
- Critter Stamina Bars now scale with Shard rarity:
  - COMMON Shard -> 1 Stamina Bar
  - UNCOMMON Shard -> 2 Stamina Bars
  - RARE Shard -> 3 Stamina Bars
  - EPIC Shard -> 4 Stamina Bars
  - LEGENDARY Shard -> 5 Stamina Bars
    - Also adjusted colors of Stamina Bars to follow the item rarity system
- Updated "Found in Grass" shard descriptions to instead refer to the Floor Drops system directly
- Added text indicating to the Murkbat Shard indicating that it has a +1 score cap
- Added text to the Special Zealot shard indicating that recombobulated eyes increase chance of Superior Dragon
- Added the Wiki Tiki and Field Mouse Shards to the Elusive family
- Improved Pocket Black Hole itemlores
### ➥ Other
- Disabled "Talk to Susan" objective for all players not in The Hub
- Players placing Recombobulated Summoning Eyes now increases the chance of getting a Superior Dragon:
  - 0/8: 4% -> 4% (Unchanged)
  - 1/8: 4% → 12.5%
  - 2/8: 4% → 25.0%
  - 3/8: 4% → 37.5%
  - 4/8: 4% → 50.0%
  - 5/8: 4% → 62.5%
  - 6/8: 4% → 75.0%
  - 7/8: 100% → 87.5%
  - 8/8: 100% (Unchanged)
- Adjusted Dragon Piper perk and increased cost from 2,500 -> 5,000 Dragon Essence:
  - Summoning Eyes you drop have a +5% chance to be recombobulated.
- Adjusted damage dealt by Vacuums:
  - SkyMart Vacuum: 100 → 100
  - SkyMart Turbo Vacuum: 120 → 150
  - SkyMart Hyper Vacuum: 150 → 200
  - Infinivacuum: 200 → 300
  - Infinivacuum Hooverius: 200 → 400
- Increased damage added to Vacuums by Bookworm's Favorite Book from 10 -> 20
## ► Bestiary Reductions
Following the conclusion of the recent SkyBlock Designer Q&A, we have had a lot of discussions about the place of the Bestiary within SkyBlock. Since its initial release as part of SkyBlock 0.11.2, the Bestiary has been through several permutations - starting off as something that was borderline impossible to max but, with the introduction of SkyBlock XP, slowly transitioned into something that was more feasible to complete.
### ➥ Bestiary Issues
However, there were still many problems with the Bestiary that quickly became apparent to anyone trying to max it:
1. The Bestiary is usually the last aspect of a piece of content that you will max out - it inflates how long you need to play a piece of content for.
2. Early-game Bestiaries (e.g. Lapis Zombie) were inherently unhealthy due to forcing veterans to compete with new players for kills.
3. While some Bestiaries had been modernized and weren't actually too difficult, there were many outliers that still demanded far too much player time.
4. By the time you max out a Bestiary, getting all of its rewards, you're completely done with that content, so you never get to reap the bonus Magic Find, Coins, XP Orbs, and other rewards on that mob.
### ➥ Resulting Bestiary Reductions
As a result of the issues identified above, we have made the decision to slash Bestiaries across the game by a massive amount. Some examples of the type of changes you can expect to see include:
1. Lapis Zombie decreased from 3,000 -> 200
2. Lonely Spider decreased from 25,000 -> 4,000
3. Butterfly reduced from 1,000 -> 100
4. COMMON Sea Creatures decreased from various -> 1,000
5. MYTHIC Sea Creatures decreased from 100 -> 50
If you wish to see the entire changelog for the Bestiary changes, which touches the majority of mobs in the game, take CLICK HERE.
### ➥ Changing Our Bestiary Philosophy
Going forward, Bestiaries will be designed to be more lenient - we will look to reach a sweet spot where players max Bestiaries at a reasonable point in an area of content so that they can feel the impact of the Bestiary rewards (the bonus Magic Find especially) as they continue to play, especially for areas of content with rarer mobs that have RNG drops, such as MYTHIC Sea Creatures.
We apologize to anyone who ended up taking the time to grind out an older Bestiary just for it to be slashed - we will be more mindful when designing Bestiaries going forwards and the reduced brackets should make things significantly more reasonable by default (for example, all COMMON Sea Creatures now being moved into the same 1,000 kills to max bracket makes it substantially simpler next time we release a new common instead of us having to decide between 1,000, 4,000, or 10,000 kills to max).
## ► Texture Adjustments
Outside of adding textures for the vast amount of Torrhus Canyon items/mobs that were missing them, we have also adjusted the textures for the following items following community feedback:
- Aspect of the Void
- Terminator
And that's all we have for you today! We hope you have a great time testing - please remember to report any bugs you encounter HERE, being sure to select "alpha.hypixel.net".

# Changelog/2026/July 26
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[July 26] Torrhus Canyon Alpha Testing #3 - Sparkling Critters
Updated: July 26, 2026
Hello again!
Today we are back once again with a third round of Alpha Testing, this time for some more additions to the Safari Zone. If you missed the first two rounds of testing, you may find links to their respective threads here (#1) and here (#2).
## ► Sparkling Critters
If you're particularly lucky when playing through the Safari Zone, you might just come across a Sparkling Critter. These little guys are rare mutations that have a base 1/8,192 chance of spawning in place of a regular Critter. They look a bit different and also have a "SPARKLING" prefix, along with some golden particles and ambient sparkling sounds. Hunting one (or receiving Loot Share for it) awards a Rainbow Feather (this is now their method of obtainment), 10x as much loot as usual, and will progress your Sparkling Critterdex.
### ➥ Sparkling Critterdex
Upon catching all 37 Critter species and completing the Critterdex, you will now gain access to Tobias' Sparkling Critterdex. A completely optional piece of side content with cosmetic rewards, the Sparkling Critterdex tasks you with catching the Sparkling variant of each Critter species, giving you rewards for each one you catch as you aim to progress your Sparkling Critter Milestones.
In the unlikely event of you catching a Sparkling Critter before you get around to unlocking the Sparkling Critterdex, worry not, for it's entirely retroactive!
Progressing through the Sparkling Critterdex does not give any SkyBlock XP, it is intended to be a completely optional grind that players can go for if they so choose, similar to Dyes.
### ➥ Sparkling Critter Milestones
Catching a new Sparkling Critter will unlock its respective rewards in the Critterdex - an additional Rainbow Feather, large amounts of Safari Essence, Hunting XP, and so on. However, the Sparkling Milestones, which reward you for reaching milestones of total unique Sparkling Critters caught, reward you with some more... exclusive rewards.
As was the case with Torrhus Canyon's initial Alpha session, some Cosmetic rewards are currently untextured and are a work in progress. Please don't send bug reports in about this - we are aware and they will be textured prior to an eventual release.
### ➥ Other Safari Zone Changes
- Added the "Sparkling Specialist" perk to the Safari Essence Shop, which starts you with the Sparkling Amulet each run
  - Increases your chance of finding Sparkling Critters by 2x
  - Does not give any SkyBlock XP
- Added Hunter Lennard, who just talks a little about a Sparkling Critter he tried - and failed - to catch
- Removed the 1.5x Pet XP Boost from Mining
  - Designer's Note: This boost was added to the game half a decade ago when Mining was a struggling skill. However, it is now inarguably one of the most-developed skills and this boost is no longer necessary, especially when one looks at how quickly Pet XP is gained from things such as Glacite Tunnels Mining Commissions.
- Un-soulbound Rainbow Feathers
- Adjusted Macaw's "Splendor" Attribute:
  - Grants a +2-20% chance to get a bonus Rainbow Feather when hunting a SPARKLING Critter.
    - Designer's Note: Can be triggered by Loot Share
- Prevented players from joining a Safari Zone instance after 30s have elapsed
- Added the Morning Dew reforge stone for the Safari Belt. It can be bought from the Safari Essence Shop for 2,500 Safari Essence:
And that's all we have for you today! We will still be doing more Torrhus Canyon changes very soon, so stay tuned for those! We hope you have a great time testing - please remember to report any bugs you encounter HERE, being sure to select "alpha.hypixel.net".
Please note that we are currently still viewing feedback pertaining to the previous Alpha Session - you can expect to see the changes made in response to that soon!

Updated: July 29, 2026
Hello!
Today on the Alpha Network we have the official Release Candidate for Hypixel SkyBlock version 0.27! To see exactly what this update brings, read on for all the details!
## ► Torrhus Canyon & Critter Safari
This update also contains the content from our previous three patchnotes, located below:
- [July 10] Torrhus Canyon Alpha Testing #1
- [July 22] Torrhus Canyon Alpha Testing #2 + Bestiary Reductions
- [July 26] Torrhus Canyon Alpha Testing #3 - Sparkling Critters
## ► July 28th Changes
### ➥ Critter Safari Changes
- Renamed the Safari Zone to the Critter Safari
- Increased spawn rate of Sparkling Critters from 1/8,192 -> 1/4,096
- Hunter Lennard can no longer appear until you've completed the Critterdex
- Increased Morning Dew's "Majestic" Bonus from +25 -> +50 Speed while in the Critter Safari
- Doomspiral's difficulty now scales with players
- Adjusted Forest Biome Milestones to now grant Foraging Fortune instead of Sweep
- Added the option to salvage your Safari shards for Safari Essence at Hunter Archie in the Torrhus Canyon
- Improved textures of all Sparkling Critters
- Increased Safari Essence gain from Safari Critters:
  - COMMON Shard: 1x → 2x
  - UNCOMMON Shard: 2x → 4x
  - RARE Shard: 4x → 8x
  - EPIC Shard: 8x → 16x
  - LEGENDARY Shard: 16x → 32x
- Sparkling Critters are now 100x more likely to drop the Emerald Dye
- Adjusted Critter Safari Ticket Trade-ins:
  - 1x Economy -> 1x Basic Safari Ticket, 250x Forest Essence
  - 1x Premium Ticket -> 1x Economy Safari Ticket, 500x Forest Essence
  - 1x First-Class Ticket -> 1x Premium Safari Ticket, 1000x Forest Essence
- Added the ability to salvage Attribute Shards obtained from the Critter Safari at Archie for Safari Essence
### ➥ Foraging Changes
- Reorganized the HOTF tree to make certain perks more accessible for newer players. From left to right, the order is:
  - HOTF 1: Sweep
  - HOTF 2: Damage Boost, Luck of the Forest, Foraging Fortune, Collector, Axe Toss
  - HOTF 3: Deep Waters, Hunter's Luck, Galatea's Might
  - HOTF 4: Lottery, Foraging Madness, Iron Lungs, 250 Gifts, Daily Wishes, Early Bird, Precision Cutting
  - HOTF 5: Tree Whisperer, Center of the Forest, Free Trial
  - HOTF 6: Homing Axe, Forest Fisher, Str. Boost, Starlyn Supreme, Spd. Boost, Efficient Forager, Maniac Slicer
  - HOTF 7: Half Empty, Ricochet, Half Full
  - HOTF 8: Monster Hunter, Forest Speed, Essence Fortune, Timber, Two-for-one, Forest Strength, Beekeeper
- Helix Trees now respawn 2x faster
- Reduced Helix Tree toughness from 200 -> 150
- Reduce Helix Tree Gift Foraging XP from 2,000 → 1,500
- Adjusted NPC sell prices of each Galatea wood type:
  - Fig: 8 Coins
  - Mangrove: 14 Coins
  - Helix: 20 Coins
    - Designer Note: Following extensive rate testing, we are committed to these final values for release. We will look into buffing them if they don't quite hit the mark, but think it's a stable starting point.
- Added Maxim to Torrhus Canyon. He sells Booster Upgrades in exchange for resources
- Added "Purchase a Booster Upgrade from Maxim" to Chapter 5 of Torrhus Canyon
- Added Freddy to Torrhus Canyon. He gives advice on where and how to hunt each mob on the island - for a price
- Added "Purchase a Hint from Freddy" to Chapter 2 of Torrhus Canyon
- Increased the scaling of Daily Whispers' Desert Whispers from 400-40,000 -> 800-80,000
- Adjusted Swoop's dialogue to correctly mention Fig Trees have a Toughness of 10
- Revamped the Mangrove Belt into Moonglade Belt, which now grants +5% more points towards Starlyn Contests
- Added the Torrhus Belt, an upgrade to the Moonglade Belt that grants +10% more points towards Starlyn Contests
- Unsoulbound all Sloth Claws and Frog Treats
- Revamped old Ruby Veilshroom caves around Torrhus Canyon into "Pangolin Hideouts" for Pangolins to spawn in
- Added new caves underwater in Torrhus Springs for players to harvest Ruby Veilshrooms in
- Added +10 Sweep as a base stat to the Frog Pet
- Increased Sloth Pet's base stat Sweep from +15 -> +25
- Improved the HOTF Token itemlore to also mention Desert Whispers and how to obtain them
### ➥ Hunting Changes
- Reduced Queen Ant Bestiary to Bracket 2, Tier 5 (50 kills to max)
- Adjusted Paragon Shard from using a weight-based system to instead having a flat chance:
  - Tier 5 Slayer: 0.5%
  - Tier 4 Slayer: 0.25%
- Added an ability to the Entangler Lasso:
  - Grants +2.5 Hunting Fortune on Forest Shards caught with this lasso.
- Added an ability to the Medium Fishing Net:
  - Grants +2.5 Hunting Fortune on Water Shards caught with this net.
- Added an ability to the Medium Pocket Black Hole:
  - Grants +2.5 Hunting Fortune on Combat Shards caught with this black hole!
- Adjusted ability on the Large Pocket Black Hole:
  - Grants +5 Hunting Fortune on Combat Shards caught with this black hole!
- Added Drowned Shard to the Drowned family
- Added Voracious Spider Shard to the Spider family
- Added Flaming Spider Shard to the Spider family
- Added Coralot Shard to the Amphibian family
- Added Lapis Creeper Shard to the Lapis family
- Removed Honeymite from the Bug family
- Adjusted Honey Badger Shard's type from Forest -> Combat
- Adjusted Howling Spirit Shard's type from Forest -> Combat
- Adjusted Thorn Shard's type from Forest -> Combat
- Adjusted Fenlord Shard's type from Water -> Forest
- Adjusted Thyst Shard's type from Combat -> Forest
- Adjusted Sprawl Shard's ID from U53 -> U71
- Adjusted Shuddersquid Shard's ID from U45 -> U53
- Adjusted Bullfrog Shard's ID from R46 -> R47
- Adjusted Wild Hog Shard's ID from R48 -> R46
- Adjusted Black Widow Shard's ID from R78 -> R87
- Adjusted Billygoat Shard's ID from R71 -> R78
- Adjusted Thorn Shard ID from E40 -> E57
- Adjusted Inferno Demonlord Shard ID from E29 -> E48
- Adjusted Rabbit Cat Shard ID from U66 -> U106
### ➥ Bestiary Changes
Following feedback to our initial wave of Bestiary changes, we have rolled out a second wave of reductions that hits other notably time-consuming Bestiaries, such as that of Dragons, Mythological mobs, and several Catacombs mobs. For the full list of this round of reductions, please click HERE.
Please note that the SkyBlock XP gained and lost from all the changes ultimately balances itself out.
And that's all we have for you today! We hope you thoroughly enjoy this update - please remember to report any bugs you encounter while testing HERE, being sure to select "alpha.hypixel.net". Have fun!

[July 3] 0.26 Release Candidate
Updated: July 3, 2026
Hello!
Today we have the 0.26 Release Candidate on the Alpha Network. This release includes the SkyBlock Resource Pack, the Loadouts system, the new Security Quest, and a few bug fixes. This thread will only mention changes since the last Alpha, you can find those previous changes here!
## ➥ Changes
- All enchantments are now removable via the enchanting menu, even if they cannot be acquired from an Enchanting Table
- Reworked Golden Dragon Gold's Power perk to buff Midas items' Greed ability
- Buffed Ragnarock by +30 Strength
- Updated the following text symbols:
  - Magic Find
  - Overflow Mana
  - Fuel
  - Checkmark
  - X
  - Left & Right arrows during Gone with the Wind
- Swapped Shark and Spooky water orb textures
- Updated Summoning Eye sound effects
## ➥ Bug Fixes
- Fixed Spider Slayer's Conjoined Brood sometimes not spawning when using certain items like Crimson Armor or Venom's Touch

- Fixed being unable to remove enchantments higher than their max enchantable level in the enchantment table

- Fixed Summoning Eyes being unplaceable
- Fixed Watering Cans functioning as Shears
- Fixed custom colors not respecting opacity
- Fixed Fragged Midas Sword having null in-hand model
- Fixed Crystal Nucleus Vault not using new textures
If you encounter any bugs or unexpected behavior, please report it HERE! That's all for today! :hypixel_smile:

- Fixed leaves not disappearing correctly after chopping down a particular Mangrove Tree on the Moonglade Marsh.
- Fixed Moonglade Marsh and Torrhus Canyon Widget previews not working in Critter Safari.

- Fixed some spots in Critter Safari where you could get stuck in a hole.
